# Onkelos Deuteronomy

תרגום אונקלוס על דברים

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy)

התרגום המרכזי לתורה שהתקבל בתלמוד כתרגום סמכותי; בתקופת התלמוד נקרא בבית הכנסת לצד קריאת התורה, ועדיין נקרא היום בקהילות יהודי תימן.

Primary Targum on the Torah accepted in the Talmud as authoritative; read publicly in synagogues in talmudic times and still today by Yemenite Jews.

Sections: Chapter-Verse

Location: Tanakh/Targum/Onkelos/Torah

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Sifsei Chachomim Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009 (Segments: 956, License: CC-BY-NC)

Version (En): Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009 [with Onkelos translation] (Segments: 956)


## Chapter 1

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:1)


אִלֵּין פִּתְגָּמַיָּא דִּי מַלִּיל משֶׁה עִם כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּעִבְרָא דְּיַרְדְּנָא **אוֹכַח יָתְהוֹן עַל דְּחָבוּ** בְמַדְבְּרָא **וְעַל דְּאַרְגִּיזוּ** בְמֵישְׁרָא לָקֳבֵל **יַם** סוּף בְּפָארָן **דְּאִתַּפָּלוּ עַל מַנָּא** וּבַחֲצֵרוֹת **דְּאַרְגִּיזוּ עַל בִּשְׂרָא וְעַל דַּעֲבָדוּ עֵגַל** דִּדְהָב:


These are the words that Moshe addressed to all of Yisroel across the Yardein; [as he had] [**reproved them for they had sinned**] in the wilderness—[**and provoked (Hashem) to anger**] on the Arava plain facing [**the Sea of**] Suf; [and as he had] between Poron [**where they scorned the mon**] and Tofel and Lovon [**where they provoked to anger regarding meat**] and Chatzeiros and Di Zohov [**because they made the Golden Calf**].


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:2)


**מַהֲלַךְ** חַד עֲשַׂר יוֹמִין מֵחֹרֵב אֹרַח טוּרָא דְשֵׂעִיר עַד **רְקַם גֵּיאָה**:


[**A journey of**] eleven days [traveling time] from Choreiv by the Mount Seir road, to Kadeish Barnei’a [**Rekam Geyah**].


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:3)


וַהֲוָה בְּאַרְבְּעִין שְׁנִין בְּחַד עֲשַׂר יַרְחָא בְּחַד לְיַרְחָא מַלִּיל משֶׁה עִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל כְּכֹל דִּי פַקִּיד יְיָ יָתֵיהּ לְוָתְהוֹן:


[Beginning] in the fortieth year, in the eleventh month, on the first of the month, Moshe addressed Bnei Yisroel with every detail that Adonoy had commanded him [to say] to them.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:4)


בָּתַר דִּמְחָא יָת סִיחוֹן מַלְכָּא דֶאֱמֹרָאָה דְּיָתֵב בְּחֶשְׁבּוֹן וְיָת עוֹג מַלְכָּא **דְמַתְנָן** דְּיָתֵב בְּעַשְׁתָּרֹת בְּאֶדְרֶעִי:


After he had smitten Sichon, King of the Emorites, who was living in Cheshbon; and Og, King of the Boshon [**Masnan**], who was living in Ashtoros, in Edrei.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:5)


בְּעִבְרָא דְּיַרְדְּנָא בְּאַרְעָא דְמוֹאָב שָׁרִי משֶׁה פָּרֵשׁ יָת **אוּלְפַן** אוֹרַיְתָא הָדָא לְמֵימָר:


Across the Yardein in the land of Moav, Moshe began to elucidate [**the teachings of**] this Torah as follows:


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:6)


יְיָ אֱלָהָנָא מַלִּיל עִמָּנָא בְּחֹרֵב לְמֵימָר סַגִּי לְכוֹן דִּיתֶבְתּוּן בְּטוּרָא הָדֵין:


Adonoy, our God, addressed us at Choreiv, saying, ‘‘Enough of your living at this mountain!


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:7)


אִתְפְּנוּ וְטוּלוּ לְכוֹן וְעוּלוּ לְטוּרָא דֶאֱמֹרָאָה וּלְכָל **מָגִירוֹהִי** בְּמֵישְׁרַיָּא בְטוּרָא וּבִשְׁפֶלְתָּא וּבִדְרוֹמָא וּבִסְפַר יַמָּא אַרְעָא דִכְנַעֲנָאָה וְלִבְנָן עַד נַהֲרָא רַבָּא נַהֲרָא פְרָת:


Turn and travel for yourselves and arrive at the Emorite hill [region] and at all its neighbors [**habitable places**]—on the plain, on the mountain, and in the lowland, and in the Negev, and on the seacoast; the land of the Canaanites and the Lebanon, up to the great river, the Euphrates River.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:8)


חֲזֵי דִּיהָבִית קֳדָמֵיכוֹן יָת אַרְעָא עוּלוּ וְאַחֲסִינוּ יָת אַרְעָא דִּי קַיַּם יְיָ לַאֲבָהָתְכוֹן לְאַבְרָהָם לְיִצְחָק וּלְיַעֲקֹב לְמִתַּן לְהוֹן וְלִבְנֵיהוֹן בַּתְרֵיהוֹן:


See, I have set the land before you; come and inherit the land that Adonoy swore to your forefathers, to Avrohom, to Yitzchok, and to Yaakov, to give them and their descendants after them.’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:9)


וַאֲמָרִית לְכוֹן בְּעִדָּנָא הַהִיא לְמֵימָר לֵית אֲנָא יָכִיל בִּלְחוֹדִי **לְסוֹבָרָא** יָתְכוֹן:


I said to you at that time saying, ‘‘I cannot carry [**bear**] you by myself.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:10)


יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן אַסְגֵּי יָתְכוֹן וְהָא אִיתֵיכוֹן יוֹמָא דֵין כְּכוֹכְבֵי שְׁמַיָּא לְמִסְגֵּי:


Adonoy, your God, has multiplied you, and you are today as numerous the stars in the sky.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:11)


יְיָ אֱלָהָא דַאֲבָהָתְכוֹן יוֹסֵף עֲלֵיכוֹן כְּוָתְכוֹן אֲלַף זִמְנִין וִיבָרֵךְ יָתְכוֹן כְּמָא דִי מַלִּיל לְכוֹן:


Adonoy, God of your forefathers, will add to you one thousandfold, and will bless you as He spoke about you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:12)


אֶכְדֵּין **אֵסוֹבַר** בִּלְחוֹדִי טָרְחֲכוֹן **וְעִסְקֵיכוֹן וְדִינְכוֹן**:


How can I carry [**bear**] alone your troublesomeness, your burdensomeness [**business**], and your contentiousness [**judgments**]?


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:13)


הָבוּ לְכוֹן גֻּבְרִין חַכִּימִין וְסוּכְלְתָנוּן וּמַדְּעָן לְשִׁבְטֵיכוֹן **וֶאֱמַנִּנּוּן רֵישִׁין עֲלֵיכוֹן**:


Provide yourselves men, wise and understanding and renowned to your tribes, and I will designate them your leaders [**appoint them to be chiefs over you**].’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:14)


וַאֲתֶבְתּוּן יָתִי וַאֲמַרְתּוּן תַּקִּין פִּתְגָּמָא דִּי מַלֶּלְתָּא לְמֶעְבָּד:


You replied to me and said, ‘‘[It is a] good thing [**right**] that you said to do.’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:15)


וּדְבָרִית יָת רֵישֵׁי שִׁבְטֵיכוֹן גֻּבְרִין חַכִּימִין וּמַדְּעָן **וּמַנֵּתִי** יָתְהוֹן רֵישִׁין עֲלֵיכוֹן רַבָּנֵי אַלְפִין וְרַבָּנֵי מָאֲוָתָא וְרַבָּנֵי חַמְשִׁין וְרַבָּנֵי עִשּׂוֹרְיָתָא וְסָרְכִין לְשִׁבְטֵיכוֹן:


I took the leaders of your tribes, men wise and renowned, and designated [**appointed**] them leaders over you: masters of thousands and masters of hundreds, and masters of fifties and masters of tens, and court officers for your tribes.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:16)


וּפַקֵּדִית יָת דַּיָנֵיכוֹן בְּעִדָּנָא הַהִיא לְמֵימָר שְׁמָעוּ בֵּין אֲחֵיכוֹן וּתְדוּנוּן **קוּשְׁטָא** בֵּין גַּבְרָא וּבֵין אֲחוּהִי וּבֵין גִּיּוֹרֵיהּ:


I commanded your judges at that time, to say, ‘‘Listen between your brothers and you are to judge right between a man and his brother and between his complainant.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:17)


לָא תִשְׁתְּמוֹדְעוּן אַפִּין בְּדִינָא **מִלֵּי** זְעֵרָא כְרַבָּא תִּשְׁמְעוּן לָא תִדְחֲלוּן מִן קֳדָם גַּבְרָא אֲרֵי דִינָא דַּיְיָ הוּא וּפִתְגָּמָא דִּי יִקְשֵׁי מִנְּכוֹן תְּקָרְבוּן לְוָתִי וְאֶשְׁמְעִנֵּיהּ:


Do not show favor in judgement; [**words**] small and great alike will you hear out; do not fear any man; for justice belongs to God. And the matter that will be too difficult for you, present to me and I will hear it.’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:18)


וּפַקֵּדִית יָתְכוֹן בְּעִדָּנָא הַהִיא יָת כָּל פִּתְגָּמַיָּא דִּי תַעְבְּדוּן:


I commanded you at that time all the things that you are to do.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:19)


וּנְטַלְנָא מֵחֹרֵב וְהַלִּיכְנָא יָת כָּל מַדְבְּרָא רַבָּא וּדְחִילָא הַהוּא דִּי חֲזֵיתוּן אֹרַח טוּרָא דֶאֱמֹרָאָה כְּמָא דִי פַקִּיד יְיָ אֱלָהָנָא יָתָנָא וַאֲתֵינָא עַד **רְקַם גֵּיאָה**:


We traveled from Choreiv and walked the entire great wilderness, that awesome one that you saw, in the direction of the Emorite hill [region] as Adonoy, our God, commanded us; and we arrived at Kadeish Barnei’a [**Rekam Geyah**].


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:20)


וַאֲמָרִית לְכוֹן אֲתֵיתוּן עַד טוּרָא דֶאֱמֹרָאָה דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָנָא יָהֵב לָנָא:


I said to you, ‘‘You have arrived at the Emorite hill [region] that Adonoy, our God, is giving us.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:21)


חֲזֵי יְהַב יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ קֳדָמָךְ יָת אַרְעָא סַק אַחֲסַן כְּמָא דִי מַלִּיל יְיָ אֱלָהָא דַאֲבָהָתָךְ לָךְ לָא תִדְחַל וְלָא תִתְּבָר:


See! Adonoy, our God, has set the land before you. Go up, inherit [it] as promised by Adonoy, the God of your forefathers, about you; do not fear and do not tremble.’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:22)


וּקְרֶבְתּוּן לְוָתִי כֻּלְּכוֹן וַאֲמַרְתּוּן נִשְׁלַח גֻּבְרִין קֳדָמָנָא וִיאַלֲלוּן לָנָא יָת אַרְעָא וִיתִיבוּן יָתָנָא פִּתְגָּמָא יָת אָרְחָא דִּי נִסֵּק בַּהּ וְיָת קִרְוַיָּא דִּי נֵעוֹל לְהֵן:


You approached me, all of you, and said, ‘‘Let us send men ahead of us to spy out the land for us, and let them bring back word to us: the route we are to go up on and the cities we will be coming to.’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:23)


וּשְׁפַר בְּעֵינַי פִּתְגָּמָא וּדְבָרִית מִנְּכוֹן תְּרֵין עֲשַׂר גֻּבְרִין גַּבְרָא חַד לְשִׁבְטָא:


The idea pleased me, so I took from you twelve men, one man per tribe.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:24)


וְאִתְפְּנִיּוּ וּסְלִיקוּ לְטוּרָא וַאֲתוֹ עַד נַחֲלָא דְּאֶתְכָּלָא וְאַלִּילוּ יָתַהּ:


They turned and went uphill and arrived at Wadi Eshkol, and they spied it out.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:25)


וּנְסִיבוּ בִידֵיהוֹן מֵאִבָּא דְאַרְעָא וַאֲחִיתוּ לָנָא וַאֲתִיבוּ יָתָנָא פִּתְגָּמָא וַאֲמָרוּ טָבָא אַרְעָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָנָא יָהֵב לָנָא:


They took in their hand some fruit of the land and brought [it] down to us. They brought back word to us and said, ‘‘Good is the land that Adonoy, our God, is giving us.’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 1:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/1:26)


וְלָא אֲבִיתוּן לְמִסַּק **וְסָרֶבְתּוּן** עַל **מֵימְרָא** דַיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן:

מֵעֲרֹעֵר דִּי עַל כֵּיף נַחֲלָא דְאַרְנֹן וְקַרְתָּא דִי בְנַחֲלָא וְעַד גִּלְעָד לָא הֲוַת קַרְתָּא דִּי תְקֵפַת מִנָּנָא יָת כֹּלָּא מְסַר יְיָ אֱלָהָנָא קֳדָמָנָא:


From Aroer, which is on the bank of Wadi Arnon—and the city that is in the wadi—up to Gilad, there was no city that was stronger than we. All of it did Adonoy, our God, defeat before us.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 2:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/2:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/2:37)


לְחוֹד לְאַרְעָא בְנֵי עַמּוֹן לָא קָרֶבְתָּא כָּל **כֵּיף** נַחֲלָא יוּבְקָא וְקִרְוֵי טוּרָא וְכֹל דִּי פַקִּיד יְיָ אֱלָהָנָא:


Only the land of the descendants of Ammon did you not attack—all along [**the shore of**] Wadi Yabok and the hill cities—everything Adonoy, our God, commanded.



## Chapter 3

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:1)


וְאִתְפְּנֵינָא וּסְלֶקְנָא אֹרַח **דְּמַתְנָן** וּנְפַק עוֹג מַלְכָּא **דְּמַתְנָן** לְקַדָּמוּתָנָא הוּא וְכָל עַמֵּיהּ לַאֲגָחָא קְרָבָא אֶדְרֶעִי:


We turned and went up the Boshon [**Masnan**] road. Og, King of the Boshon [**Masnan**], went out toward us, he and his entire people, to [wage] war in Edrei.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:2)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לִי לָא תִדְחַל יָתֵיהּ אֲרֵי בִידָךְ מְסָרִית יָתֵיהּ וְיָת כָּל עַמֵּיהּ וְיָת אַרְעֵיהּ וְתַעְבֶּד לֵיהּ כְּמָא דִי עֲבַדְתָּא לְסִיחֹן מַלְכָּא דֶאֱמֹרָאָה דִּי יָתֵב בְּחֶשְׁבּוֹן:


Adonoy said to me, ‘‘Do not fear him, for I have put him in your hand, with all his people and his land, and you will do to him as you did to Sichon, King of the Emorites who was living in Cheshbon.’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:3)


וּמְסַר יְיָ אֱלָהָנָא בִּידָנָא אַף יָת עוֹג מַלְכָּא דְמַתְנָן וְיָת כָּל עַמֵּיהּ וּמְחִנּוֹהִי עַד דְּלָא אִשְׁתָּאַר לֵיהּ מְשֵׁזֵב:


Adonoy, our God, put into our hand also Og, King of the Boshon, and his entire people; and we smote him without leaving him a survivor.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:4)


וּכְבַשְׁנָא יָת כָּל קִרְווֹהִי בְּעִדָּנָא הַהִיא לָא הֲוַת קַרְתָּא דִּי לָא נְסִבְנָא מִנְּהוֹן שִׁתִּין קִרְוִין כָּל **בֵּית פָּלָךְ טְרָכוֹנָא** מַלְכְּוָתָא דְעוֹג בְּמַתְנָן:


We conquered all his cities at that time; there was no city that we did not take from them—sixty cities, the entire province of Argov [**Tarchona**], the kingdom of Og in the Boshon.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:5)


כָּל אִלֵּין קִרְוִין כְּרִיכָן מַקְּפָן שׁוּר רָם דִּילְהֶן דָּשִׁין וְעַבְּרִין בַּר מִקִּרְוֵי פַּצְחַיָּא סַגִּי לַחֲדָא:

וְגַמַּרְנָא יָתְהוֹן כְּמָא דִי עֲבַדְנָא לְסִיחֹן מַלְכָּא דְחֶשְׁבּוֹן גַּמַּר כָּל קִרְוֵי גֻבְרַיָּא נְשַׁיָּא וְטַפְלָא:


We annihilated them as we had done to Sichon, King of Cheshbon, annihilating every city’s men, women, and children.

And all the animals and the booty of the cities we plundered for ourselves.

The Tzidonites call Chermon Siryon, and the Emorites call it Sneer [**Mount Snow**].

כֹּל קִרְוֵי מֵישְׁרָא וְכָל גִּלְעָד וְכָל **דְּמַתְנָן** עַד סַלְכָה וְאֶדְרֶעִי קִרְוֵי מַלְכְּוָתָא דְעוֹג בְּמַתְנָן:


All the cities of the plain and entire Gilod and the entire Boshon [**Masnan**] to Salcho and Edre’i; cities of Og’s kingdom in the Boshon.

For only Og, King of the Boshon, survived from the remainder of the giants. Look, his bed—an iron bed—isn’t it in Rabbas of the descendants of Ammon: nine *amohs* its length and four *amohs* its width, in a man’s [**king’s**] *amoh.*

Yair son of Menashe took all of the Argov [**Tarchona**] province until the border of the Geshurites and the Ma’achasites [**Apekorsites**]; and he named them for himself—the Boshon [**Masnan**]—the cities of Chavos Yair until this day.

And to the Reuvenites and to the Gaddites I gave from the Gilod to Wadi Arnon, within the wadi and the border, until Yabok, the wadi at the border of the descendants of Ammon;

וּמֵישְׁרָא וְיַרְדְּנָא וּתְחוּמֵיהּ **מִגֵּנוֹסַר** וְעַד יַמָּא דְמֵישְׁרָא יַמָּא דְמִלְחָא תְּחוֹת מַשְׁפַּךְ מְרָמָתָא מַדִינְחָא:


and the Arava and the Yardein and the border; from Kinneres [**Ginosar**] until Arava Lake, the salt lake, beneath the slopes of the crest to the east.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 3:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:18)


וּפַקֵּדִית יָתְכוֹן בְּעִדָּנָא הַהִיא לְמֵימָר יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן יְהַב לְכוֹן יָת אַרְעָא הָדָא לְמֵירְתַהּ **מְזָרְזִין** תַּעְבְּרוּן קֳדָם אֲחֵיכוֹן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל כָּל **מְזָרְזֵי** חֵילָא:


I commanded you at the time, saying, ‘‘Adonoy, your God, has given you this land to inherit it. Cross over in the forefront [**well armed**], ahead of your brothers the Bnei Yisroel, all valorous [**armed**] soldiers.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 3:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:19)


לְחוֹד נְשֵׁיכוֹן וְטַפְלְכוֹן וּבְעִירְכוֹן יְדַעְנָא אֲרֵי בְעִיר סַגִּי לְכוֹן יֵתְבוּן בְּקִרְוֵיכוֹן דִּי יְהָבִית לְכוֹן:


But your wives and your children and your cattle—I know you own much cattle—will live in your cities, which I have given you.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:20)


עַד דִּי יְנִיחַ יְיָ לַאֲחֵיכוֹן כְּוָתְכוֹן וְיֵרְתוּן אַף אִנּוּן יָת אַרְעָא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן יָהֵב לְהוֹן בְּעִבְרָא דְיַרְדְּנָא וּתְתוּבוּן גְּבַר לִירֻתְּתֵיהּ דִּי יְהָבִית לְכוֹן:


Until Adonoy grants your brothers [peace] like you, and they too inherit the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving them across the Yardein. Then will you return each of you to his inheritance that I have given you.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:21)


וְיָת יְהוֹשׁוּעַ פַּקֵּדִית בְּעִדָּנָא הַהִיא לְמֵימָר עֵינָיךְ חֲזָאָה יָת כָּל דִּי עֲבַד יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן לִתְרֵין מַלְכַיָּא הָאִלֵּין כֵּן יַעְבֵּד יְיָ לְכָל מַלְכְּוָתָא דִּי אַתְּ עָבֵר לְתַמָּן:


And Yehoshua I commanded at that time, to say, ‘‘Your eyes will be seeing that everything Adonoy, your God, did to these two kings, so will Adonoy do to all the kingdoms that your are crossing into.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:22)


לָא תִדְחֲלוּן מִנְּהוֹן אֲרֵי יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן **מֵימְרֵיהּ** יְגִיחַ לְכוֹן:


Do not fear them, for Adonoy, your God, is the One Who [**His word**] is waging war for you.’’

**וְצַלֵּיתִי קֳדָם** יְיָ בְּעִדָּנָא הַהִיא לְמֵימָר:


I pleaded [**prayed**] to Adonoy at that time, saying:


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 3:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:24)


יְיָ אֱלֹּהִים אַתְּ שָׁרֵיתָא לְאַחֲזָאָה יָת עַבְדָּךְ יָת רְבוּתָךְ וְיָת יְדָךְ תַּקֶּפְתָּא דִּי **אַתְּ הוּא אֱלָהָא דִּשְׁכִנְתָּךְ** בִּשְׁמַיָּא **מִלְעֵלָּא וְשַׁלִּיט** בְּאַרְעָא **וְלֵית** דְּיַעְבֵּד כְּעוֹבָדָיךְ וּכְגִבָּרְתָּךְ:


‘‘Adonoy, El him, You have begun to show Your servant Your greatness and Your powerful hand, that there is no power in [**You are God, Whose Shechinah is in**] heaven [**above**] or [**Ruler**] on earth that [**there is none who**] can perform Your deeds and Your acts of power.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 3:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:25)


אֵעִבַר כְּעַן וְאֶחֱזֵי יָת אַרְעָא טַבְתָא דִּי בְּעִבְרָא דְיַרְדְּנָא טוּרָא טָבָא הָדֵין **וּבֵית מַקְדָּשָׁא**:


Please, allow me to cross over and see the good land that is across the Yardein, this good mountain and the Lebanon [**Beis Hamikdosh**].’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 3:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:26)


וַהֲוָה רְגַז **מִן קֳדָם** יְיָ עֲלַי בְּדִילְכוֹן וְלָא קַבִּיל מִנִּי וַאֲמַר יְיָ לִי סַגִּי לָךְ לָא תוֹסֵף לְמַלָּלָא **קֳדָמַי** עוֹד בְּפִתְגָּמָא הָדֵין:


[**From before**] Adonoy grew angry at me through your fault and did not heed me; Adonoy said to me, ‘‘Enough for you! Do not go on speaking to [**before**] Me any more about this matter.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 3:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:27)


סַק לְרֵישׁ רָמָתָא וּזְקוֹף עֵינָיךְ לְמַעֲרָבָא וּלְצִפּוּנָא וְלִדְרוֹמָא וּלְמָדִינְחָא וַחֲזֵי בְעֵינָיִךְ אֲרֵי לָא תְעִבַר יָת יַרְדְּנָא הָדֵין:


Go up to the peak of the Pisgah and look around—west, north, south, and east—see with your own eyes; for you will not cross this Yardein.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 3:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:28)


וּפַקֵּד יָת יְהוֹשֻׁעַ וְתַקֶּפְהִי וְאַלֶּמְהִי אֲרֵי הוּא יֵעִבַר קֳדָם עַמָּא הָדֵין וְהוּא יַחְסֵן יָתְהוֹן יָת אַרְעָא דִּי תֶחֱזֵי:


And command Yehoshua, and encourage him and embolden him, because he will cross at the head of this people and he will apportion to them the land that you will see.’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 3:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/3:29)


**וִיתֵבְנָא** בְחֵילָתָא לָקֳבֵל בֵּית פְּעוֹר:


We lived [**dwelt**] in the valley facing Beis Peor.



## Chapter 4

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:1)


וּכְעַן יִשְׂרָאֵל שְׁמַע לִקְיָמַיָּא וּלְדִינַיָּא דִּי אֲנָא מְאַלֵּף יָתְכוֹן לְמֶעְבַּד בְּדִיל דְּתֵיחוּן וְתֵיתוּן וְתֵירְתוּן יָת אַרְעָא דִּי יְיָ אֱלָהָא דַאֲבָהָתְכוֹן יָהֵב לְכוֹן:


And now Yisroel, listen to the statutes and to the laws that I am teaching you to fulfill, in order that you will live, and will arrive in, and will inherit the land that Adonoy, the God of your fathers, is giving you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:2)


לָא תוֹסְפוּן עַל פִּתְגָּמָא דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקֵּד יָתְכוֹן וְלָא תִמְנְעוּן מִנֵּיהּ לְמִטַּר יָת פִּקּוּדַיָּא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקֵּד יָתְכוֹן:


Do not add to the matters that I am commanding you, and do not subtract from them; to guard the commandments of Adonoy, your God, that I am commanding you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:3)


עֵינֵיכוֹן חָזָן יָת דִּי עֲבַד יְיָ בְּבַעַל פְּעוֹר אֲרֵי כָּל גַּבְרָא דִּי אֲזַל בָּתַר בַּעַל פְּעוֹר שֵׁצְיֵיהּ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ מִבֵּינָךְ:


Your eyes have witnessed what Adonoy did regarding Ba’al Peor, that every man who followed Ba’al Peor—Adonoy, your God, destroyed him from within you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:4)


וְאַתּוּן דְּאַדְבֶּקְתּוּן **בְּדַחַלְתָּא** דַיְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן קַיָּמִין כֻּלְּכוֹן יוֹמָא דֵין:


But you who cling to [**the fear of**] Adonoy, your God—all of you are alive today.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:5)


חֲזוֹ דְּאַלֵּפִית יָתְכוֹן קְיָמַיָּא וְדִינַיָּא כְּמָא דִי פַקְּדַנִי יְיָ אֱלָהָי לְמֶעְבַּד כֵּן בְּגוֹ אַרְעָא דִּי אַתּוּן עַלִּין תַּמָּן לְמֵירְתַהּ:


See, I have taught you statutes and laws as I was commanded by Adonoy, my God, to fulfill within the land that you are arriving there to inherit.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:6)


וְתִטְּרוּן וְתַעְבְּדוּן אֲרֵי הִיא חָכְמַתְכוֹן וְסוּכְלְתָנוּתְכוֹן לְעֵינֵי עַמְמַיָּא דִּי יִשְׁמְעוּן יָת כָּל קְיָמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין וְיֵמְרוּן לְחוֹד עַם חַכִּים וְסוּכְלְתָן עַמָּא רַבָּא הָדֵין:


You will preserve and you will fulfill [them], for that [displays] your wisdom and your understanding before the nations, who will hear all these statutes and will say, ‘‘The only wise and understanding people is this great nation.’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:7)


אֲרֵי מָן עַם רַב דִּי לֵיהּ אֱלָהָא קָרִיב לֵיהּ **לְקַבָּלָא צְלוֹתֵיהּ בְּעִדַּן עַקְתֵיהּ** כַּיְיָ אֱלָהָנָא בְּכָל עִדָּן דַּאֲנַחְנָא **מְצַלִּין קֳדָמוֹהִי**:


For who is a great nation that has God close to it [**to receive its prayers in time of tribulation**] as [is] Adonoy, our God, whenever we call to [**pray before**] Him?


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:8)


וּמָן עַם רַב דִּי לֵיהּ קְיָמִין וְדִינִין קַשִּׁיטִין כְּכֹל אוֹרַיְתָא הָדָא דִּי אֲנָא יָהֵב קֳדָמֵיכוֹן יוֹמָא דֵין:


And who is a great nation that has upright statutes and laws, like this entire Torah that I am putting before you today?


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:9)


לְחוֹד אִסְתַּמַּר לָךְ וְטַר נַפְשָׁךְ לַחֲדָא דִּילְמָא תִנְשֵׁי יָת פִּתְגָּמַיָּא דִּי חֲזוֹ עֵינָיִךְ וְדִילְמָא יְעִדּוּן מִלִּבָּךְ כֹּל יוֹמֵי חַיָּיךְ וּתְהוֹדְעִנּוּן לִבְנָיךְ וְלִבְנֵי בְנָיךְ:


Look out for yourself and guard your life exceedingly, lest you forget the words your eyes witnessed, and lest they are removed from your mind, all the days of your life; you will make them known to your children and to your grandchildren.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:10)


יוֹמָא דִּי קַמְתָּא קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בְּחֹרֵב כַּד אֲמַר יְיָ לִי כְּנוֹשׁ **קֳדָמַי** יָת עַמָּא וְאַשְׁמְעִנּוּן יָת פִּתְגָּמָי דִּי יֵלְפוּן לְמִדְחַל קֳדָמַי כָּל יוֹמַיָּא דִּי אִנּוּן קַיָּמִין עַל אַרְעָא וְיָת בְּנֵיהוֹן יַלְּפוּן:


The day you stood before Adonoy, your God, at Choreiv, when Adonoy said to me, ‘‘Assemble for [**before**] Me the people, and I will let them hear My statements, so that they will learn to fear Me all the years that they are living on the land, and will teach their sons.’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:11)


וּקְרֶבְתּוּן וְקַמְתּוּן **בְּשִׁפּוֹלֵי** טוּרָא וְטוּרָא בָּעֵר בְּאֶשָּׁתָא עַד **צֵית** שְׁמַיָּא חֲשׁוֹכָא עֲנָנָא וַאֲמִיטְתָא:


You approached and stood beneath [**at the base of**] the mountain, while the mountain was burning with fire up to the heart [**height**] of the heavens—darkness, cloud, and thick cloud.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:12)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִמְּכוֹן מִגּוֹ אֶשָּׁתָא קַל פִּתְגָּמִין אַתּוּן שָׁמְעִין וּדְמוּת לֵיתֵיכוֹן חָזָן אֶלָּהֵן קָלָא:


Adonoy addressed you from within the fire; you heard the sound of speech, seeing no image, solely sound.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:13)


וְחַוִּי לְכוֹן יָת קְיָמֵיהּ דִּי פַקִּיד יָתְכוֹן לְמֶעְבַּד עֲשֶׂרֶת פִּתְגָּמִין וּכְתָבִנוּן עַל תְּרֵין לוּחֵי אַבְנַיָּא:


He told you His covenant, which He commanded you to fulfill—the ten statements—and He wrote them on two tablets of stone.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:14)


וְיָתִי פַּקִּיד יְיָ בְּעִדָּנָא הַהִיא לְאַלָּפָא יָתְכוֹן קְיָמִין וְדִינִין לְמֶעְבָּדְכוֹן יָתְהוֹן בְּאַרְעָא דִּי אַתּוּן עָבְרִין תַּמָּן לְמֵירְתַהּ:


Adonoy commanded me at that time to teach you statutes and laws, for you to fulfill them in the land that you are crossing over there to inherit.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:15)


וְתִסְתַּמְּרוּן לַחֲדָא לְנַפְשָׁתֵיכוֹן אֲרֵי לָא חֲזֵיתוּן כָּל דְּמוּת בְּיוֹמָא דִּי מַלִּיל יְיָ עִמְּכוֹן בְּחֹרֵב מִגּוֹ אֶשָּׁתָא:


Be extremely cautious for your lives, for you did not see any image on the day Adonoy addressed you at Choreiv from within the fire.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:16)


דִּילְמָא תְחַבְּלוּן וְתַעְבְּדוּן לְכוֹן צֶלֶם דְּמוּת כָּל צוּרָא דְּמוּת דְּכַר אוֹ נֻקְבָּא:


Lest you destructively make for yourselves a statue in the image of any form, the representation of a male or a female;


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:17)


דְּמוּת כָּל בְּעִירָא דִּי בְאַרְעָא דְּמוּת כָּל צִפַּר גַּדְפָא דִּי פָרַח **בַּאֲוֵר רְקִיעַ** שְׁמַיָּא:


[or] the representation of any animal that is on earth; [or] the representation of any winged bird that flies in [**the air of the expanse of**] the sky;


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:18)


דְּמוּת כָּל רִחֲשָׁא דִי בְאַרְעָא דְּמוּת כָּל נוּנֵי דִי בְמַיָּא מִלְּרַע לְאַרְעָא:


[or] the representation of anything that crawls on the ground; [or] the representation of any fish that is in the water beneath the earth.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:19)


וְדִילְמָא תִזְקוֹף עֵינָיךְ לִשְׁמַיָּא וְתֶחֱזֵי יָת שִׁמְשָׁא וְיָת סִיהֲרָא וְיָת כּוֹכְבַיָּא כֹּל חֵילֵי שְׁמַיָּא וְתִטְעֵי וְתִסְגּוּד לְהוֹן וְתִפְלְחִנּוּן דִּי זַמִּין יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָתְהוֹן לְכֹל עַמְמַיָּא תְּחוֹת כָּל שְׁמַיָּא:


Or lest you raise your eyes heavenward and see the sun or the moon or the stars—the entire host of the heavens—and you will err and bow to them and worship them, when Adonoy, your God, has made them available to all the peoples who are under the whole sky.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:20)


וְיָתְכוֹן **קָרִיב יְיָ לְדַחַלְתֵּיהּ** וְאַפֵּק יָתְכוֹן מִכּוּרָא דְפַרְזְלָא מִמִּצְרָיִם לְמֵהֱוֵי לֵיהּ לְעַמָּא אַחֲסָנָא כְּיוֹמָא הָדֵין:


But Adonoy took you [for Himself] [**drew you near to His service**] when He took you out of the iron crucible, from Egypt, to be for Him a people-territory like this day.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:21)


וּמִן קֳדָם יְיָ הֲוָה רְגַז עֲלַי עַל פִּתְגָּמֵיכוֹן וְקַיִּים בְּדִיל דְּלָא לְמֵעֲבַר יָת יַרְדְּנָא וּבְדִיל דְּלָא לְמֵיעַל לְאַרְעָא טָבָא דִּי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ אַחֲסָנָא:


Yet Adonoy grew angry at me on account of your statements, and He swore that I would not cross the Yardein and would not enter the good land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you as territory.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:22)


אֲרֵי אֲנָא מָאִית בְּאַרְעָא הָדָא לֵית אֲנָא עָבֵר יָת יַרְדְּנָא וְאַתּוּן עָבְרִין וְתֵירְתוּן יָת אַרְעָא טָבָא הָדָא:


For I am to die in this land, I am not crossing the Yardein; but you are crossing, and will inherit this good land.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:23)


אִסְתַּמָּרוּ לְכוֹן דִּילְמָא תִתִּנְשׁוּן יָת קְיָמָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן דִּי גְזַר עִמְּכוֹן וְתַעְבְּדוּן לְכוֹן צֶלֶם דְּמוּת כֹּלָּא דִּי פַקְּדָךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ:


Look out for yourselves lest you forget the covenant of Adonoy, your God, that He made with you, and you make for yourselves a statue—an image of anything—that Adonoy, your God, commanded you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:24)


אֲרֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ **מֵימְרֵיהּ** אֶשָּׁא אָכְלָא הוּא אֵל קַנָּא:


For Adonoy, your God, [**His word**] is a consuming fire, a jealous Almighty.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:25)


אֲרֵי תוֹלְדוּן בְּנִין וּבְנֵי בְנִין וְתִתְעַתְּקוּן בְּאַרְעָא וּתְחַבְּלוּן וְתַעְבְּדוּן צֶלֶם דְּמוּת כֹּלָּא וְתַעְבְּדוּן דְּבִישׁ **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְאַרְגָּזָא **קֳדָמוֹהִי**:


When you have children and grandchildren, and have grown old in the land; and you will be corrupt and make a statue—an image of anything—and do what is evil in the eyes of [**before**] Adonoy, your God, to anger [**before**] Him.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:26)


אַסְהֵדִית בְּכוֹן יוֹמָא דֵין יָת שְׁמַיָּא וְיָת אַרְעָא אֲרֵי מֵיבַד תֵּיבְדוּן בִּפְרִיעַ מֵעַל אַרְעָא דִּי אַתּוּן עָבְרִין יָת יַרְדְּנָא לְתַמָּן לְמֵירְתַהּ לָא תוֹרְכוּן יוֹמִין עֲלַהּ אֲרֵי אִשְׁתְּצָאָה תִּשְׁתֵּצוּן:


I bring as witness against you today heaven and earth, that you will be completely and swiftly removed from upon the land, that you are crossing the Yardein there to inherit; you will not live long upon it, but will be completely destroyed.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:27)


וִיבַדַּר יְיָ יָתְכוֹן בְּעַמְמַיָּא וְתִשְׁתָּאֲרוּן עַם דְּמִנְיָן בְּעַמְמַיָּא דִּי יְדַבַּר יְיָ יָתְכוֹן לְתַמָּן:


Adonoy will disperse you among the peoples, and you will remain few in number among the nations that Adonoy leads you to.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:28)


וְתִפְלְחוּן תַּמָּן **לְעַמְמַיָּא פָלְחֵי** טַעֲוָתָא עֹבַד יְדֵי אֱנָשָׁא אָעָא וְאַבְנָא דִּי לָא יֶחֱזוּן וְלָא שָׁמְעִין וְלָא אָכְלִין וְלָא מְרִיחִין:


There you will serve [**a nation who serves**] man-made gods of wood and stone that do not see, do not hear, do not eat, and do not smell.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:29)


וְתִתְבְּעוּן מִתַּמָּן **דַּחַלְתָּא** דַיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ וְתִשְׁכָּח אֲרֵי תִבְעֵי **מִן קֳדָמוֹהִי** בְּכָל לִבָּךְ וּבְכָל נַפְשָׁךְ:


You will seek from there [**the fear of**] Adonoy, your God, and you will find [Him], when you seek [**before**] Him wholeheartedly and with your whole being.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:30)


כַּד יֵיעוֹק לָךְ וְיַשְׁכְּחֻנָּךְ כֹּל פִּתְגָּמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין בְּסוֹף יוֹמַיָּא וּתְתוּב **לְדַחַלְתָּא** דַיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ וּתְקַבֵּל **לְמֵימְרֵיהּ**:


When you are in distress and all these things happen to you, at the end of time, you will return to [**the fear of**] Adonoy, your God, and will obey Him [**His word**].


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:31)


אֲרֵי אֱלָהָא רַחֲמָנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לָא יִשְׁבְּקִנָּךְ וְלָא יְחַבְּלִנָּךְ וְלָא יִתִּנְשֵׁי יָת קְיָמָא דַאֲבָהָתָךְ דִּי קַיִּים לְהוֹן:


For a merciful Almighty is Adonoy, your God; He will not forsake you nor destroy you; nor will He forget the covenant of your fathers that He swore to them.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:32)


אֲרֵי שְׁאֵל כְּעַן לְיוֹמַיָּא קַדְמָאֵי דַּהֲווֹ קֳדָמָךְ לְמִן יוֹמָא דִּי בְרָא יְיָ אָדָם עַל אַרְעָא וּלְמִסְּיָפֵי שְׁמַיָּא וְעַד סְיָפֵי שְׁמַיָּא הַהֲוָה כְּפִתְגָּמָא רַבָּא הָדֵין אוֹ הַאִשְׁתְּמַע כְּוָתֵיהּ:


Inquire now about the early years that preceded you, from the day that God created Adam on earth and from one end of the heavens to the other end of the heavens: Did anything ever happen comparable to this great event, or did anyone ever hear of such a thing?


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:33)


הַשְׁמַע עַמָּא קַל **מֵימְרָא** דַיְיָ מְמַלֵּל מִגּוֹ אֶשָּׁתָא כְּמָא דִי שְׁמַעַתְּ אַתְּ וְיִתְקַיָּם:


Did any people [ever] hear [**the word of**] God’s voice speaking from within the fire as you heard and survive?


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:34)


אוֹ **נִסִּין דִּי עֲבַד** יְיָ **לְאִתְגַּלָּאָה** לְמִפְרַק לֵיהּ עַם מִגּוֹ עַם בְּנִסִּין בְּאָתִין וּבְמוֹפְתִין וּבִקְרָבָא וּבִידָא תַקִּיפָא וּבִדְרָעָא מְרָמָא וּבְחֶזְוָנִין רַבְרְבִין כְּכֹל דִּי עֲבַד לְכוֹן יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן בְּמִצְרַיִם לְעֵינָיךְ:


Or [**miracles that He performed to become revealed,**] did any god ever miraculously come and take for himself a nation from within a nation through tests, with signs and with wonders, and through warfare, and with a strong hand and with an extended arm, and with great displays; entirely as Adonoy, your God, did for you in Egypt as you watched?


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:35)


אַתְּ אִתְחֲזֵיתָא לְמִדַּע אֲרֵי יְיָ הוּא אֱלֹּהִים לֵית עוֹד בַּר מִנֵּיהּ:


You have been shown that you might know that Adonoy, He is *the* God; there is no [power] other than He.
