[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:36)


מִן שְׁמַיָּא אַשְׁמְעָךְ יָת קָל **מֵימְרֵיהּ** לְאַלָּפוּתָךְ וְעַל אַרְעָא אַחְזְיָךְ יָת אֶשָּׁתֵיהּ רַבְּתָא וּפִתְגָּמוֹהִי שְׁמַעַתְּ מִגּוֹ אֶשָּׁתָא:


From the sky He made audible to you [**the word of**] His voice to teach you, and on the earth He showed you His great fire, and you heard His words form within the fire.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:37)


וָחֳלַף אֲרֵי רְחִים יָת אֲבָהָתָךְ וְאִתִּרְעֵי בִבְנוֹהִי בַּתְרוֹהִי וְאַפְּקָךְ **בְּמֵימְרֵיהּ** בְּחֵילֵיהּ רַבָּא מִמִּצְרָיִם:


Because He loved your forefathers, He chose his descendants after him; and He took you out before Him [**by His word**] with His great strength from Egypt.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:38)


לְתָרָכָא עַמְמִין רַבְרְבִין וְתַקִּיפִין מִנָּךְ מִן קֳדָמָךְ לְאָעָלוּתָךְ לְמִתַּן לָךְ יָת אַרְעֲהוֹן אַחֲסָנָא כְּיוֹמָא הָדֵין:


To expel nations greater and stronger than you from before you; to bring you in [and] to give you their land as terrritory, like this day.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:39)


וְתִדַּע יוֹמָא דֵין וּתְתוּב לְלִבָּךְ אֲרֵי יְיָ הוּא אֱלָהָא **דִּשְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ** בִּשְׁמַיָּא מִלְּעֵלָּא **וְשַׁלִּיט** עַל אַרְעָא מִלְּרָע לֵית עוֹד:


You will know today, and will restore to your perception that Adonoy is *the* God [**Whose Shechinah is**] in heaven above and [**Ruler**] on earth below; there is no other.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:40)


וְתִטַּר יָת קְיָמוֹהִי וְיָת פִּקוּדוֹהִי דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקְּדָךְ יוֹמָא דֵין דִּי יֵיטַב לָךְ וְלִבְנָיךְ בַּתְרָךְ וּבְדִיל דְּתוֹרִיךְ יוֹמִין עַל אַרְעָא דִּי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ כָּל יוֹמַיָּא:


You will guard His statutes and His commandments, which I am commanding you today, so that you have it good and your children after you, and in order that you live long on the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you for all time.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:41)


בְּכֵן יַפְרֵשׁ משֶׁה תְּלַת קִרְוִין בְּעִבְרָא דְּיַרְדְּנָא מִדְנַח שִׁמְשָׁא:


Then Moshe set aside three cities across the Yardein, where the sun rises,


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:42)


לְמֵעִירוֹק תַּמָּן קָטוֹלָא דִּי יִקְטוֹל יָת חַבְרֵיהּ בְּלָא מַנְדְּעֵי וְהוּא לָא סָנֵי לֵיהּ מֵאִתְמַלֵּי וּמִדְּקַמּוֹהִי וְיֵעִרוֹק לְחַד מִן קִרְוַיָּא הָאִלֵּין וְיִתְקַיָּם:


for a murderer to escape there, who has murdered his neighbor without intent, and he had not been his enemy yesterday [or] the day before. He is to flee to one of these cities to survive:


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:43)


יָת בֶּצֶר בְּמַדְבְּרָא בְּאַרְעָא מֵישְׁרָא **לְשִׁבְטָא** דִרְאוּבֵן וְיָת רָאמוֹת בַּגִּלְעָד **לְשִׁבְטָא** דְגָד וְיָת גּוֹלָן בְּמַתְנָן **לְשִׁבְטָא** דִמְנַשֶּׁה:


Betzer in the wilderness in the plains [**of the tribe**] of the Reuvenites, Romos in the Gil’od [**of the tribe**] of the Gaddites, and Golon in the Boshon [**of the tribe**] of the Menashians.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:44)


וְדָא אוֹרַיְתָא דִּי **סַדַּר** משֶׁה קֳדָם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


And this is the Torah that Moshe set [**in order**] before Bnei Yisroel.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:45

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:45](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:45)


אִלֵּין סָהֶדְוָתָא וּקְיָמַיָּא וְדִינַיָּא דִּי מַלִּיל משֶׁה עִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּמִפָּקְהוֹן מִמִּצְרָיִם:


These are the testimonies and the statutes and the laws that Moshe addressed to Bnei Yisroel when they came out of Egypt.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:46

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:46](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:46)


בְּעִבְרָא דְיַרְדְּנָא בְּחֵילָתָא לָקֳבֵל בֵּית פְּעוֹר בְּאַרְעָא דְּסִיחֹן מַלְכָּא דֶאֱמֹרָאָה דִּי יָתֵב בְּחֶשְׁבּוֹן דִּי מְחָא משֶׁה וּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּמִפָּקְהוֹן מִמִּצְרָיִם:


Across the Yardein, in the valley facing Beis Peor, in the land of Sichon, King of the Emorites, who lived in Cheshbon, whom Moshe and Bnei Yisrael smote when they came out of Egypt.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:47

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:47](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:47)


וִירִיתוּ יָת אַרְעֵיהּ וְיָת אַרְעָא דְּעוֹג מַלְכָּא **דְמַתְנָן** תְּרֵין מַלְכֵי אֱמֹרָאָה דִּי בְּעִבְרָא דְיַרְדְּנָא מִדְנַח שִׁמְשָׁא:


They inherited his land and the land of Og, King of the Boshon [**Masnan**]—the two Emorite kings who were across the Yardein, where the sun rises.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:48

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:48](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:48)


מֵעֲרֹעֵר דִּי עַל כֵּיף נַחֲלָא דְאַרְנֹן וְעַד טוּרָא דְשִׂיאֹן הוּא חֶרְמוֹן:


From Aroer, which is on the bank of Wadi Arnon, up to Mount Sion which is Chermon.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:49

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:49](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/4:49)


וְכָל מֵישְׁרָא עִבְרָא דְיַרְדְּנָא לְמָדִינְחָא וְעַד יַמָּא דְמֵישְׁרָא תְּחוֹת מַשְׁפַּךְ מְרָמָתָא:


And the entire Arava across the Yardein to the east, to Arava Lake, beneath the slopes of the Pisgah.



## Chapter 5

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:1)


וּקְרָא משֶׁה לְכָל יִשְׂרָאֵל וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן שְׁמַע יִשְׂרָאֵל יָת קְיָמַיָּא וְיָת דִּינַיָּא דִּי אֲנָא מְמַלֵּל קֳדָמֵיכוֹן יוֹמָא דֵין וְתַלְּפוּן יָתְהוֹן וְתִטְּרוּן לְמֶעְבָּדְהוֹן:


Moshe called all of Yisroel and said to them, ‘‘Hear, Yisroel, the statutes and the laws that I am relating in your presence today, for you are to study them and to be careful to fulfill them.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:2)


יְיָ אֱלָהָנָא גְזַר עִמָּנָא קְיָם בְּחֹרֵב:


Adonoy, our God, made a covenant with us at Choreiv.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:3)


לָא עִם אֲבָהָתָנָא גְּזַר יְיָ יָת קְיָמָא הָדָא אֶלָּהֵן עִמָּנָא אֲנַחְנָא אִלֵּין הָכָא יוֹמָא דֵין כֻּלָּנָא קַיָּמִין:


Not [only] with our fathers did Adonoy make this covenant, but with us, we who are here today, all of us alive.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:4)


**מַמְלָל** עִם **מַמְלָל** מַלִּיל יְיָ עִמְּכוֹן בְּטוּרָא מִגּוֹ אֶשָּׁתָא:


Face [**speech**] to face [**speech**] Adonoy spoke with you at the mountain from within the fire.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:5)


אֲנָא הֲוֵיתִי קָאֵם בֵּין **מֵימְרָא** דַיְיָ וּבֵינֵיכוֹן בְּעִדָּנָא הַהִיא לְחַוָּאָה לְכוֹן יָת פִּתְגָּמָא דַיְיָ אֲרֵי דְחֶלְתּוּן **מִקֳּדָם** אֶשָּׁתָא וְלָא סְלֶקְתּוּן בְּטוּרָא לְמֵימָר:


I was standing between [**the word of**] Adonoy and you at that time, to tell you Adonoy’s word, because you were afraid of [**before**] the fire and did not go up on the mountain, saying:


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:6)


אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ דִּי אַפֵּקְתָּךְ מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם מִבֵּית עַבְדּוּתָא:


‘‘I am Adonoy, your God, Who took you out of the land of Egypt, the house of slavery.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:7)


לָא יְהֵא לָךְ אֱלָהָא אָחֳרָן **בַּר מִנִּי**:


You will not have any other gods in My Presence [**except for Me**].


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:8)


לָא תַעְבֵּד לָךְ צֶלֶם כָּל דְּמוּת דִּי בִשְׁמַיָּא מִלְּעֵלָּא וְדִי בְאַרְעָא מִלְּרָע וְדִי בְמַיָּא מִלְּרַע לְאַרְעָא:


Do not make for yourself a statue of any representation that is in the skies above or that is on the earth below, or that is in the water beneath the earth.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:9)


לָא תִסְגּוּד לְהוֹן וְלָא תִפְלְחִנּוּן אֲרֵי אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ אֵל קַנָּא מַסְעַר חוֹבֵי אֲבָהָן עַל בְּנִין **מְרָדִין** עַל דַּר תְּלִיתַי וְעַל דַּר רְבִיעַי לְשַׂנְאָי **כַּד מְשַׁלְּמִין בְּנַיָּא לְמֶחֱטֵי בָּתַר אֲבָהָתְהוֹן**:


Do not bow to them, and do not serve them, for I am Adonoy, your God, jealous Almighty, Who reckons the sin of parents for [**rebellious**] children and for the third generation and for the fourth generation of My enemies, [**when the children continue to sin after the fashion of their fathers,**]


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:10)


וְעָבֵד טֵיבוּ לְאַלְפֵי דָרִין לְרַחֲמַי וּלְנָטְרֵי פִקּוֹדָי:


and performs kindliness for thousands [of generations] for those who love Me and for those who guard My commandments.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:11)


לָא תֵימֵי יָת שְׁמָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְמַגָּנָא אֲרֵי לָא יְזַכֵּי יְיָ יָת דִּי יֵמֵי בִשְׁמֵיהּ **לְשִׁקְרָא**:


Do not swear using the Name of Adonoy, your God, in vain; for Adonoy will not hold innocent whoever swears by His Name in vain [**in a lie**].


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:12)


טַר יָת יוֹמָא דְשַׁבְּתָא לְקַדָּשׁוּתֵיהּ כְּמָא דִי פַקְדָּךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ:


Preserve the day of Shabbos to sanctify it, as Adonoy, your God, commanded you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:13)


שִׁתָּא יוֹמִין תִּפְלָח וְתַעְבֵּד כָּל עִבִדְתָּךְ:


Six days will you work and perform all your labor,


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:14)


וְיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה שַׁבְּתָא קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לָא תַעְבֵּד כָּל עִבִידָא אַתְּ וּבְרָךְ וּבְרַתָּךְ וְעַבְדָּךְ וְאַמְתָךְ וְתוֹרָךְ וַחֲמָרָךְ וְכָל בְּעִירָךְ וְגִיּוֹרָךְ דִּי **בְקִרְוָךְ** בְּדִיל דִּי יְנוּחַ עַבְדָּךְ וְאַמְתָךְ כְּוָתָךְ:


but the seventh day, Shabbos for Adonoy, your God, do not perform any labor—you, your son and your daughter, your male slave and your female slave, your ox and your donkey and all your animals, and the non-Jew who dwells in your cities [**midst**]—in order that your male slave will rest—and your female slave—like you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:15)


וְתִדְכַּר אֲרֵי עַבְדָּא הַוֵיתָא בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם וְאַפְּקָךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ מִתַּמָּן בִּידָא תַקִּיפָא וּבִדְרָעָא מְרָמָא עַל כֵּן פַּקְּדָךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְמֶעְבַּד יָת יוֹמָא דְשַׁבְּתָא:


Remember that you were a slave in the land of Egypt, and Adonoy, your God, took you out of there with a strong hand and an extended arm. That is why Adonoy, your God, commanded you to celebrate the Shabbos day.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:16)


יַקַּר יָת אֲבוּךְ וְיָת אִמָּךְ כְּמָא דִי פַקְּדָךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בְּדִיל דְּיוֹרְכוּן יוֹמָיךְ וּבְדִיל דְּיוֹטֶב לָךְ עַל אַרְעָא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ:


Honor your father and your mother in the way Adonoy, your God, commanded you; in order that you live long and in order that you have it good on the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:17)


לָא תִקְטוֹל **נְפָשׁ**. וְלָא תְגוּף. וְלָא תִגְנוֹב. וְלָא תַסְהֵד בְּחַבְרָךְ סַהֲדוּתָא דְשִׁקְרָא:


Do not murder [**a person**]. Do not commit adultery. And do not kidnap. And do not testify against your neighbor in vain.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:18)


וְלָא תַחְמֵד אִתַּת חַבְרָךְ וְלָא תֵרוֹג בֵּית חַבְרָךְ חַקְלֵיהּ וְעַבְדֵּיהּ וְאַמְתֵיהּ תּוֹרֵיהּ וַחֲמָרֵיהּ וְכֹל דִּי לְחַבְרָךְ:


And do not covet your neighbor’s wife. And do not desire your neighbor’s house, his field, or his male slave or his female slave, his ox or his donkey, or anything else that belongs to your neighbor.’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:19)


יָת פִּתְגָּמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין מַלִּיל יְיָ עִם כָּל קְהָלְכוֹן בְּטוּרָא מִגּוֹ אֶשָּׁתָא עֲנָנָא וַאֲמִיטְתָא קָל רַב וְלָא פְסַק וּכְתָבִנּוּן עַל תְּרֵין לוּחֵי אַבְנַיָּא וִיהָבִנּוּן לִי:


These words did Adonoy address to your entire assemblage at the mountain from within the fire, the cloud, and the thick cloud—a voice great and without cessation. He wrote them on two tablets of stone, and He gave them to me.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:20)


וַהֲוָה כַּד שְׁמַעֲכוֹן יָת קָלָא מִגּוֹ חֲשׁוֹכָא וְטוּרָא בָּעֵר בְּאֶשָּׁתָא וְקָרֶבְתּוּן לְוָתִי כָּל רֵישֵׁי שִׁבְטֵיכוֹן וְסָבֵיכוֹן:


When you heard the voice from within the darkness, as the mountain burned with fire, you approached me—all the leaders of your tribes and your elders.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:21)


וַאֲמַרְתּוּן הָא אַחְזְיָנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָנָא יָת יְקָרֵיהּ וְיָת רְבוּתֵיהּ וְיָת קָל **מֵימְרֵיהּ** שְׁמַעְנָא מִגּוֹ אֶשָּׁתָא יוֹמָא הָדֵין חֲזֵינָא אֲרֵי יְמַלֵּל יְיָ עִם אֲנָשָׁא וְיִתְקַיָּם:


You said, ‘‘Look! Adonoy, our God, showed us His glory and His greatness, and His voice [**of His word**] we heard from within the fire; today we saw that God can address man and he will survive.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:22)


וּכְעַן לְמָא נְמוּת אֲרֵי תֵיכְלִנָּנָא אֶשָּׁתָא רַבְּתָא הָדָא אִם מוֹסְפִין אֲנַחְנָא לְמִשְׁמַע יָת קָל **מֵימְרָא** דַיְיָ אֱלָהָנָא עוֹד וּמָיְתִין אֲנַחְנָא:


Now, why should we die when this great fire will consume us? If we continue to hear the [**word of His**] voice of Adonoy, our God, any longer we will die.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:23)


אֲרֵי מָן כָּל בִּשְׂרָא דִי שְׁמַע קָל **מֵימְרָא** דַיְיָ קַיָּמָא מְמַלֵּל מִגּוֹ אֶשָּׁתָא כְּוָתָנָא וְאִתְקַיָּם:


For who of all flesh has heard the voice of the [**word of the**] living God speaking from within the fire like us, and survived?


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:24)


קְרַב אַתְּ וּשְׁמַע יָת כָּל דִּי יֵימַר יְיָ אֱלָהָנָא וְאַתְּ תְּמַלֵּל עִמָּנָא יָת כָּל דִּי מַלֵּל יְיָ אֱלָהָנָא עִמָּךְ וּנְקַבֵּל וְנַעְבֵּד:


Approach—yourself—and listen to everything that Adonoy, our God, says; and you will tell us everything that Adonoy, our God, tells you and we will accept and fulfill.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:25)


וּשְׁמִיעַ **קֳדָם** יְיָ יָת קָל פִּתְגָּמֵיכוֹן בְּמַלָּלוּתְכוֹן עִמִּי וַאֲמַר יְיָ לִי שְׁמִיעַ **קֳדָמַי** יָת קָל פִּתְגָּמֵי עַמָּא הָדֵין דִּי מַלִּילוּ עִמָּךְ **אַתְקִינוּ** כָּל דִּי מַלִּילוּ:


[**Before**] Adonoy [**was**] heard the intent of your words when you spoke to me; and Adonoy said to me, ‘‘[**Before Me**] I have heard the intent of this people’s words that they spoke to you—everything they said is excellent[**correct**].


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:26)


לְוַי דִּי יְהֵי **לִבָּא הָדֵין** לְהוֹן לְמִדְחַל קֳדָמַי וּלְמִטַּר יָת כָּל פִּקּוּדַי כָּל יוֹמַיָּא בְּדִיל דְּיֵיטַב לְהוֹן וְלִבְנֵיהוֹן לְעָלָם:


I wish they would retain their present attitude to fear Me and to preserve all My commandments always; so that they and their children benefit forever.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:27)


אִזֵּל אֱמַר לְהוֹן תּוּבוּ לְכוֹן לְמַשְׁכְּנֵיכוֹן:


Go say to them, ‘Return for yourselves to your tents.’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:28)


וְאַתְּ הָכָא קִים **קֳדָמַי** וֶאֱמַלֵּל עִמָּךְ יָת כָּל תַּפְקֶדְתָּא וּקְיָמַיָּא וְדִינַיָּא דִי תַלְּפוּן וְיַעְבְּדוּן בְּאַרְעָא דִּי אֲנָא יָהֵב לְהוֹן לְמֵירְתַהּ:


You, however, remain here with [**before**] Me, and I will tell you all the *mitzvohs* and the statutes and the laws that you will teach them; and they will fulfill [them] in the land that I am giving them to inherit.’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:29)


וְתִטְּרוּן לְמֶעְבַּד כְּמָא דִי פַקִּיד יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן יָתְכוֹן לָא תִסְטוּן יַמִּינָא וּשְׂמָאלָא:


You will be careful to fulfill as Adonoy, your God, has commanded you; do not deviate right or left.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 5:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/5:30)


בְּכָל אָרְחָא דִּי פַקִּיד יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן יָתְכוֹן תְּהָכוּן בְּדִיל דְּתֵיחוּן וְיֵיטַב לְכוֹן וְתוֹרְכוּן יוֹמִין בְּאַרְעָא דִּי תֵירְתוּן:


Along the entire way that Adonoy, your God, commanded you, shall you go; in order that you live and enjoy good, and you live long in the land that you are inheriting.



## Chapter 6

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:1)


וְדָא תַפְקֶדְתָּא קְיָמַיָּא וְדִינַיָּא דִּי פַקִּיד יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן לְאַלָּפָא יָתְכוֹן לְמֶעְבַּד בְּאַרְעָא דִּי אַתּוּן עָבְרִין תַּמָּן לְמֵירְתַהּ:


This is the *mitzvah,* the statutes and the laws that Adonoy, your God, commanded to teach you to fulfill in the land that you are crossing over there to inherit.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:2)


בְּדִיל דְּתִדְחַל **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְמִטַּר יָת כָּל קְיָמוֹהִי וּפִקּוּדוֹהִי דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקְּדָךְ אַתְּ וּבְרָךְ וּבַר בְּרָךְ כָּל יוֹמֵי חַיָּיךְ וּבְדִיל דְּיוֹרְכוּן יוֹמָיךְ:


In order that you will fear [**before**] Adonoy, your God, to preserve all His statutes and commandments that I am commanding you—you and your son and your grandson—all the days of your life, and in order that you live long.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:3)


וּתְקַבֵּל יִשְׂרָאֵל וְתִטַּר לְמֶעְבַּד דִּי יֵיטַב לָךְ וְדִי תִסְגּוּן לַחֲדָא כְּמָא דִי מַלִּיל יְיָ אֱלָהָא דַאֲבָהָתָךְ לָךְ אַרְעָא עָבְדָא חֲלַב וּדְבָשׁ:


You will heed, Yisroel, and you will be careful to fulfill, so that you will benefit and so that you will multiply exceedingly, as Adonoy, God of your forefathers, spoke about you—a land flowing milk and honey.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:4)


שְׁמַע יִשְׂרָאֵל יְיָ אֱלָהָנָא יְיָ חָד:


Listen, Yisroel! Adonoy is our God, Adonoy is one.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:5)


וְתִרְחַם יָת יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בְּכָל לִבָּךְ וּבְכָל נַפְשָׁךְ וּבְכָל **נִכְסָךְ**:


You are to love Adonoy, your God, with all your heart, with all your soul, and with all your possessions.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:6)


וִיהוֹן פִּתְגָּמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקְּדָךְ יוֹמָא דֵין עַל לִבָּךְ:


And these words that I command you today shall be upon your heart.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:7)


וּתְתָנִנּוּן לִבְנָיךְ וּתְמַלֵּל בְּהוֹן בְּמִתְּבָךְ בְּבֵיתָךְ וּבִמְהָכָךְ בְּאָרְחָא וּבְמִשְׁכְּבָךְ וּבִמְקִימָךְ:


You are to teach them to your children and you are to discuss them, when you sit at home, and when you journey on the road, and when you go to sleep, and when you rise.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:8)


וְתִקְטְרִנּוּן לְאָת עַל יְדָךְ וִיהוֹן **לִתְפִלִּין** בֵּין עֵינָיךְ:


You are to tie them as a sign on your arm and they are to be *totafos* [**tefillin**] between your eyes.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:9)


וְתִכְתְּבִנּוּן עַל מְזוּזְיָן **וְתִקְבְּעִנּוּן בְּסִפֵּי** בֵיתָךְ וּבְתַרְעָיךְ:


You are to write them on the doorposts [**and affix them to the lintels**] of your house, and on your gateposts.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:10)


וִיהֵי אֲרֵי יָעֵלִנָּךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְאַרְעָא דִּי קַיִּים לַאֲבָהָתָךְ לְאַבְרָהָם לְיִצְחָק וּלְיַעֲקֹב לְמִתַּן לָךְ קִרְוִין רַבְרְבָן וְטָבָן דִּי לָא בְנֵיתָא:


When Adonoy your God will have brought you to the land that He swore to your forefathers—to Avrohom, to Yitzchok, and to Yaakov—to give you: great and good cities that you did not build.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 6:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:11)


וּבָתִּין מְלָן כָּל טוּב דִּי לָא מַלֵּיתָא וְגֻבִּין פְּסִילָן דִּי לָא פְסַלְתָּא כַּרְמִין וְזֵיתִין דִּי לָא נְצֶבְתָּא וְתֵיכוּל וְתִשְׂבָּע:


And houses full of all bounty that you did not fill, and hewn cisterns that you did not hew, vineyards and olive stands that you did not plant; and you will eat and be full.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 6:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:12)


אִסְתַּמַּר לָךְ דִּילְמָא תִנְשֵׁי יָת (דַּחַלְתָּא דַ)יְיָ דִּי אַפְּקָךְ מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם מִבֵּית עַבְדוּתָא:


Look out for yourself, lest you forget Adonoy, Who took you out of the land of Egypt, from the house of slavery.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 6:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:13)


יָת יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ תִּדְחַל **וְקָדָמוֹהִי** תִּפְלָח וּבִשְׁמֵיהּ תְּקַיַּם:


Fear Adonoy, your God, and serve [**before**] Him, and swear by His name.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 6:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:14)


לָא תְהָכוּן בָּתַר **טַעֲוַת עַמְמַיָּא מִטַּעֲוַת** **עַמְמַיָּא** דִּי בְסַחֲרָנֵיכוֹן:


Do not follow other gods [**idols of the nations**], from the gods [**idols**] of the peoples around you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 6:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:15)


אֲרֵי אֵל קַנָּא יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ **שְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ** בֵּינָךְ דִּילְמָא יִתְקֵף רָגְזָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בָּךְ וִישֵׁצָךְ מֵעַל אַפֵּי אַרְעָא:


For a jealous Almighty is Adonoy, your God, [**His Shechinah is**] within you; lest there be aroused the anger of Adonoy, your God, against you and He destroy you from upon the surface of the land.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 6:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:16)


לָא תְנַסּוּן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן כְּמָא דִי נַסֵּתוּן בְּנִסֵּיתָא:


Do not test Adonoy, your God, as you tested at Massoh.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 6:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:17)


מִטַּר תִּטְּרוּן יָת פִּקּוּדַיָּא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן וּסְהֶדְוָתֵיהּ וּקְיָמוֹהִי דִּי פַקְדָּךְ:


Carefully guard the commandments of Adonoy, your God, and His testimonies and His statutes that He commanded you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 6:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:18)


וְתַעְבֵּד דְּכָשַׁר **וּדְתַקֵן** קֳדָם יְיָ בְּדִיל דְּיֵיטַב לָךְ וְתֵעוּל וְתֵירַת יָת אַרְעָא טַבְתָא דִּי קַיִּים יְיָ לַאֲבָהָתָךְ:


Perform the upright and the good [**correct**] in Adonoy’s eyes, in order that you benefit and arrive in, and inherit the good land that Adonoy swore to your forefathers.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 6:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:19)


**לְמִתְּבַר** יָת כָּל בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבָךְ מִקֳּדָמָךְ כְּמָא דִי מַלִּיל יְיָ:


To smash [**break**] all your enemies before you, as Adonoy declared.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 6:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:20)


אֲרֵי יִשְׁאֲלִנָּךְ בְּרָךְ מְחָר לְמֵימָר מָא סָהֶדְוָתָא וּקְיָמַיָּא וְדִינַיָּא דִּי פַקִּיד יְיָ אֱלָהָנָא יָתְכוֹן:


When your son asks you tomorrow, saying, ‘‘What are the testimonies and the statues and the laws that Adonoy, our God, commanded you?’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 6:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:21)


וְתֵימַר לִבְנָךְ עַבְדִין הֲוֵינָא לְפַרְעֹה בְּמִצְרָיִם וְאַפְּקָנָא יְיָ מִמִּצְרַיִם בִּידָא תַקִּיפָא:


You will tell your son, ‘‘We were slaves to Pharaoh in Egypt, and Adonoy took us out of Egypt with a strong hand.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 6:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:22)


וִיהַב יְיָ אָתִין וּמוֹפְתִין רַבְרְבִין וּבִישִׁין בְּמִצְרַיִם בְּפַרְעֹה וּבְכָל אֱנַשׁ בֵּיתֵיהּ לְעֵינָנָא:


And Adonoy set signs and wonders [that were] great and harmful, upon Egypt, upon Pharaoh, and upon his entire household as we watched.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 6:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:23)


וְיָתָנָא אַפֵּק מִתַּמָּן בְּדִיל לְאַעֲלָא יָתָנָא לְמִתַּן לָנָא יָת אַרְעָא דִּי קַיִּים לַאֲבָהָתָנָא:


And us He took out of there, in order to bring us into, and to give us the land He swore to our forefathers.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 6:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:24)


וּפַקְּדָנָא יְיָ לְמֶעְבַּד יָת כָּל קְיָמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין לְמִדְחַל יָת יְיָ אֱלָהָנָא לְטַב לָנָא כָּל יוֹמַיָּא לְקַיָּמוּתָנָא כְּיוֹמָא הָדֵין:


Adonoy commanded us to perform all these statutes to [show that we] fear Adonoy, our God; to benefit us for all time, to keep us alive like this day.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 6:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/6:25)


**וְזָכוּתָא** תְּהֵיא לָנָא אֲרֵי נִטַּר לְמֶעְבַּד יָת כָּל תַּפְקֶדְתָּא הָדָא קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָנָא כְּמָא דִי פַקְּדָנָא:


And it will be to our credit [**merit**] if we are careful to fulfill this entire *mitzvah* before Adonoy, our God, as He commanded us.



## Chapter 7

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:1)


אֲרֵי יְעֵלִנָּךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְאַרְעָא דִּי אַתְּ עָלֵל לְתַמָּן לְמֵירְתַהּ וִיתָרֵךְ עַמְמִין סַגִּיאִין מִן קֳדָמָךְ חִתָּאֵי וְגִרְגָּשָׁאֵי וֶאֱמֹרָאֵי וּכְנַעֲנָאֵי וּפְרִזָּאֵי וְחִוָּאֵי וִיבוּסָאֵי שִׁבְעָא עַמְמִין סַגִּיאִין וְתַקִּיפִין מִנָּךְ:


When Adonoy, your God, brings you into the land that you are coming there to inherit, He will cast out many nations from before you—the Chittites, the Girgashites and the Emorites, the Canaanites and the Perizites, the Chivites and the Yevusites—seven nations more numerous and more powerful than you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:2)


וְיִמְסְרִנּוּן יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ קֳדָמָךְ וְתִמְחִנּוּן גַּמָּרָא תְגַמַּר יָתְהוֹן לָא תִגְזַר לְהוֹן קְיָם וְלָא **תְרַחַם** עֲלֵיהוֹן:


Adonoy, your God, will defeat them before you, and you will smite them. You must surely annihilate them. Do not make a treaty with them, and do not favor [**show mercy on**] them.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:3)


וְלָא תִתְחַתַּן בְּהוֹן בְּרַתָּךְ לָא תִתֵּן לִבְרֵיהּ וּבְרַתֵּיהּ לָא תִסַּב לִבְרָךְ:


Do not marry them; do not give your daughter to his son, nor take his daughter for your son.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:4)


אֲרֵי יַטְעֲיַן יָת בְּרָךְ מִבָּתַר **פָּלְחָנִי** וְיִפְלְחוּן **לְטַעֲוַת עַמְמַיָּא** וְיִתְקֵף רָגְזָא דַיְיָ בְּכוֹן וִישֵׁצָךְ בִּפְרִיעַ:


For he will turn your son away from Me [**My service**] and they will serve other gods [**idols of the nations**]; and Adonoy’s anger will be aroused against you and He will destroy you swiftly.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:5)


אֲרֵי אִם כְּדֵין תַּעְבְּדוּן לְהוֹן אֱגוֹרֵיהוֹן תְּתָרְעוּן וְקָמָתְהוֹן תְּתַבְּרוּן וַאֲשֵׁרֵיהוֹן תְּקוֹצְצוּן וְצַלְמֵי **טַעֲוָתְהוֹן** תּוֹקְדוּן בְּנוּרָא:


Rather, thus shall you do to them: raze their altars and smash their monuments, cut down their worship-trees and burn their statues [**idols**] in fire.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:6)


אֲרֵי עַם קַדִּישׁ אַתְּ קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בָּךְ אִתִּרְעֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְמֶהֱוֵי לֵיהּ לְעַם חַבִּיב מִכֹּל עַמְמַיָּא דִּי עַל אַפֵּי אַרְעָא:


For you are a holy people to Adonoy, your God; Adonoy, your God, chose you to be for Him a treasured people from all the peoples that are on the face of the earth.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:7)


לָא מִדְּסַגִּיאִין אַתּוּן מִכָּל עַמְמַיָּא צָבֵי יְיָ בְּכוֹן וְאִתִּרְעֵי בְכוֹן אֲרֵי אַתּוּן זְעֵרִין מִכָּל עַמְמַיָּא:


Not because of the multitude your population beyond all peoples did Adonoy desire you and choose you; for you are the least of all the peoples.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:8)


אֲרֵי מִדְּרַחַם יְיָ יָתְכוֹן וּמִדִּנְטַר יָת קְיָמָא דִּי קַיִּים לַאֲבָהָתְכוֹן אַפֵּיק יְיָ יָתְכוֹן בִּידָא תַקִּיפָא וּפָרְקָךְ מִבֵּית עַבְדוּתָא מִידָא דְפַרְעֹה מַלְכָּא דְמִצְרָיִם:


Rather, out of Adonoy’s love for you and out of His keeping the oath that He swore to your forefathers did Adonoy take you out with a strong hand, and did He redeem you from the house of slavery, from the hand of Pharaoh, king of Egypt.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:9)


וְתִדַּע אֲרֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ הוּא אֱלֹּהִים אֱלָהָא מְהֵימְנָא נָטֵר קְיָמָא וְחִסְדָּא לְרַחֲמוֹהִי וּלְנָטְרֵי פִקּוֹדוֹהִי לְאַלְפֵי דָרִין:


You will know that Adonoy, your God, is *the* God, the trusted Almighty, who keeps the covenant and the kindliness for those who love Him and for those who keep His commandments, for a thousand generations.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:10)


וּמְשַׁלֵּם לְסָנְאוֹהִי **טָבָן דִּי אִנּוּן עָבְדִין קֳדָמוֹהִי בְּחַיֵּיהוֹן** לְאוֹבָדֵיהוֹן לָא מְאַחַר **עֹבַד טָב** לְסָנְאוֹהִי **טַבְוָן דִּי אִנּוּן עָבְדִין קֳדָמוֹהִי בְּחַיֵּיהוֹן** מְשַׁלֵּם **לְהוֹן**:


And He pays His enemy [**for the good that they have done before Him**] to his face [**in their lifetime**] to destroy him. He does not delay [**to do good**] for His enemy [**for the good that they have done before Him**]—to his face [**in their lifetime**] does He pay him [**them**].


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:11)


וְתִטַּר יָת תַּפְקֶדְתָּא וְיָת קְיָמַיָּא וְיָת דִּינַיָּא דִי אֲנָא מְפַקְּדָךְ יוֹמָא דֵין לְמֶעְבָּדְהוֹן:


You are to guard the commandments and the statutes and the laws that I am commanding you today, to fulfill.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:12)


וִיהֵי **חֳלַף** דִּי תְקַבְּלוּן יָת דִּינַיָּא הָאִלֵּין וְתִטְּרוּן וְתַעְבְּדוּן יָתְהוֹן וְיִטַּר יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לָךְ יָת קְיָמָא וְיָת חִסְדָּא דִּי קַיִּים לַאֲבָהָתָךְ:


In the future, as a consequence of [**in exchange for**] your heeding these laws, and your guarding and fulfilling them; Adonoy, your God, will guard for you the covenant and the kindliness that He swore to your forefathers.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:13)


וְיִרְחֲמִנָּךְ וִיבָרֵכִנָּךְ וְיַסְגִּנָּךְ וִיבָרֵךְ וַלְדָּא דִמְעָךְ וְאִבָּא דְאַרְעָךְ עִבוּרָךְ וְחַמְרָךְ וּמִשְׁחָךְ בַּקְרֵי תוֹרָיךְ וְעֶדְרֵי עָנָךְ עַל אַרְעָא דִּי קַיִּים לַאֲבָהָתָךְ לְמִתַּן לָךְ:


He will love you, and bless you, and multiply you; and He will bless the fruit of your belly and the fruit of your soil—your grain, your wine, and your olive oil—the offspring of your cattle, and the herds of your sheep, upon the soil that He swore to your forefathers to give you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:14)


בְּרִיךְ תְּהֵי מִכָּל עַמְמַיָּא לָא יְהֵי בָךְ עֲקַר וְעַקְרָא וּבִבְעִירָךְ:


You will be blessed more than all the peoples; there will not be among you a sterile male or a barren female, nor among your animals.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:15)


וְיֶעְדֵּי יְיָ מִנָּךְ כָּל מַרְעִין וְכָל מַכְתְּשֵׁי מִצְרַיִם בִּישַׁיָּא דִּי יְדַעַתְּ לָא יְשַׁוִּנּוּן בָּךְ וְיִתְּנִנּוּן בְּכָל סָנְאָךְ:


Adonoy will remove from you all illness; and all the harmful diseases of Egypt that you know, He will not place upon you, but will give them to all your enemies.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:16)


**וּתְגַמַּר** יָת כָּל עַמְמַיָּא דִּי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ לָא תְחוּס עֵינָךְ עֲלֵיהוֹן וְלָא תִפְלַח יָת **טַעֲוָתְהוֹן** אֲרֵי לְתַקְלָא הוּא לָךְ:


You will consume all the peoples that Adonoy, your God, is giving you; do not take pity on them. Do not serve their gods [**idols**], because they are a snare for you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:17)


דִּילְמָא תֵימַר בְּלִבָּךְ סַגִּיאִין עַמְמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין מִנִּי אֶכְדֵּין אִכּוּל לְתָרָכוּתְהוֹן:


Perhaps you may think to yourself, ‘‘These nations are more numerous than I, how can I inherit them?’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:18)


לָא תִדְחַל מִנְּהוֹן מִדְכַּר תִּדְכַּר יָת דִּי עֲבַד יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְפַרְעֹה וּלְכָל מִצְרָיִם:


Do not fear them. Recall well what Adonoy, your God, did to Pharaoh and to all the Egyptians.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:19)


נִסִּין רַבְרְבִין דִּי חֲזָאָה עֵינָךְ וְאָתַיָּא וּמוֹפְתַיָּא וִידָא תַקֶּפְתָּא וּדְרָעָא מְרָמְמָא דִּי אַפְּקָךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ כֵּן יַעְבֵּד יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְכָל עַמְמַיָּא דִּי אַתְּ דָּחֵל מִקֳּדָמֵיהוֹן:


The great tests you witnessed, and the signs and the wonders and the strong hand and the extended arm with which Adonoy, your God, took you out; so will Adonoy, your God, do to all the peoples that you are afraid of.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:20)


וְאַף יָת עֲרָעִיתָא **יְגָרֵי** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בְּהוֹן עַד דִיֵבַדּוּן דְּאִשְׁתָּאָרוּ וּדְאִטַּמָּרוּ מִקֳּדָמָךְ:


Also the *tzir’oh* will Adonoy, your God, send against [**set upon**] them, until the destruction of those who survive and hide from you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:21)


לָא תִתְּבַר מִקֳּדָמֵיהוֹן אֲרֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ **שְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ** בֵּינָךְ אֱלָהָא רַבָּא וּדְחִילָא:


Do not be intimidated by them, for Adonoy, your God, [**His Shechinah**] is amid you, the great and fearsome Almighty.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:22)


וִיתָרֵךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָת עַמְמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין מִקֳּדָמָךְ זְעֵר זְעֵר לָא תִכּוּל לְשֵׁצָיוּתְהוֹן בִּפְרִיעַ דִּילְמָא תִסְגֵּי עֲלָךְ חֵיוַת בָּרָא:


Adonoy, your God, will expel these nations before you, gradually; you will be unable to exterminate them rapidly, lest the wild animals multiply against you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:23)


וְיִמְסְרִנּוּן יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ קֳדָמָךְ וִישַׁגֵּשִׁנּוּן שִׁגּוּשׁ רַב עַד דִּישֵׁיצִנּוּן:


Adonoy, your God, will defeat them before you, and will confuse them with great confusion until they are destroyed.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:24)


וְיִמְסַר מַלְכֵיהוֹן בִּידָךְ וְתוֹבַד יָת שִׁמְהוֹן מִתְּחוֹת שְׁמַיָּא לָא יִתְעַתַּד אֱנַשׁ מִקֳּדָמָךְ עַד דְּתִשֵׁיצֵי יָתְהוֹן:


He will put their kings in your hand, and you will eliminate their name from beneath the heavens; no man will stand up to you until you have destroyed them.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:25)


צַלְמֵי **טַעֲוָתְהוֹן** תּוֹקְדוּן בְּנוּרָא לָא תַחְמֵד כַּסְפָּא וְדַהֲבָא דִי עֲלֵיהוֹן וְתִסַּב לָךְ דִּילְמָא תִּתָּקַל בֵּיהּ אֲרֵי מְרָחָקָא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ הוּא:


Burn the statues of their gods [**idols**] in fire; do not covet the silver and gold upon them and take it for yourself, lest you be ensnared by it, because it is abominated by Adonoy, your God.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 7:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/7:26)


וְלָא תָעֵל דִּמְרָחָק לְבֵיתָךְ וּתְהֵי חֶרְמָא כְּוָתֵיהּ שַׁקָּצָא תְשַׁקְּצִנֵּיהּ **וְרָחָקָא תְּרַחֲקִנֵּיהּ** אֲרֵי חֶרְמָא הוּא:


Do not bring abomination into your house [lest] you become *cheirem* like it; thoroughly revile and abominate [**distance**] it, for it is *cheirem.*



## Chapter 8

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:1)


כָּל תַּפְקֶדְתָּא דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקְּדָךְ יוֹמָא דֵין תִּטְּרוּן לְמֶעְבָּד בְּדִיל דְּתֵיחוּן וְתִסְגּוּן וְתֵיתוּן וְתֵירְתוּן יָת אַרְעָא דִּי קַיִּים יְיָ לַאֲבָהָתְכוֹן:


The entire *mitzvah* that I am commanding you today, are you to guard so as to fulfill; in order that you live and multiply and arrive to inherit the land that Adonoy swore to your forefathers.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:2)


וְתִדְכַּר יָת כָּל אָרְחָא דִּי דַבְּרָךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ דְּנַן אַרְבְּעִין שְׁנִין בְּמַדְבְּרָא בְּדִיל לְעַנָּיוּתָךְ לְנַסָּיוּתָךְ לְמִדַּע יָת דִּי בְלִבָּךְ הֲתִטַּר פִּקּוּדוֹהִי אִם לָא:


You are to recall the entire way on which Adonoy, your God, led you these forty years in the wilderness; in order to oppress you, to test you, to know what is in your heart—will you guard His commandments or not?


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:3)


וְעַנְּיָךְ וְאַכְפָּנָךְ וְאוֹכָלָךְ יָת מַנָּא דִּי לָא יְדַעְתָּא וְלָא יָדְעוּן אֲבָהָתָיךְ בְּדִיל לְאוֹדָעוּתָךְ אֲרֵי לָא עַל לַחְמָא בִלְחוֹדוֹהִי מִתְקַיַּם אֱנָשָׁא אֲרֵי עַל כָּל אַפָּקוּת מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ יִתְקַיַּם אֱנָשָׁא:


He oppressed you and made you hungry, and He fed you the *mon* that neither you knew nor your forefathers knew, in order to inform you that not by bread alone does man live, rather by the entire expression of Adonoy’s word does man live.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:4)


כְּסוּתָךְ לָא אִבְלִיאַת מִנָּךְ וּמְסָנָךְ לָא יְחֵפוּ דְּנַן אַרְבְּעִין שְׁנִין:


Your garment did not wear out on you, nor did your foot swell these forty years.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:5)


וְתִדַּע עִם לִבָּךְ אֲרֵי כְּמָא דִי מְאַלֵּף גַּבְרָא יָת בְּרֵיהּ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ מְאַלֵּף לָךְ:


You will know with your heart that the way a man trains his son does Adonoy, your God, train you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:6)


וְתִטַּר יָת פִּקּוּדַיָּא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לִמְהַךְ בְּאָרְחַן דְּתַקְנָן קֳדָמוֹהִי וּלְמִדְחַל יָתֵיהּ:


You are to guard the commandments of Adonoy, your God, to go in His ways and to fear Him.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:7)


אֲרֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ מַעֲלָךְ לְאַרְעָא טַבְתָא אַרְעָא נָגְדָא נַחֲלִין דְּמַיִּין מַבּוּעֵי עֵינָיָן וּתְהוֹמִין נָפְקִין בְּבִקְעָן וּבְטוּרִין:


For Adonoy, your God, is bringing you to a good land, a land with streams of water, springs and groundwater that emerge in valley and hill.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:8)


אַרְעָא חִטִּין וְסַעֲרִין וְגוּפְנִין וּתְאֵנִין וְרִמּוֹנִין אַרְעָא דְּזֵיתָהָא **עָבְדִין מִשְׁחָא וְהִיא עָבְדָא** דְבָשׁ:


A land of wheat and barley, and grapes and figs and pomegranates; a land of olives [**yeilding oil**] and [**producing**] honey.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:9)


אַרְעָא דִּי לָא בְעִצּוּרִין תֵּיכוּל בַּהּ לַחְמָא לָא תֶחְסַר כָּל מִדַּעַם בַּהּ אַרְעָא דִּי אַבְנָהָא פַרְזְלָא וּמִטּוּרָהָא תִּפְסוּל נְחָשָׁא:


A land where you will not eat bread in poverty, you will lack nothing there; a land whose stones are iron, and from whose hills you will mine copper.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 8:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:10)


וְתֵיכוּל וְתִשְׂבַּע וּתְבָרַךְ יָת יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ עַל אַרְעָא טַבְתָא דִיהַב לָךְ:


You will eat and be full, and you will bless Adonoy, your God, for the good land He has given you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 8:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:11)


אִסְתַּמַּר לָךְ דִּילְמָא תִתְנְשֵׁי יָת **דַּחַלְתָּא** דַיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בְּדִיל דְּלָא לְמִטַּר פִּקּוֹדוֹהִי וְדִינוֹהִי וּקְיָמוֹהִי דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקְּדָךְ יוֹמָא דֵין:


Look out for yourself lest you forget [**the fear of**] Adonoy, your God, to not guard His commandments, His laws, and His statutes that I am commanding you today.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 8:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:12)


דִּילְמָא תֵיכוּל וְתִשְׂבַּע וּבָתִּין **שַׁפִּירִין** תִּבְנֵי וְתֵיתֵב:


Lest you eat and be full, and build good [**beautiful**] houses and live [in them.]


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 8:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:13)


וְתוֹרָךְ וְעָנָךְ יִסְגּוּן וְכַסְפָּא וְדַהֲבָא יִסְגֵּא לָךְ וְכֹל דִּי לָךְ יִסְגֵּא:


And your cattle and your sheep multiply, and silver and gold multiply for you, and everything you own multiplies.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 8:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:14)


וִירִים לִבָּךְ וְתִנְשֵׁי יָת **דַּחַלְתָּא** דַיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ דִּי אַפְּקָךְ מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם מִבֵּית עַבְדוּתָא:


And your pride increases, and you forget [**the fear of**] Adonoy, your God, Who took you out of the land of Egypt, from the house of slavery;


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 8:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:15)


דְּדַבְּרָךְ בְּמַדְבְּרָא רַבָּא וּדְחִילָא **אֲתַר חֵיוָן קָלָן וְעַקְרַבִּין וּבֵית צַחֲוָנָא אֲתַר** דִּי לֵית מַיָּא דְּאַפֵּק לָךְ מַיָּא מִטִּנָּרָא **תַּקִּיפָא**:


Who led you through the wilderness—the great and awesome[one]—[**a place of**] snake[**s**], serpent[**s**] and scorpion[**s**], and thirst[**ing**] [**a place**] where there is no water; Who took water out for you from solid [**mighty**] rock;


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 8:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:16)


דְּאוֹכָלָךְ מַנָּא בְּמַדְבְּרָא דִּי לָא יָדְעוּן אֲבָהָתָךְ בְּדִיל לְעַנָּיוּתָךְ וּבְדִיל לְנַסָּיוּתָךְ לְאוֹטָבָא לָךְ בְּסוֹפָךְ:


Who fed you *mon* in the wilderness that your forefathers had not known, in order to oppress you and in order to test you, to ultimately benefit you—


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 8:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:17)


וְתֵימַר בְּלִבָּךְ חֵילִי וּתְקֵף יְדִי קְנוֹ לִי יָת נִכְסַיָּא הָאִלֵּין:


And you think, ‘‘My strength and the power of my hand have acquired this wealth for me.’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 8:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:18)


וְתִדְכַּר יָת יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ אֲרֵי הוּא יָהֵב לָךְ עֵצָה לְמִקְנֵי נִכְסִין בְּדִיל לְקַיָּמָא יָת קְיָמֵיהּ דִּי קַיִּים לַאֲבָהָתָךְ כְּיוֹמָא הָדֵין:


Recall Adonoy, your God, for it is He Who gives you counsel to acquire wealth, in order to establish His covenant that He swore to your forefathers this day.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 8:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:19)


וִיהֵי אִם מִנְשָׁאָה תִנְשֵׁי יָת **דַּחַלְתָּא** דַיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ וּתְהַךְ בָּתַר טַעֲוַת עַמְמַיָּא וְתִפְלְחִנּוּן וְתִסְגּוּד לְהוֹן אַסְהֵדִית בְּכוֹן יוֹמָא דֵין אֲרֵי מֵיבַד תֵּיבְדוּן:


When and if you forget [**the fear of**] Adonoy, your God, and follow other gods, and serve them and bow to them, I testify about you today that you will surely be destroyed.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 8:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/8:20)


כְּעַמְמַיָּא דִּי יְיָ מֹאבִיד מִקָּדָמֵיכוֹן כֵּן תֵּיבְדוּן חֳלַף דִּלָא קַבֶּלְתּוּן **בְּמֵימְרָא** דַּיְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן:


Like the nations that Adonoy is destroying before you, so will you be destroyed, as a consequence of your not heeding [**the word of**] Adonoy, your God.



## Chapter 9

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/9:1)


שְׁמַע יִשְׂרָאֵל אַתְּ עָבֵר יוֹמָא דֵין יָת יַרְדְּנָא לְמֵעַל לְמֵירַת עַמְמִין רַבְרְבִין וְתַקִּיפִין מִנָּךְ קִרְוִין רַבְרְבָן וּכְרִיכָן עַד **צֵית** שְׁמַיָּא:


Listen, Yisroel! You are today crossing the Yardein to come inherit nations greater and more powerful than you; cities great and fortified to the [**height of the**] sky.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/9:2)


עַם רַב וְתַקִּיף בְּנֵי **גִבָּרַיָּא** דִּי אַתְּ יְדַעַתְּ וְאַתְּ שְׁמַעַתְּ מָן יִכּוּל לְמֵיקַם קֳדָם בְּנֵי **גִבָּרַיָּא**:


A great and powerful people, descendants of the Anokim [**giants**], about whom you know and heard, ‘‘who can stand up to the descendants of Anok?’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/9:3)


וְתִדַּע יוֹמָא דֵין אֲרֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ הוּא דְעִבַר קֳדָמָךְ **מֵימְרֵיהּ** אֶשָּׁא אָכְלָא הוּא יְשֵׁצִנּוּן וְהוּא יְתָרֵכִנּוּן קֳדָמָךְ וּתְתָרֵכִנּוּן וְתוֹבְדִנּוּן בִּפְרִיעַ כְּמָא דִי מַלִּיל יְיָ לָךְ:


You know today that Adonoy, your God, is the One crossing before you, [**His word is**] a consuming fire, He will destroy them and He will subdue them before you; you will expel them and destroy them quickly, as Adonoy promised for you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/9:4)


לָא תֵימַר בְּלִבָּךְ בִּדְיִתְּבַר יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָתְהוֹן מִקֳּדָמָךְ לְמֵימַר **בְּזָכוּתִי** אַעֲלַנִי יְיָ לְמֵירַת יָת אַרְעָא הָדָא **וּבְחוֹבֵי** עַמְמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין יְיָ מְתָרֵךְ לְהוֹן מִקֳּדָמָךְ:


Do not think, when Adonoy, your God, smashes them before you, saying: ‘‘Because of my righteousness [**merit**] has Adonoy brought me to inherit this land,’’ and because of the wickedness [**sins**] of these nations is Adonoy expelling them before you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/9:5)


לָא **בְזָכוּתָךְ** וּבְקַשִּׁיטוּת לִבָּךְ אַתְּ עָלֵל לְמֵירַת יָת אַרְעֲהוֹן אֲרֵי **בְּחוֹבֵי** עַמְמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ מְתָרֶכְהוֹן מִקֳּדָמָךְ וּבְדִיל לַאֲקָמָא יָת פִּתְגָּמָא דִּי קַיִּים יְיָ לַאֲבָהָתָךְ לְאַבְרָהָם לְיִצְחָק וּלְיַעֲקֹב:


Not because of your righteousness [**merit**] and the uprightness of your heart are you coming to inherit their land. Rather, because of the wickedness [**sins**] of these nations is Adonoy, your God, expelling them before you, and in order to fulfill the matter that Adonoy swore to your forefathers—to Avrohom, to Yitzchok, and to Yaakov.

Lest [the inhabitants] say—[of] the land that you took us out of—‘Because of Adonony’s inability to bring them [**up**] to the land that He promised for them, and because of His hatred of them, He took them out to kill them in the wilderness.’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 9:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/9:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/9:29)


וְאִנּוּן עַמָּךְ וְאַחֲסַנְתָּךְ דִּי אַפֶּקְתָּא בְּחֵילָךְ רַבָּא וּבִדְרָעָךְ מְרָמְמָא:


But they are Your people, Your territory, whom You took out with Your great power and with Your extended arm.’’

בְּעִדָּנָא הַהִיא אֲמַר יְיָ לִי פְּסַל לָךְ תְּרֵין לוּחֵי אַבְנַיָּא כְּקַדְמָאֵי וְסַק **לָקֳדָמַי** לְטוּרָא וְתַעְבֵּד לָךְ אֲרוֹנָא דְאָעָא:


At that time, Adonoy said to me, ‘‘Hew for yourself two stone tablets like the originals, and ascend to [**before**] Me on the mountain; and make for yourself a wooden case.

And I will write on the tablets the words that were on the original tablets that you smashed, and you will place them in the case.’’

So I made a case of *shittim* wood, and I hewed two stone tablets like the originals, and I ascended the mountain with the two tablets in my hand.

For Adonoy, your God, is God over judges and Lord over lords [**kings**]; the Almighty—the Great, the Powerful, and the Awesome [One]—Who neither exercises [**that there is not before Him**] favoritism nor accepts bribes.

You are to love the convert, because you were strangers in the land of Egypt.

You are to fear [**before**] Adonoy, your God, serve Him, cling to Him [**come near to the fear of Him**], and swear by His name.

He is your praise and He is your God, Who performed with you these great and fearsome things that your eyes witnessed.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/10:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/10:22)


בְּשַׁבְעִין נַפְשָׁן נְחָתוּ אֲבָהָתָךְ לְמִצְרָיִם וּכְעַן שַׁוְּיָךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ כְּכוֹכְבֵי שְׁמַיָּא לְמִסְגֵּי:


Numbering seventy people, your forefathers went down to Egypt; and now Adonoy, your God, has placed you like the stars of the sky, numerous.



## Chapter 11

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 11:1

You will know today that not with your children who have not known and who have not seen the teaching of Adonoy, your God—His greatness, His powerful hand and His extended arm,

and His signs and His deeds that He performed within Egypt, to Pharaoh king of Egypt, and to his entire land;

וְדִי עֲבַד **לְמַשִּׁרְיַת** מִצְרַיִם לְסוּסָוָתֵיהּ וְלִרְתִיכוֹהִי דִּי אַטִּיף יָת מֵי יַמָּא דְסוּף עַל אַפֵּיהוֹן בְּמִרְדָּפְהוֹן בַּתְרֵיכוֹן וְאוֹבָדִנּוּן יְיָ עַד יוֹמָא הָדֵין:


And what He did to the Egyptian army [**host**], to its horses and to its chariots—that He flooded the waters of Yam Suf over them when they were chasing you—Adonoy destroyed them until this day;


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:5)


וְדִי עֲבַד לְכוֹן בְּמַדְבְּרָא עַד מֵיתֵיכוֹן עַד אַתְרָא הָדֵין:


And what He did for you in the wilderness, until you arrived at this place;


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:6)


וְדִי עֲבַד לְדָתָן וְלַאֲבִירָם בְּנֵי אֱלִיאָב בַּר רְאוּבֵן דִּי פְתָחַת אַרְעָא יָת פּוּמַהּ וּבְלָעָתְנוּן וְיָת **אֱנַשׁ** בָּתֵּיהוֹן וְיָת מַשְׁכְּנֵיהוֹן וְיָת כָּל יְקוּמָא **דִּי עִמְּהוֹן** בְּגוֹ כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל:


And what He did to Dosson and to Avirom sons of Eli’ov son of Re’uven, that the earth opened its mouth and swallowed them and [**the members of**] their families and their tents and all the sustenance at their feet [**that was with them**] amid all of Yisroel.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 11:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:7)


אֲרֵי עֵינֵיכוֹן חֲזָאָה יָת כָּל עוֹבָדָא דַיְיָ רַבָּא דִּי עֲבָד:


Rather *your* eyes have been witnessing the entire overwhelming activity of Adonoy that He has performed.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 11:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:8)


וְתִטַּר יָת (כָּל) תַּפְקֶדְתָּא דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקְּדָךְ יוֹמָא דֵין בְּדִיל דְּתִתְקְפוּן **וְתֵעֲלוּן** וְתֵירְתוּן יָת אַרְעָא דִּי אַתּוּן עָבְרִין תַּמָּן לְמֵירְתַהּ:


You will guard the entire *mitzvah* that I am commanding you today, in order that you be encouraged and will arrive in, [**amd go up to,**] and inherit the land that you are crossing over there to inherit.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 11:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:9)


וּבְדִיל דְּתוֹרְכוּן יוֹמִין עַל אַרְעָא דִּי קַיִּים יְיָ לַאֲבָהָתְכוֹן לְמִתַּן לְהוֹן וְלִבְנֵיהוֹן אַרְעָא **עָבְדָא** חֲלַב וּדְבָשׁ:


And in order that you will last long on the land that Adonoy swore to your forefathers to give to them and to their descendants, a land flowing [**producing**] milk and honey.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 11:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:10)


אֲרֵי אַרְעָא דִּי אַתְּ עָלֵל לְתַמָּן לְמֵירְתַהּ לָא כְאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם הִיא דִּי נְפַקְתּוּן מִתַּמָּן דִּי תִזְרַע יָת זַרְעָךְ וּמַשְׁקַת לֵיהּ בְּרַגְלָךְ כְּגִנְתָא יַרְקָא:


For the land where you are arriving to inherit is not like the land of Egypt from which you departed, where you planted your seed and watered [it] on foot like a vegetable garden.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:11)


וְאַרְעָא דִּי אַתּוּן עָבְרִין תַּמָּן לְמֵירְתַהּ אֲרַע טוּרִין וּבִקְעָן לִמְטַר שְׁמַיָּא תִּשְׁתֵּי מַיָּא:


Rather, the land where you are crossing to inherit is a land of mountains and plains—by the rain of the skies will you drink water.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 11:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:12)


אַרְעָא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ תָּבַע יָתַהּ תְּדִירָא עֵינֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בַּהּ מֵרֵישָׁא דְּשַׁתָּא וְעַד סוֹפָא דְשַׁתָּא:


A land that Adonoy, your God, looks after; the eyes of Adonoy, your God, are always upon it, from the year’s beginning until the year’s end.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 11:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:13)


וִיהֵי אִם קַבָּלָא תְקַבְּלוּן לְפִקּוֹדַי דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקֵּד יָתְכוֹן יוֹמָא דֵין לְמִרְחַם יָת יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן וּלְמִפְלַח **קֳדָמוֹהִי** בְּכָל לִבְּכוֹן וּבְכָל נַפְשְׁכוֹן:


Should you thoroughly heed My commandments that I am commanding you today—to love Adonoy, your God, and to serve [**before**] Him with all your desires and with your entire beings,

וְאֶתֵּן מְטַר אַרְעֲכוֹן בְּעִדָּנֵיהּ בַּכִּיר וְלַקִּישׁ וְתִכְנוֹשׁ עִבּוּרָךְ וְחַמְרָךְ וּמִשְׁחָךְ:


I will provide the rain of your land in its time, fall rain and spring rain, and you will harvest your grain and your wine and your olive oil.

וְאֶתֵּן עִשְׂבָּא בְחַקְלָךְ לִבְעִירָךְ וְתֵיכוּל וְתִשְׂבָּע:


And I will provide grass in your field for your animals, and you will eat and be full.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 11:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:16)


אִסְתַּמָּרוּ לְכוֹן דִּילְמָא **יִטְעוּן** לִבְּכוֹן וְתִסְטוּן וְתִפְלְחוּן לְ**טַעֲוַת עַמְמַיָּא** וְתִסְגְּדוּן לְהוֹן:


Look out for yourselves lest your heart be misled [**mistaken**] and you turn away and serve other gods [**idols of the nations**] and bow to them,

וְיִתְקוֹף רָגְזָא דַיְיָ בְּכוֹן וְיֵחוֹד יָת שְׁמַיָּא וְלָא יְהֵי מִטְרָא וְאַרְעָא לָא תִתֵּן יָת עֲלַלְתַּהּ וְתֵיבְדוּן בִּפְרִיעַ מֵעַל אַרְעָא טַבְתָא דַּיְיָ יָהֵב לְכוֹן:


And Adonoy’s anger will be aroused against you, and He will restrain the skies and there will be no rain, and the soil will not yield its produce, and you will swiftly be removed from upon the good land that Adonoy is giving you.

You are to place these words of Mine on your mind and on your being, and bind them as a sign on your hand and they will be *totafos* [**tefillin**] on your head;

וְתַלְּפוּן יָתְהוֹן יָת בְּנֵיכוֹן לְמַלָּלָא בְהוֹן בְּמִתְּבָךְ בְּבֵיתָךְ וּבִמְהָכָךְ בְּאָרְחָא וּבְמִשְׁכְּבָךְ וּבִמְקִימָךְ:


And you are to teach them to your sons to speak in them when you sit at home, and when you journey on the road, and when you go to sleep, and when you rise;

וְתִכְתְּבִנּוּן עַל מְזוּזְיָן **וְתִקְבְּעִנּוּן בְּסִפֵּי** בֵיתָךְ וּבְתַרְעָךְ:


And you are to write them on the doorposts [**and affix them to the lintels**] of your home and your gateways.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:21)


בְּדִיל דְּיִסְגּוּן יוֹמֵיכוֹן וְיוֹמֵי בְנֵיכוֹן עַל אַרְעָא דִּי קַיִּים יְיָ לַאֲבָהָתְכוֹן לְמִתַּן לְהוֹן כְּיוֹמֵי שְׁמַיָּא עַל אַרְעָא:


In order that they will be many—your years and the years of your children on the soil that Adonoy swore to your forefathers to give them, like the years of heaven on earth.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 11:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:22)


אֲרֵי אִם מִטַּר תִּטְּרוּן יָת כָּל תַּפְקֶדְתָּא הָדָא דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקֵּד יָתְכוֹן לְמֶעְבְּדַהּ לְמִרְחַם יָת יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן לִמְהַךְ בְּכָל אָרְחָן **דְּתַקְנָן קֳדָמוֹהִי וּלְאִתְקְרָבָא לְדַחַלְתֵּיהּ**:


For if you truly guard this entire *mitzvah* that I am commanding you to perform, to love Adonoy, your God, to walk in all His ways [**that are correct before Him**] and to cling to Him [**come near to the fear of Him**].

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:23)


וִיתָרַךְ יְיָ יָת כָּל עַמְמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין מִקֳּדָמֵיכוֹן וְתֵירְתוּן עַמְמִין רַבְרְבִין וְתַקִּיפִין מִנְּכוֹן:


Adonoy will expel all these nations before you, and you will inherit nations greater and more powerful than you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 11:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:24)


כָּל אַתְרָא דִּי תִדְרוֹךְ פַּרְסַת רַגְלְכוֹן בֵּיהּ דִּלְכוֹן יְהֵי מִן מַדְבְּרָא וְלִבְנָן מִן נַהֲרָא נְהַר פְּרָת וְעַד יַמָּא **מַעַרְבָא** יְהֵי תְחוּמְכוֹן:


Every place that the sole of your foot steps on will be yours, from the wilderness and the Lebanon, form the river—the Euphrates River—up to the ultimate [**western**] sea, will be your boundary.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 11:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:25)


לָא יִתְעַתַּד אֱנָשׁ קֳדָמֵיכוֹן דַּחֲלָתְכוֹן וְאֵימָתְכוֹן יִתֵּן יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן עַל אַפֵּי כָל אַרְעָא דִּי תִדְרְכוּן בַּהּ כְּמָא דִּי מַלִּיל לְכוֹן:


No man will stand up to you; fear of you and awe of you will Adonoy, your God, place upon the surface of the entire land that you are stepping into, as He promised you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 11:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:26)


חֲזֵי דִּי אֲנָא יָהֵב קֳדָמֵיכוֹן יוֹמָא דֵין בִּרְכָן וּלְוָטִין:


Look, I place before you today, a blessing and a curse.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 11:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:27)


יָת בִּרְכָן דִּי **תְקַבְּלוּן** לְפִקּוּדַיָּא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקֵּד יָתְכוֹן יוֹמָא דֵין:


The blessing—that you heed [**obey**] the commandments of Adonoy, your God, that I am commanding you today.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 11:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:28)


וּלְוָטַיָּא אִם לָא תְקַבְּלוּן לְפִקּוּדַיָּא דַיְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן וְתִסְטוּן מִן אָרְחָא דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקֵּד יָתְכוֹן יוֹמָא דֵין לִמְהַךְ בָּתַר **טַעֲוַת עַמְמַיָּא** דִּי לָא יְדַעְתּוּן:


And the curse—if you do not heed the commandments of Adonoy, your God, and you veer from the course which I command you this day, to follow other gods [**idols of the nations**] which you did not know.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 11:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:29)


וִיהֵי אֲרֵי יְעֵלִנָּךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְאַרְעָא דִּי אַתְּ עָלֵל תַּמָּן לְמֵירְתַהּ וְתִתֵּן יָת מְבָרְכַיָּא עַל טוּרָא דִגְּרִזִּין וְיָת מְלַטְטַיָּא עַל טוּרָא דְעֵיבָל:


When Adonoy, your God, has brought you to the land that you are coming to inherit, you shall place the blessers on Mount Gerizim and the cursers on Mount Eival.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 11:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:30)


הֲלָא אִנּוּן בְּעִבְרָא דְיַרְדְּנָא אֲחוֹרֵי אֹרַח מַעֲלָנֵי שִׁמְשָׁא בְּאַרְעָא כְּנַעֲנָאָה דְּיָתֵב בְּמֵישְׁרָא לָקֳבֵל גִּלְגְּלָא **בִּסְטַר** **מֵישְׁרֵי** מֹרֶה:


Are they not across the Yardein and far beyond, along the route of the sunset? In the land of the Canaanites who dwell on the Arava Plain, opposite Gilgol, next to the [**at the side of**] Moreh Plain.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 11:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:31)


אֲרֵי אַתּוּן עָבְרִין יָת יַרְדְּנָא לְמֵעַל לְמֵירַת יָת אַרְעָא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן יָהֵב לְכוֹן וְתֵירְתוּן יָתַהּ וְתֵיתְבוּן בַּהּ:


For you shall pass across the Yardein to come to inherit the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you; you will inherit it and will dwell in it.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 11:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/11:32)


וְתִטְּרוּן לְמֶעְבַּד יָת כָּל קְיָמַיָּא וְיָת דִּינַיָּא דִּי אֲנָא יָהֵב קֳדָמֵיכוֹן יוֹמָא דֵין:


You will guard to fulfill all the statutes and the laws that I am setting before you today.



## Chapter 12

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:1)


אִלֵּין קְיָמַיָּא וְדִינַיָּא דִּי תִטְּרוּן לְמֶעְבַּד בְּאַרְעָא דִּיהַב יְיָ אֱלָהָא דַאֲבָהָתָךְ לָךְ לְמֵירְתַהּ כָּל יוֹמַיָּא דִּי אַתּוּן קַיָּמִין עַל אַרְעָא:


These are the statutes and the laws that you will guard to fulfill in the land that Adonoy, God of your forefathers, has given to inherit, all the years that you are alive on the land.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:2)


אַבָּדָא תְאַבְּדוּן יָת כָּל אַתְרַיָּא דִּי פְלָחוּ תַמָּן עַמְמַיָּא דִּי אַתּוּן יָרְתִין יָתְהוֹן יָת **טַעֲוָתְהוֹן** עַל טוּרַיָּא רָמַיָּא וְעַל רָמָתָא וּתְחוֹת כָּל אִילַן עַבּוֹף:


You must obliterate all the places where the nations worshipped, whom you are inheriting, their gods [**idols**], on the high mountains, and on the hills, and under every evergreen tree.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:3)


וּתְתָרְעוּן יָת **אֱגוֹרֵיהוֹן** וּתְתַבְּרוּן יָת קָמָתְהוֹן וַאֲשֵׁרֵיהוֹן תּוֹקְדוּן בְּנוּרָא וְצַלְמֵי **טַעֲוָתְהוֹן** תְּקוֹצְצוּן וְתוֹבְדוּן יָת שִׁמְהוֹן מִן אַתְרָא הַהוּא:


You are to break apart their altars, and you are to smash their pillars, and their asherahs are you to burn in fire, and the statues of their gods [**idols**] are you to cut down; and you are to obliterate their name from that place.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:4)


לָא תַעְבְּדוּן כֵּן **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן:


Do not act this way to [**before**] Adonoy, your God.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:5)


אֶלָּהֵן לְאַתְרָא דִּי יִתִּרְעֵי יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן מִכָּל שִׁבְטֵיכוֹן **לְאַשְׁרָאָה שְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ** תַּמָּן **לְבֵית שְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ** תִּתְבְּעוּן וְתֵיתוּן לְתַמָּן:


Rather at the place that Adonoy, your God, chooses from all your tribes to set His Presence [**have His Shechinah dwell**] there, [**at the abode of His Shechinah**] will you seek His Presence and come there.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:6)


וְתַיְתוּן לְתַמָּן עֲלָוָתֵיכוֹן וְנִכְסַת קוּדְשֵׁיכוֹן וְיָת מַעְשְׂרָתֵיכוֹן וְיָת **אַפְרָשׁוּת** יֶדְכוֹן וְנִדְרֵיכוֹן וְנִדְבָתֵיכוֹן וּבְכוֹרֵי תוֹרְכוֹן וְעָנְכוֹן:


You are to bring there your burnt-offerings and your sacred-offerings, and your tithes and the elevation of your hands [**hand-offerings**], and your pledges, and your donations, and the firstborn of your cattle and your flocks.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:7)


וְתֵיכְלוּן תַּמָּן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן וְתֶחֱדוּן בְּכֹל אוֹשָׁטוּת יֶדְכוֹן אַתּוּן **וֶאֱנַשׁ** בָּתֵּיכוֹן דִּי בָרְכָךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ:


You are to eat there before Adonoy, your God, and you will be happy with the sum of your handiwork, you and [**the members of**] your households, as Adonoy, your God, has blessed you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:8)


לָא תַעְבְּדוּן כְּכֹל דִּי אֲנַחְנָא עָבְדִין הָכָא יוֹמָא דֵין גְּבַר כָּל מָן דְּכָשָׁר **קֳדָמוֹהִי**:


You may not do everything we do here today, each what is upright in his eyes [**before him**].


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:9)


אֲרֵי לָא אֲתֵיתוּן עַד כְּעָן **לְבֵית נְיָחָא** וּלְאַחֲסָנָא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ:


For you will not have come as yet to the peace [**the place of tranquility**] and to the territory that Adonoy, your God, is giving you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:10)


וְתַעְבְּרוּן יָת יַרְדְּנָא וְתֵיתְבוּן בְּאַרְעָא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן מַחֲסֵן יָתְכוֹן וִינִיחַ לְכוֹן מִכָּל בַּעֲלֵי **דְבָבֵיכוֹן** מִסְּחוֹר סְחוֹר וְתֵיתְבוּן לְרָחְצָן:


When you cross the Yardein and settle in the land that Adonoy, your God, is apportioning to you, and He has granted you peace from all your enemies around, and you will live secure.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:11)


וִיהֵי אַתְרָא דִּי יִתִּרְעֵי יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן בֵּיהּ לְאַשְׁרָאָה **שְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ** תַּמָּן לְתַמָּן תַּיְתוּן יָת כָּל דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקֵּד יָתְכוֹן עֲלָוָתֵיכוֹן וְנִכְסַת **קוּדְשֵׁיכוֹן** מַעְשְׂרָתֵיכוֹן וְאַפְרָשׁוּת יֶדְכוֹן וְכֹל שְׁפַר נִדְרֵיכוֹן דִּי תִדְּרוּן **קֳדָם** יְיָ:


Let it be that the place where Adonoy, your God, chooses to place His Presence [**Shechinah**] therein—it is there you shall bring all that I am commanding you: your burnt-offerings and your sacred-offerings, your tithes and your hand-offerings, and your choice promissory offerings that you pledge to [**before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:12)


וְתֶחֱדוּן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן אַתּוּן וּבְנֵיכוֹן וּבְנָתֵיכוֹן וְעַבְדֵיכוֹן וְאַמְהָתֵיכוֹן וְלֵוָאָה דִּי בְקִרְוֵיכוֹן אֲרֵי לֵית לֵיהּ חֳלָק וְאַחֲסָנָא עִמְּכוֹן:


You are to rejoice in the presence of Adonoy, your God, you and your sons and your daughters, your male slaves and your female slaves, and the Levite who is in your cities, for he has no portion or territory with you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:13)


אִסְתַּמַּר לָךְ דִּילְמָא תַסֵּק עֲלָוָתָךְ בְּכָל אַתְרָא דִּי תֶחֱזֵי:


Take heed, lest you offer up your burnt-offerings anywhere you envision as proper.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:14)


אֶלָּהֵן בְּאַתְרָא דִּי יִתִּרְעֵי יְיָ בְּחַד מִן שִׁבְטָיךְ תַּמָּן תַּסֵּק עֲלָוָתָךְ וְתַמָּן תַּעְבֵּד כֹּל דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקְּדָךְ:


Solely in the place that Adonoy chooses within one of your tribes, there are you to offer up your burnt-offerings, and there are you to do everything that I am commanding you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:15)


לְחוֹד בְּכָל רְעוּת נַפְשָׁךְ תִּכּוֹס וְתֵיכוּל בִּשְׂרָא כְּבִרְכְּתָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ דִּי יְהַב לָךְ בְּכָל קִרְוָיךְ מְסָאָבָא וְדַכְיָא יֵכְלֻנֵיהּ **כִּבְשַׂר** טַבְיָא וְאַיְלָא:


Except with the fullness of your appetite you may slaughter and eat meat in accord with the blessing of Adonoy, your God, that He has given you in all your cities; the ritually unclean and the clean will eat it like the [**meat of the**] deer and the gazelle.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:16)


לְחוֹד דְּמָא לָא תֵיכְלוּן עַל אַרְעָא תִשְׁדִּנֵּיהּ כְּמַיָּא:


However, you may not eat the blood; on the earth are you to spill it like water.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:17)


לֵית לָךְ רְשׁוּ לְמֵיכַל בְּקִרְוָיךְ מַעְשַׂר עִבוּרָךְ חַמְרָךְ וּמִשְׁחָךְ וּבְכוֹרֵי תוֹרָךְ וְעָנָךְ וְכָל נִדְרָךְ דִּי תִדַּר וְנִדְבָתָיךְ **וְאַפְרָשׁוּת** יְדָךְ:


You are not permitted to eat in your cities the tithe of your grain and your wine and your olive oil, and the first-born of your cattle and your flocks, and all your pledges that you pledge, and your donations and the terumah-separation of your hand**-offerings**].


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:18)


אֶלָּהֵן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ תֵּיכְלִנֵּיהּ בְּאַתְרָא דִּי יִתִּרְעֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בֵּיהּ אַתְּ וּבְרָךְ וּבְרַתָּךְ וְעַבְדָּךְ וְאַמְתָךְ וְלֵוָאָה דִּי בְקִרְוָיךְ וְתֶחֱדֵי קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בְּכָל אוֹשָׁטוּת יְדָךְ:


Rather, before Adonoy, your God, are you to eat it, in the place that Adonoy, your God, chooses—you and your son and your daughter, and your male slave and your female slave, and the Levite in your cities; and you will be happy in the presence of Adonoy, your God, with the sum of your handiwork.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:19)


אִסְתַּמַּר לָךְ דִּילְמָא תִשְׁבּוֹק יָת לֵוָאֵי כָּל יוֹמָיךְ עַל אַרְעָךְ:


Watch yourself, lest you forsake the Levite, all your years on your land.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:20)


אֲרֵי יַפְתֵּי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָת תְּחוּמָךְ כְּמָא דִי מַלִּיל לָךְ וְתֵימַר אֵכוּל בִּשְׂרָא אֲרֵי תִתִּרְעֵי נַפְשָׁךְ לְמֵיכַל בִּשְׂרָא בְּכָל רְעוּת נַפְשָׁךְ תֵּיכוּל בִּשְׂרָא:


When Adonoy, your God, expands your border as He promised you, and you say, ‘‘I would like to eat meat’’ because you have an appetite to eat meat; to the full extent of your appetite eat meat.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:21)


אֲרֵי יִתְרָחַק מִנָּךְ אַתְרָא דִּי יִתִּרְעֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְאַשְׁרָאָה **שְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ** תַּמָּן וְתִכּוֹס מִתּוֹרָךְ וּמֵעָנָךְ דִּי יְהַב יְיָ לָךְ כְּמָא דִי פַּקֵּדִתָּךְ וְתֵיכוּל בְּקִרְוָיךְ בְּכֹל רְעוּת נַפְשָׁךְ:


When the place is distant from you that Adonoy, your God, chooses to set His Presence [**Shechinah**] there, you may slaughter some of your cattle or your flocks that Adonoy gave you, as I have commanded you; and you will eat in your cities with all your appetite.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:22)
