בְּרַם כְּמָא דִי מִתְאֲכֵל **בְּשַׂר** טַבְיָא וְאַיְלָא כֵּן תֵּיכְלִנֵּיהּ מְסָאָבָא וְדַכְיָא כַּחֲדָא יֵכְלִנֵּיהּ:


Only, as the [**meat of the**] dear and the gazelle may be eaten, so may you eat it; the ritually unclean and the clean together may eat it.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:23)


לְחוֹד תְּקֵף **בְּדִיל** דְּלָא לְמֵיכַל דְּמָא אֲרֵי דְמָא הוּא נַפְשָׁא וְלָא תֵיכוּל נַפְשָׁא עִם בִּשְׂרָא:


Only, exercise strength in not eating [**in order not to eat**] the blood, for the blood is the life; and do not eat the spirit with the meat.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:24)


לָא תֵּיכְלִנֵּיהּ עַל אַרְעָא תִשְׁדִּנֵּיהּ כְּמַיָּא:


Do not eat it; onto the earth are you to spill it like water.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:25)


לָא תֵּיכְלִנֵּיהּ בְּדִיל דְּיֵיטַב לָךְ וְלִבְנָיךְ בַּתְרָךְ אֲרֵי תַעְבֵּד **דְּכָשָׁר** קֳדָם יְיָ:


Do not eat it, so that you may enjoy beneficience, and your children after you, when you do the upright [**proper**] in Adonoy’s eyes.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:26)


לְחוֹד **מַעְשַׂר** קוּדְשָׁיךְ דִּיהוֹן לָךְ וְנִדְרָךְ תִּטּוֹל וְתֵיתֵי לְאַתְרָא דִּי יִתִּרְעֵי יְיָ:


However, your sacred offerings [**tithes**] that you will have, and your pledges, you are to bring when you come to the place that Adonoy chooses.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:27)


וְתַעְבֵּד עֲלָוָתָךְ בִּשְׂרָא וּדְמָא עַל מַדְבְּחָא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ וְדַם **נִכְסַת קוּדְשָׁיךְ** יִשְׁתְּפֵךְ עַל מַדְבְּחָא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ וּבִשְׂרָא תֵּיכוּל:


You are to execute your burnt-offerings—the meat and the blood—on the altar of Adonoy, your God; and the blood of your peace [**sacred**]-offerings will be poured on the altar of Adonoy, your God, then you will eat the meat.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:28)


טַר **וּתְקַבַּל** יָת כָּל פִּתְגָּמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקְּדָךְ בְּדִיל דְּיֵיטַב לָךְ וְלִבְנָיךְ בַּתְרָךְ עַד עֳלָם אֲרֵי תַעְבֵּד **דְּתָקֵן וּדְכָשָׁר קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ:


Preserve and heed [**obey**] all these words that I am commanding you, in order that you have it good and your children after you forever, when you do that which is good and that which is upright [**correct and proper before**] in the eyes of Adonoy, your God.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:29)


אֲרֵי יְשֵׁצֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָת עַמְמַיָּא דִּי אַתְּ עָלֵל לְתַמָּן לְתָרָכָא יָתְהוֹן מִקֳּדָמָךְ וְתֵירַת יָתְהוֹן וְתֵיתַב בְּאַרְעֲהוֹן:


When Adonoy, your God, will have eliminated the nations whom you are coming there to inherit, from before you; and you inherit them and dwell in their land;


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:30)


אִסְתַּמַּר לָךְ דִּילְמָא תִתָּקַל בַּתְרֵיהוֹן בָּתַר דְּיִשְׁתֵּצוּן מִקֳּדָמָךְ וְדִילְמָא תִתְבַּע **לְטַעֲוָתְהוֹן** לְמֵימַר אֶכְדֵּין פָּלְחִין עַמְמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין יָת **טַעֲוָתְהוֹן** וְאֶעְבֵּד כֵּן אַף אָנָא:


Look out for yourself, lest you are torn apart along with them, after they have been annihilated before you, and lest you inquire about their gods [**idols**], saying, ‘‘How did these nations worship their gods, let me do so, too.’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 12:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/12:31)


לָא תַעְבֵּד כֵּן **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ אֲרֵי כָּל **דִּמְרָחָק קֳדָם** יְיָ דִּי סָנֵי עֲבָדוּ **לְטַעֲוָתְהוֹן** אֲרֵי אַף יָת בְּנֵיהוֹן וְיָת בְּנָתֵיהוֹן יוֹקְדִין בְּנוּרָא **לְטַעֲוָתְהוֹן**:


Do not do so to [**before**] Adonoy, your God, because whatever is abominated by [**distanced before**] God, what He hates, they have done for their gods [**idols**]; for even their sons and their daughters do they burn in fire to their gods [**idols**].



## Chapter 13

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:1)


יָת כָּל פִּתְגָּמָא דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקֵּד יָתְכוֹן יָתֵיהּ תִּטְּרוּן לְמֶעְבָּד לָא תוֹסְפוּן עֲלוֹהִי וְלָא תִמְנְעוּן מִנֵּיהּ:


Everything that I am commanding you—be careful to fulfill it; do not add to it and do not subtract from it.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:2)


אֲרֵי יְקוּם בֵּינָךְ נְבִיָּא אוֹ חָלֵם חֶלְמָא וְיִתֶּן לָךְ אָת אוֹ מוֹפְתָא:


If a prophet arises among you or a dreamer of a dream, and he gives you an omen or a miracle,


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:3)


וְיֵיתֵי אָתָא וּמוֹפְתָא דִּי מַלִּיל עִמָּךְ לְמֵימָר נְהַךְ בָּתַר **טַעֲוַת עַמְמַיָּא** דִּי לָא יְדַעְתָּנוּן וְנִפְלְחִנּוּן:


And the omen or the miracle happens—the one he told you about—saying, ‘‘Let us go after other gods [**idols of other nations**], that you do not know, and let us serve them.’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:4)


לָא **תְקַבֵּל** לְפִתְגָּמֵי נְבִיָּא הַהוּא אוֹ לְחָלֵם חֶלְמָא הַהוּא אֲרֵי מְנַסֵּי יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן יָתְכוֹן לְמִדַּע הַאִיתֵיכוֹן רָחֲמִין יָת יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן בְּכָל לִבְּכוֹן וּבְכָל נַפְשְׁכוֹן:


Do not listen to [**accept**] the words of that prophet or to that dreamer of a dream, because Adonoy, your God, is testing you to know whether you love Adonoy, your God, wholeheartedly and with your entire beings.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:5)


בָּתַר **דַּחַלְתָּא** דַיְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן תְּהָכוּן וְיָתֵיהּ תִּדְחֲלוּן וְיָת פִּקּוֹדוֹהִי תִטְּרוּן **וּלְמֵימְרֵיהּ תְּקַבְּלוּן וְקָדָמוֹהִי** תִפְלְחוּן **וּבְדַחַלְתֵּיהּ** תִּתְקָרְבוּן:


After [**the fear of**] Adonoy, your God, are you to go, fear Him, keep His commandments, heed His voice [**accept His word**], serve [**before**] Him, and cleave to [**the fear of**] Him.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 13:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:6)


וּנְבִיָּא הַהוּא אוֹ חָלֵם חֶלְמָא הַהוּא יִתְקְטֵל אֲרֵי מַלִּיל סַטְיָא עַל יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן דִּי אַפֵּיק יָתְכוֹן מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם וּדְפָרְקָךְ מִבֵּית עַבְדוּתָא לְאַטְעֲיוּתָךְ מִן אָרְחָא דִּי פַקְּדָךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לִמְהַךְ בַּהּ וּתְפַלֵּי עָבֵד **דְּבִישׁ** מִבֵּינָךְ:


And that prophet or that dreamer of a dream is to be executed, for he has uttered a fabrication about Adonoy, your God—Who took you out of the land of Egypt, and Who redeemed you from the house of bondage—to mislead you from the way that Adonoy, your God, commanded you to go upon, and you will eliminate the evil [**doers**] from within you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 13:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:7)


אֲרֵי יְמַלְּכִנָּךְ אֲחוּךְ בַּר אִמָּךְ אוֹ בְרָךְ אוֹ בְרַתָּךְ אוֹ אִתַּת **קְיָמָךְ** אוֹ חַבְרָךְ דִּי כְנַפְשָׁךְ בְּסִתְרָא לְמֵימָר נְהַךְ וְנִפְלַח **לְטַעֲוַת עַמְמַיָּא** דִּי לָא יְדַעַתְּ אַתְּ וַאֲבָהָתָךְ:


If one shall incite you, your brother—son of your mother—or your son or your daughter, or the wife of your bosom [**covenant**], or your neighbor who is your soul mate, clandestinely, saying, ‘‘Let us go and serve other gods [**idols of other nations**]’’ whom you never knew—neither you nor your forefathers.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 13:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:8)


**מִטַּעֲוַת** עַמְמַיָּא דִּי בְסַחֲרָנֵיכוֹן דִּקְרִיבִין לָךְ אוֹ דִּרְחִיקִין מִנָּךְ מִסְּיָפֵי אַרְעָא וְעַד סְיָפֵי אַרְעָא:


From the gods [**idols**] of the nations that surround you, who are near you or at a distance from you, from the ends of the earth to the ends of the earth,


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 13:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:9)


לָא תֵיבֵי לֵיהּ וְלָא **תְקַבֵּל** מִנֵּיהּ וְלָא תְחוּס עֵינָךְ עֲלוֹהִי וְלָא תְרַחַם וְלָא תְכַסֵּי עֲלוֹהִי:


Do not be favorbly inclined towards him, and do not listen to [**accept from**] him; and do not view him compassionately, and do not take pity and do not cover for him.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 13:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:10)


אֲרֵי מִקְטַל תִּקְטְלִנֵּיהּ יְדָךְ תְּהֵי בֵיהּ בְּקַדְמֵיתָא לְמִקְטְלֵיהּ וִידָא דְכָל עַמָּא בְּבַתְרֵיתָא:


For you must surely execute him. Let your hand strike him first to execute him, and the hand of the entire people afterward.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 13:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:11)


וְתִרְגְּמִנֵּיהּ בְּאַבְנַיָּא וִימוּת אֲרֵי בָעָא לְאַטְעֲיוּתָךְ **מִדַּחַלְתָּא** דַיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ דִּי אַפְּקָךְ מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם מִבֵּית עַבְדוּתָא:


And you are to stone him with stones so that he dies, for he sought to mislead you from [**the fear of**] Adonoy, your God, Who took you out of the land of Egypt, from the house of slavery.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 13:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:12)


וְכָל יִשְׂרָאֵל יִשְׁמְעוּן וְיִדְחֲלוּן וְלָא יוֹסְפוּן לְמֶעְבַּד כְּפִתְגָּמָא בִישָׁא הָדֵין בֵּינָךְ:


And all of Yisroel will hear and will fear, and they will not proceed to do so evil a thing as this, within you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 13:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:13)


אֲרֵי תִשְׁמַע בְּחַד מִקִּרְוָיךְ דִּי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ לְמִתַּב תַּמָּן לְמֵימָר:


If you hear in one of your cities that Adonoy, your God, is giving you in which to settle, the following:


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 13:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:14)


נְפָקוּ גֻּבְרִין **בְּנֵי רִשְׁעָא** מִבֵּינָךְ וְאַטְעָיוּ יָת יָתְבֵי קַרְתְּהוֹן לְמֵימָר נְהַךְ וְנִפְלַח **לְטַעֲוַת עַמְמַיָּא** דִּי לָא יְדַעְתּוּן:


‘‘Men have gone out—unscrupulous [**evil**]—from among you, and they have misled the inhabitants of their city by saying, ‘Let us go and serve other gods [**idols of other nations**]’ that you do not know.’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 13:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:15)


וְתִתְבַּע וְתִבְדּוֹק וְתִשְׁאַל יָאוּת וְהָא קֻשְׁטָא כֵּוָן פִּתְגָּמָא אִתְעֲבִידָא תּוֹעֶבְתָּא הָדָא בֵּינָךְ:


You must investigate, inquire, and interrogate thoroughly; and if in fact the report is true and accurate—this abomination was committed among you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 13:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:16)


מִמְחָא תִמְחֵי יָת יָתְבֵי קַרְתָּא הַהִיא לְפִתְגַּם דְּחָרֶב גַּמַּר יָתַהּ וְיָת כָּל דִּי בַהּ וְיָת בְּעִירַהּ לְפִתְגַּם דְּחָרֶב:


You must surely strike down the inhabitants of that city by the sword; annihilate it and everything that is in it, and its livestock by the sword.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 13:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:17)


וְיָת כָּל עֲדָאַהּ תִּכְנוֹשׁ לְגוֹ פְתָיַּהּ וְתוֹקֵד בְּנוּרָא יָת קַרְתָּא וְיָת כָּל עֲדָאַהּ גְּמִיר **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ וּתְהֵי תֵּל חָרִיב לְעָלָם לָא תִתְבְּנֵי עוֹד:


And all its booty are you to collect within its plaza, and you are to burn in fire the city and all its booty—totally—for [**before**] Adonoy, your God; and it will be a ruin forever, it is never to be rebuilt.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 13:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:18)


וְלָא יִדְבַּק בִּידָךְ מִדַּעַם מִן חֶרְמָא בְּדִיל דִּיתוּב יְיָ מִתְּקוֹף רוּגְזֵיהּ וְיֵהַב לָךְ רַחֲמִין וִירַחַם עֲלָךְ וְיַסְגִּנָּךְ כְּמָא דִי קַיִּים לַאֲבָהָתָךְ:


Let nothing of the *cherem* cling to your hand; so that Adonoy withdraws His raising fury and grant you mercy, and He will be merciful toward you and multiply you as He swore to your forefathers


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 13:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/13:19)


אֲרֵי **תְקַבֵּל לְמֵימְרָא** דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְמִטַּר יָת כָּל פִּקּוֹדוֹהִי דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקְּדָךְ יוֹמָא דֵין לְמֶעְבַּד **דְּכָשָׁר קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ:


When you heed the voice [**accept the word**] of Adonoy, your God, to guard all His commandments that I am commanding you today, to do the upright [**proper before**] in the eyes of Adonoy, your God.



## Chapter 14

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:1)


בְּנִין אַתּוּן **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן לָא תִתְחַמְמוּן וְלָא תְשַׁוּוּן מְרָט בֵּין עֵינֵיכוֹן עַל מִית:


You are sons to [**before**] Adonoy, your God; do not lacerate yourselves and do not make yourselves bald between your eyes for a dead person.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:2)


אֲרֵי עַם קַדִּישׁ אַתְּ קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ וּבָךְ אִתִּרְעֵי יְיָ לְמֶהֱוֵי לֵיהּ לְעַם חַבִּיב מִכֹּל עַמְמַיָּא דִּי עַל אַפֵּי אַרְעָא:


For you are a sacred people to Adonoy, your God, and Adonoy has chosen you to be for Him a treasured people from all the peoples who are on the surface of the earth.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:3)


לָא תֵיכוּל כָּל דִּמְרָחָק:


Do not eat any abomination.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:4)


דֵּין בְּעִירָא דְּתֵיכְלוּן תּוֹרִין אִמְּרִין דִּרְחֵלִין וְגָדְיָן דְּעִזִּין:


These are the animals that you are to eat: the bovine, the sheep and the goat;


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:5)


אַיָּלָא וְטַבְיָא וְיַחְמוּרָא וְיַעְלָא וְרֵימָא וְתוֹרְבָּלָא וְדִיצָא:


The gazelle, the deer and the fallow deer; and the ibex and the adax; and the wild ox and the wild sheep.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:6)


וְכָל בְּעִירָא דִּסְדִיקָא פַרְסָתָא וּמַטִּלְפָא טִלְפִין תַּרְתֵּין פַּרְסָתָא מַסְּקָא פִשְׁרָא בִּבְעִירָא יָתַהּ תֵּיכְלוּן:


And every animal whose sole is cloven, whose hoof is truly split in two, regurgitating [its] cud within the animals, it may you eat.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:7)


בְּרַם יָת דֵּין לָא תֵיכְלוּן מִמַּסְּקֵי פִשְׁרָא וּמִמַּסְדִּיקֵי פַרְסָתָא **מַטִּלְפֵי טִלְפַיָּא** יָת גַּמְלָא וְיָת אַרְנְבָא וְיָת טַפְזָא אֲרֵי מַסְּקֵי פִשְׁרָא אִנּוּן וּפַרְסָתְהוֹן לָא סְדִיקָא מְסָאֲבִין אִנּוּן לְכוֹן:


These, however, do not eat from the regurgitators of cud and from the cloven-soled [**split into hooves**]: the dromedary, the camel, and the hare and the hyrax; for regurgitators of cud are they, but have no cloven sole; they are ritually unclean for you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:8)


וְיָת חֲזִירָא אֲרֵי מַסְדִּיק פַּרְסָתָא הוּא וְלָא פָשַׁר מְסָאָב הוּא לְכוֹן מִבִּסְרְהוֹן לָא תֵיכְלוּן וּבִנְבִלְתְּהוֹן לָא **תִקְרְבוּן**:


Also the pig, because it is cloven-soled but does not [regurgitate its] cud; it is ritually unclean for you. From their flesh do not eat, and do not come in contact [**near**] with their carcasses.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:9)


יָת דֵּין תֵּיכְלוּן מִכֹּל דִּי בְמַיָּא כֹּל דִּי לֵיהּ צִיצִין וְקַלְפִין תֵּיכְלוּן:


These are you to eat from whatever is in the water: whatever has fins and scales may you eat.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:10)


וְכֹל דִּי לֵית לֵיהּ צִיצִין וְקַלְפִין לָא תֵיכְלוּן מְסָאָב הוּא לְכוֹן:


And whatever does not have fins and scales do not eat; they are ritually unclean for you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:11)


כָּל צִפַּר דַּכְיָא תֵּיכְלוּן:


You may eat any pure bird.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:12)


וְדֵין דִּי לָא תֵיכְלוּן מִנְּהוֹן נִשְׁרָא וְעָר וְעַזְיָא:


And this is what you may not eat: the griffon vulture and the bearded vulture, and the black vulture;


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:13)


וּבַת כַּנְפָא וְטָרָפִיתָא וְדַיְּתָא לִזְנַהּ:


The ra’ah and the *ayah,* and the kite according to its kind;


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:14)


וְיָת כָּל עֹרְבָא לִזְנֵיהּ:


And every raven according to its kind;


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:15)


וְיָת בַּת נַעֲמִיתָא וְצִיצָא וְצִפַּר שַׁחְפָּא וְנָצָא לִזְנוֹהִי:


And the dark desert eagle owl and the kestrel, and the gull, and the sparrow hawk according to its kind;


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:16)


וְקַדְיָא וְקִפּוֹפָא וּבַוְתָא:


The dark little owl and the long-eared owl, and the *tinshames*;


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:17)


וְקָתָא וִירַקְרֵקָא וְשָׁלֵי נוּנָא:


And the light little owl and the Egyptian vulture, and the shalach;


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:18)


וְחַוָּרִיתָא וְאִבּוּ לִזְנַהּ וְנַגַּר טוּרָא וַעֲטַלֵּפָא:


And the stork, and the heron according to its kind, and the duchifas, and the bat.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:19)


וְכֹל רִחֲשָׁא דְעוֹפָא מְסָאָב הוּא לְכוֹן לָא יִתְאָכְלוּן:


And all flying creeping creatures are ritually unclean for you; they may not be eaten.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:20)


כָּל עוֹפָא דְכֵי תֵּיכְלוּן:


Every ritually clean bird you may eat.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:21)


לָא תֵיכְלוּן כָּל נְבִילָא לְתוֹתַב **עָרֵל** דִּי בְקִרְוָיךְ תִּתְּנִנַּהּ וְיֵיכְלִנַּהּ אוֹ תְזַבְּנִנַּהּ **לְבַר עַמְמִין** אֲרֵי עַם קַדִּישׁ אַתְּ קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לָא תֵיכוּל **בְּשַׂר בַּחֲלָב**:


Do not eat any carcass; to the [**uncircumcised**] alien in your cities give it and he will eat it or sell it to a non-Jew [**foreigner**], for you are a people sanctified to Adonoy, your God; do not cook a kid in its mother’s milk [**milk with meat**].


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:22)


עַשְּׂרָא תְעַשֵּׂר יָת כָּל עֲלָלַת זַרְעָךְ דְּיִפּוֹק חַקְלָא שַׁתָּא בְשַׁתָּא:


You must surely tithe all the produce of your planting, that your field yields on a yearly basis.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:23)


וְתֵיכוּל קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בְּאַתְרָא דִּי יִתִּרְעֵי לְאַשְׁרָאָה **שְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ** תַּמָּן מַעְשַׂר עִבוּרָךְ חַמְרָךְ וּמִשְׁחָךְ וּבְכוֹרֵי תוֹרָךְ וְעָנָךְ בְּדִיל דְּתֵילַף לְמִדְחַל **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ כָּל יוֹמַיָּא:


You shall eat in the presence of Adonoy, your God, in the place He chooses to house His Presence [**Shechinah**] there—the tithe of your grain, your wine, and your olive oil, and the firstborn of your cattle and flocks; in order that you learn to fear [**before**] Adonoy, your God, all the years.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:24)


וַאֲרֵי יִסְגֵּי מִנָּךְ אָרְחָא אֲרֵי לָא תִכּוּל לְמִטְּלֵיהּ אֲרֵי יִתְרָחַק מִנָּךְ אַתְרָא דִּי יִתִּרְעֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְאַשְׁרָאָה **שְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ** תַּמָּן אֲרֵי יְבָרֵכִנָּךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ:


If the journey will be beyond you; if you will be unable to carry it because the place is distant for you where Adonoy, your God, chooses to set His Presence [**Shechinah**] there, for Adonoy, your God, has blessed you—


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:25)


וְתִתֵּן בְּכַסְפָּא וּתְצוּר כַּסְפָּא בִּידָךְ וּתְהַךְ לְאַתְרָא דִּי יִתִּרְעֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בֵּיהּ:


you will substitute coins. You will bundle the coins in your hand, and will go to the place that Adonoy, your God, chooses.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:26)


וְתִתֵּן כַּסְפָּא בְּכֹל דִּי יִתִּרְעֵי נַפְשָׁךְ בְּתוֹרֵי וּבְעָנָא **וּבַחֲמַר חֲדַת וְעַתִּיק** וּבְכֹל דִּי תִשְׁאֲלִנָּךְ נַפְשָׁךְ וְתֵיכוּל תַּמָּן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ וְתֶחֱדֵי אַתְּ **וֶאֱנַשׁ** בֵּיתָךְ:


You will spend the money for anything you desire—for cattle, sheep, wine, intoxicating liquor [**fresh and aged wine**]—and for anything that you wish; and you will eat there in the presence of Adonoy, your God, and will rejoice, you and [**the members of**] your household.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:27)


וְלֵוָאָה דִי בְקִרְוָיךְ לָא תִשְׁבְּקִנֵּיהּ אֲרֵי לֵית לֵיהּ חֳלָק וְאַחֲסָנָא עִמָּךְ:


And the Levite who is in your city, you must not abandon; since he has no portion or inheritance with you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:28)


מִסּוֹף תְּלַת שְׁנִין תַּפֵּק יָת כָּל מַעְשַׂר עֲלַלְתָּךְ בְּשַׁתָּא הַהִיא וְתַצְנַע בְּקִרְוָיךְ:


At the end of three years, separate all the tithes of your produce of that year and set them aside in your city.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 14:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/14:29)


וְיֵיתֵי לֵוָאָה אֲרֵי לֵית לֵיהּ חֳלָק וְאַחֲסָנָא עִמָּךְ וְגִיּוֹרָא וְיִתַּמָּא וְאַרְמְלָא דִּי בְקִרְוָיךְ וְיֵיכְלוּן וְיִשְׂבְּעוּן בְּדִיל דִּי יְבָרֵכִנָּךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בְּכָל עוֹבָדֵי יְדָךְ דִּי תַעְבֵּד:


The Levite shall come, for he has no portion or inheritance with you, and the proselyte and the orphan and the widow who are in your city, let them eat their fill; in order that Adonoy, your God, bless you in all the endeavors that you make.



## Chapter 15

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:1)


מִסּוֹף שְׁבַע שְׁנִין תַּעְבֵּד שְׁמִטְּתָא:


At the end of seven years, you are to make *shemitah.*


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:2)


וְדֵין פִּתְגַּם שְׁמִטְּתָא דְּתַשְׁמֵט כָּל גְּבַר מָרֵי **רְשׁוּ** דִּי יַרְשֵׁי בְּחַבְרֵיהּ לָא יִתְבַּע מִן חַבְרֵיהּ וּמִן אֲחוּהִי אֲרֵי קְרָא שְׁמִטְּתָא **קֳדָם** יְיָ:


And this is the statement of *shemitah:* suspend every creditor’s hand from his loan to his neighbor; he will not claim [it] from his neighbor or his brother because it was proclaimed *shemitah* to [**before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:3)


מִן **בַּר עַמְמִין** תִּתְבַּע וְדִי יְהֵי לָךְ עִם אָחוּךְ תַּשְׁמֵט יְדָךְ:


You should demand payment from the stranger [**foreigner**], but what you have with your brother your hand will relinquish.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:4)


לְחוֹד אֲרֵי לָא יְהֵי בָךְ מִסְכֵּנָא אֲרֵי בָרָכָא יְבָרֵכִנָּךְ יְיָ בְּאַרְעָא דִּי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ אַחֲסָנָא לְמֵירְתַהּ:


End it [poverty], so that there will be no one, destitute among you when Adonoy will surely bless you in the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you as territory to inherit.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:5)


לְחוֹד אִם **קַבָּלָא תְקַבֵּל לְמֵימְרָא** דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְמִטַּר לְמֶעְבַּד יָת כָּל תַּפְקֶדְתָּא הָדָא דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקְּדָךְ יוֹמָא דֵין:


Only if you will listen to [**heed**] the voice [**word**] of Adonoy, your God, to guard to fulfill this entire *mitzvah* that I am commanding you today.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 15:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:6)


אֲרֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בָּרְכָךְ כְּמָא דִּי מַלִּיל לָךְ וְתוֹזֵף לְעַמְמִין סַגִּיאִין וְאַתְּ לָא תְזוּף וְתִשְׁלוֹט בְּעַמְמִין סַגִּיאִין וּבָךְ לָא יִשְׁלְטוּן:


When Adonoy, your God, blesses you just as He told you, you will grant loans to nations but you will not borrow; and you will rule over many nations, but over you they will not rule.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 15:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:7)


אֲרֵי יְהֵי בָךְ מִסְכֵּנָא חַד מֵאַחָיךְ בַּחֲדָא מִקִּרְוָיךְ בְּאַרְעָךְ דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ לָא תַתְקֵף יָת לִבָּךְ וְלָא תִקְפֹּץ יָת יְדָךְ מֵאֲחוּךְ מִסְכֵּנָא:


If there should be someone destitute in your midst among one of your brothers in one of your cities, in your land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you; do not harden your heart and do not close your hand against your destitute brother.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 15:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:8)


אֶלָּא מִפְתַּח תִּפְתַּח יָת יְדָךְ לֵיהּ וְאוֹזָפָא תּוֹזְפִנֵּיהּ **כְּמִסַּת** חֶסְרוֹנֵיהּ דְּיַחֲסִיר לֵיהּ:


Rather, you must surely open your hand generously to him, and you must surely extend a loan to him to cover the wants [**the measure of that**] which he lacks.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 15:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:9)


אִסְתַּמַּר לָךְ דִּילְמָא יְהֵי פִתְגָּם עִם לִבָּךְ בִּרְשַׁע לְמֵימַר קְרִיבָא שַׁתָּא שְׁבִיעֲתָא שַׁתָּא דִשְׁמִטְּתָא וְתַבְאֵשׁ עֵינָךְ בְּאָחוּךְ מִסְכֵּנָא וְלָא תִתֵּן לֵיהּ וְיִקְרֵי עֲלָךְ קֳדָם יְיָ וִיהֵי בָךְ חוֹבָא:


Look out for yourself lest there be an evil thought in your mind, saying, ‘‘The seventh year is approaching, the *shemitah* year,’’ and you will look askance at your destitute brother and will not give him; and if he cries out concerning you to Adonoy, you will be regarded as sinful.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 15:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:10)


מִתַּן תִּתֵּן לֵיהּ וְלָא יַבְאֵשׁ לִבָּךְ בְּמִתְּנָךְ לֵיהּ אֲרֵי בְּדִיל פִּתְגָּמָא הָדֵין יְבָרֵכִנָּךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בְּכָל עוֹבָדָךְ וּבְכֹל אוֹשָׁטוּת יְדָךְ:


You must surely give him, and let your heart not hurt when you give him; for, as a consequence of this thing, Adonoy, your God, will bless you in all your work and in all your commerce.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 15:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:11)


אֲרֵי לָא יִפְסוֹק מִסְכֵּנָא מִגּוֹ אַרְעָא עַל כֵּן אֲנָא מְפַקְּדָךְ לְמֵימַר מִפְתַּח תִּפְתַּח יָת יְדָךְ לַאֲחוּךְ לְעַנְיָךְ וּלְמִסְכֵּנָךְ בְּאַרְעָךְ:


For the destitute will not cease to exist within the land; therefore I am commanding you saying, ‘‘Open your hand generously to your brother, to your indigent, and to your destitute in your land.’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 15:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:12)


אֲרֵי יִזְדַּבַּן לָךְ אֲחוּךְ בַּר יִשְׂרָאֵל אוֹ בַּת יִשְׂרָאֵל וְיִפְלְחִנָּךְ שִׁית שְׁנִין וּבְשַׁתָּא שְׁבִיעֲתָא **תִּפְטְרִנֵּיהּ** בַּר חוֹרִין מֵעִמָּךְ:


If your brother—Jew or Jewess—is sold to you and he serves you six years; then in the seventh year you are to send him free from you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 15:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:13)


וַאֲרֵי תִפְטְרִנֵּיהּ **בַּר חוֹרִין** מֵעִמָּךְ לָא תִפְטְרִנֵּיהּ רֵיקָן:


When you send him [**a**] free [**man**] from you, do not send him away empty-handed.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 15:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:14)


אַפְרָשָׁא תַפְרֵשׁ לֵיהּ מֵעָנָךְ וּמֵאִדְּרָךְ וּמִמַּעֲצַרְתָּךְ דִּי בָרְכָךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ תִּתֶּן לֵיהּ:


You must surely present him with a severance bonus from your flocks, and from your threshing area, and your wine cellar, whatever Adonoy, your God, has blessed you with are you to give him.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 15:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:15)


וְתִדְכַּר אֲרֵי עַבְדָּא הֲוֵיתָא בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם וּפָרְקָךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ עַל כֵּן אֲנָא מְפַקְּדָךְ יָת פִּתְגָּמָא הָדֵין יוֹמָא דֵין:


Remember that you were a slave in the land of Egypt and Adonoy, your God, redeemed you; that is why I am commanding you this matter today.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 15:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:16)


וִיהֵי אֲרֵי יֵימַר לָךְ לָא אֶפּוֹק מֵעִמָּךְ אֲרֵי רָחֲמָךְ וְיָת **אֱנַשׁ** בֵּיתָךְ אֲרֵי טַב לֵיהּ עִמָּךְ:


Should he say to you, ‘‘I am not leaving you;’’ because he loves you and [**the members of**] your household, for he has it good with you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 15:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:17)


וְתִסַּב יָת מַרְצְעָא וְתִתֵּן בְּאֻדְנֵיהּ וּבְדָשָׁא וִיהֵי לָךְ **עִבֵד פָּלַח** לְעָלָם וְאַף לְאַמְתָךְ תַּעְבֶּד כֵּן:


Take the awl and put it through his ear and the door, and he will become your perpetual slave [**ministering servant**]. And do the same to your maid-servant as well.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 15:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:18)


לָא יִקְשֵׁי בְעֵינָךְ בְּמִפְטְרָךְ יָתֵיהּ **בַּר חוֹרִין** מֵעִמָּךְ אֲרֵי **עַל חַד תְּרֵין** כַּאֲגַר אֲגִירָא פָּלְחָךְ שִׁית שְׁנִין וִיבָרֵכִנָּךְ יְיָ אֳלָהָךְ בְּכֹל דִּי תַעְבֵּד:


Let it not seem difficult to you when you send him [**a**] free [**man**] from you, for double [**two for one**] the profit produced by the wage earner has he worked for you six years; and Adonoy, your God, will bless you in everything you do.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 15:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:19)


כָּל בּוּכְרָא דִּי יִתְיְלִיד בְּתוֹרָךְ וּבְעָנָךְ דִּכְרִין תַּקְדֵּשׁ קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לָא תִפְלַח בְּבוּכְרָא דְתוֹרָךְ וְלָא תֵגוֹז בּוּכְרָא דְעָנָךְ


Every firstborn that is born in your cattle, and in your flocks—a male—you must consecrate to Adonoy, your God; you may not work with your first-born ox, or shear the first-born of your flocks.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 15:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:20)


קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ תֵּיכְלִנֵּיהּ שַׁתָּא בְשַׁתָּא בְּאַתְרָא דִּי יִתִּרְעֵי יְיָ אַתְּ **וֶאֱנַשׁ** בֵּיתָךְ:


Before Adonoy, your God, you must eat it, each year, in the place that Adonoy chooses, you and [**the members of**] your household.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 15:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:21)


וַאֲרֵי יְהֵי בֵיהּ מוּמָא חֲגִיר אוֹ עֲוִיר כֹּל מוּם בִּישׁ לָא תִכְּסִנֵּיהּ **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ:


And if it has a blemish—if it is crippled or blind—or has any severe blemish, do not slaughter it to Adonoy, your God.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 15:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:22)


בְּקִרְוָיךְ תֵּיכְלִנֵּיהּ מְסָאָבָא וְדַכְיָא כַּחֲדָא **כִּבְסַר** טַבְיָא וְאַיְלָא:


In your cities may you eat it; the ritually unclean and the clean together, like the [**meat of the**] deer and the gazelle.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 15:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/15:23)


לְחוֹד יָת דְּמֵיהּ לָא תֵיכוּל עַל אַרְעָא תִשְׁדִּנֵּיהּ כְּמַיָּא:


Only, do not eat its blood; spill it like water on the ground.



## Chapter 16

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:1)


**טַר** יָת יַרְחָא דַאֲבִיבָא וְתַעְבֵּד פִּסְחָא **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ אֲרֵי בְּיַרְחָא דַאֲבִיבָא אַפְּקָךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ מִמִּצְרַיִם **וַעֲבַד לָךְ נִסִּין** בְּלֵילְיָא:


Take heed of [**observe**] the month of spring, when you will celebrate *Pesach* for [**before**] Adonoy your God; for in the month of spring Adonoy, your God, took you out from Egypt [**and performed miracles for you**] at night.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:2)


וְתִכּוֹס פִּסְחָא **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ **מִן בְּנֵי עָנָא וְנִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָּא מִן תּוֹרֵי** בְּאַתְרָא דִּי יִתִּרְעֵי יְיָ לְאַשְׁרָאָה **שְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ** תַּמָּן:


You shall slaughter the *pesach*-offering to [**before**] Adonoy, your God, flocks of ruminants [**young lambs**] and cattle [**sacred offerings of oxen**] in the place that Adonoy chooses to house His Presence [**Shechinah**] there.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:3)


לָא תֵיכוּל עֲלוֹהִי חֲמִיעַ שִׁבְעָא יוֹמִין תֵּיכוּל עֲלוֹהִי פַטִּיר לְחֵם עֹנִי אֲרֵי בִּבְהִילוּ נְפַקְתָּא מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם בְּדִיל דְּתִדְכַּר יָת יוֹמָא מִפְּקָךְ מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם כָּל יוֹמֵי חַיָּיךְ:


Do not eat *chometz* on it; seven days are you to eat on it *matzos,* bread of anguish; since in haste you left the land of Egypt, so that you remember the day of your exodus from the land of Egypt all the days of your life.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:4)


וְלָא יִתְחֲזֵי לָךְ חֲמִיר בְּכָל תְּחוּמָךְ שִׁבְעָא יוֹמִין וְלָא יְבִית מִן בִּסְרָא דִּי תִכּוֹס בְּרַמְשָׁא בְּיוֹמָא קַדְמָאָה לְצַפְרָא:


And no sourdough of yours may be seen in all of your boundary seven days; and none of the flesh may remain overnight which you slaughtered towards the evening of the first day—until morning.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:5)


לֵית **לָךְ רְשׁוּ** לְמִכֹּס יָת פִּסְחָא בַּחֲדָא מִקִּרְוָיךְ דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ:


You are forbidden [**have no permission**] to slaughter the *pesach* in any of your cities that Adonoy, your God, is giving you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 16:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:6)


אֶלָּהֵן לְאַתְרָא דִּי יִתִּרְעֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְאַשְׁרָאָה **שְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ** תַּמָּן תִּכּוֹס יָת פִּסְחָא בְּרַמְשָׁא כְּמֵעַל שִׁמְשָׁא זְמַן מִפְּקָךְ מִמִּצְרָיִם:


Solely in the place that Adonoy, your God, chooses to house His Presence [**Shechinah**], there will you slaughter the *pesach* towards the afternoon, at sunset, at the time you left Egypt.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 16:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:7)


וּתְבַשַּׁל וְתֵיכוּל בְּאַתְרָא דִּי יִתִּרְעֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בֵּיהּ וְתִתְפְּנֵי בְצַפְרָא וּתְהַךְ לְקִרְוָךְ:


You shall cook [it] and eat [it] in the place that Adonoy, your God, chooses; and you may depart in the morning and go to your residence.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 16:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:8)


שִׁתָּא יוֹמִין תֵּיכוּל פַּטִּירָא וּבְיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה **כְּנֵשׁ** קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לָא תַעְבֵּד עִבִידָא:


For six days you shall eat *matzos,* and on the seventh day, is one of withdrawal [**assembly**] for the sake of Adonoy, your God, do not do work.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 16:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:9)


שִׁבְעָא שָׁבוּעִין תִּמְנֵי לָךְ מִדְּשַׁרְיוּת מַגְּלָא בַּחֲצַד עוּמְרָא דַאֲרָמוּתָא תִּשְׁרֵי לְמִמְנֵי שִׁבְעָא שָׁבוּעִין:


Seven weeks count for yourself; from the time the sickle begins felling the standing grain, begin to count seven weeks.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 16:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:10)


וְתַעְבֵּד חַגָּא דְשָׁבוּעַיָּא **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ מִסַּת נִדְבַת יְדָךְ דִּי תִתֵּן כְּמָא דִי יְבָרֵכִנָּךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ:


You are to celebrate the festival of *Shovuos* for [**before**] Adonoy, your God, to the fullness of your open-handed gift that you can give, as Adonoy, your God, has blessed you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 16:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:11)


וְתֶחֱדֵי **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ אַתְּ וּבְרָךְ וּבְרַתָּךְ וְעַבְדָּךְ וְאַמְתָךְ וְלֵוָאָה דִּי בְקִרְוָיךְ וְגִיּוֹרָא וְיִתַּמָּא וְאַרְמְלָא דִּי בֵינָךְ בְּאַתְרָא דִּי יִתִּרְעֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְאַשְׁרָאָה **שְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ** תַּמָּן:


You are to rejoice in the presence of [**before**] Adonoy, your God—you, and your son and your daughter, and your male slave and your female slave, and the Levite who is in your city, and the proselyte, and the orphan and the widow who are among you—in the place that Adonoy, your God, chooses to house His Presence [**Shechinah**] there.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 16:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:12)


וְתִדְכַּר אֲרֵי עַבְדָּא הֲוֵיתָא בְּמִצְרָיִם וְתִטַּר וְתַעְבֵּד יָת קְיָמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין:


Remember that you were a slave in Egypt; you are to guard and celebrate these statutes.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 16:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:13)


חַגָּא דִמְטַלַּיָּא תַּעְבֵּד לָךְ שִׁבְעָא יוֹמִין בְּמִכְנְשָׁךְ מֵאִדְּרָךְ וּמִמַּעֲצַרְתָּךְ:


The festival of *Sukkos* celebrate for yourself seven days, when you harvest your threshing-floor and your wine-press.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 16:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:14)


וְתֶחֱדֵי בְּחַגָּךְ אַתְּ וּבְרָךְ וּבְרַתָּךְ וְעַבְדָּךְ וְאַמְתָךְ וְלֵוָאָה וְגִיּוֹרָא וְיִתַּמָּא וְאַרְמְלָא דִּי בְקִרְוָיךְ:


You are to rejoice during your festival—you and your son and your daughter, and your male slave and your female slave, and the Levite and the proselyte, and the orphan and the widow who are in your city.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 16:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:15)


שִׁבְעָא יוֹמִין תֵּחוֹג קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בְּאַתְרָא דִּי יִתִּרְעֵי יְיָ אֲרֵי יְבָרֵכִנָּךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בְּכָל עֲלַלְתָּךְ וּבְכָל עוֹבָדֵי יְדָיךְ וּתְהֵי בְּרַם חָדֵי:


Seven days are you to be festive for Adonoy, your God, in the place Adonoy chooses, for Adonoy, your God, will bless you in all your produce and in all your endeavors; and you shall experience pure joy.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 16:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:16)


תְּלַת זִמְנִין בְּשַׁתָּא יִתְחֲזֵי כָל דְּכוּרָךְ **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בְּאַתְרָא דִּי יִתִּרְעֵי בְּחַגָּא דְפַטִּירַיָּא וּבְחַגָּא דְשָׁבוּעַיָּא וּבְחַגָּא דִמְטַלַּיָּא וְלָא יִתְחֲזֵי **קֳדָם** יְיָ רֵיקָנוּן:


Three times a year are all your males to be seen in the presence of [**before**] Adonoy, your God, in the place that He chooses—in the festival of Matzos, and on the festival of Shovuos, and on the festival of Sukkos—and he shall not appear in the presence of [**before**] Adonoy empty-handed.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 16:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:17)


גְּבַר כְּמַתְּנַת יְדֵיהּ כְּבִרְכְּתָא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ דִּי יְהַב לָךְ:


Everyone according to the gift appropriate to his means, according to the blessing of Adonoy, your God, that He gave you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 16:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:18)


דַּיָּנִין וּפֻרְעָנִין תְּמַנֵּי לָךְ בְּכָל קִרְוָיךְ דִּי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ לְשִׁבְטָיךְ וִידוּנוּן יָת עַמָּא **דִּין דִּקְשׁוֹט**:


Judges and police officers you shall appoint for yourself in all of your cities that Adonoy, your God, is giving you for your tribes; who will judge the people righteous justice [**true judgment**].


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 16:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:19)


לָא תַצְלֵי דִין לָא תִשְׁתְּמוֹדַע אַפִּין וְלָא תְקַבֵּל שֹׁחֲדָא אֲרֵי שֹׁחֲדָא מְעַוֵּר עֵינֵי חַכִּימִין **וּמְקַלְקֵל פִּתְגָּמִין תְּרִיצִין**:


Do not pervert justice; do not display favoritism; and do not accept bribery, for bribery blinds the eyes of the wise and distorts words that are just [**perverts the words of justice**].


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 16:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:20)


קֻשְׁטָא קֻשְׁטָא תְּהֵי רָדִיף בְּדִיל דְּתֵיחֵי וְתֵירַת יָת אַרְעָא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ:


Pursue absolute justice so that you may live and inherit the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 16:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:21)


לָא **תִצּוֹב** לָךְ אֲשֵׁרַת כָּל אִילָן בִּסְטַר מַדְבְּחָא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ דִּי תַעְבֶּד לָךְ:


Do not plant an Asherah for yourself [or] any tree near the altar of Adonoy, your God, that you will make for yourself.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 16:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/16:22)


וְלָא תְקִים לָךְ קָמָא דִּי סָנֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ:


And do not erect for yourself a monument that Adonoy, your God, detests.



## Chapter 17

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:1)


לָא תִכּוֹס **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ תּוֹר וְאִמָּר דִּי יְהֵי בֵיהּ מוּמָא כֹּל מִדַּעַם בִּישׁ **אֲרֵי מְרָחָק קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ הוּא:


Do not sacrifice to [**before**] Adonoy, your God, an ox or a lamb that has a blemish, any bad thing; for it is abominated [**distanced**] by [**before**] Adonoy, your God.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:2)


אֲרֵי יִשְׁתְּכַח בֵּינָךְ בַּחֲדָא מִקִּרְוָיךְ דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ גְּבַר אוֹ אִתְּתָא דִּי יַעְבֵּד יָת דְּבִישׁ **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְמֶעְבַּר עַל קְיָמֵיהּ:


If there is found among you, in one of your cities that Adonoy, your God, is giving you, a man or a woman who does what is evil in the eyes of Adonoy, your God, by violating His covenant.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:3)


וַאֲזַל וּפְלַח **לְטַעֲוַת עַמְמַיָּא** וּסְגִיד לְהוֹן וּלְשִׁמְשָׁא אוֹ לְסִיהֲרָא אוֹ לְכָל חֵילֵי שְׁמַיָּא דִּי לָא פַקֵּדִית:


He goes and serves other gods [**idols of the nations**] and bows to them—whether to the sun or to the moon or to any of the host of heaven that I did not command.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:4)


וִיחַוּוּן לָךְ וְתִשְׁמָע וְתִתְבַּע יָאוּת וְהָא קֻשְׁטָא כֵּוַן פִּתְגָּמָא אִתְעֲבִידַת תּוֹעֶבְתָּא הָדָא בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל:


When you are told and you understand, you are to inquire thoroughly. If in fact the report is authenticated and accurate—this abomination was committed within Yisroel.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 17:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:5)


וְתַפֵּק יָת גַּבְרָא הַהוּא אוֹ יָת אִתְּתָא הַהִיא דִּי עֲבָדוּ יָת פִּתְגָּמָא בִישָׁא הָדֵין **לִתְרַע בֵּית דִּינָךְ** יָת גַּבְרָא אוֹ יָת אִתְּתָא וְתִרְגְּמִנּוּן בְּאַבְנַיָּא וִימוּתוּן:


You are to take out that man or that woman who did this evil thing to your city [**the door of your court**]—the man or the woman—and you are to stone them with stones so that they die.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 17:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:6)


עַל מֵימַר תְּרֵין סַהֲדִין אוֹ תְּלָתָא סַהֲדִין יִתְקְטֵל **דְּחַיַּב קְטוֹל** לָא יִתְקְטֵל עַל מֵימַר סָהִיד חָד:


By the speech of two witnesses or three witnesses is the guilty one [**the one sentenced to death**] to be executed; he is not to be executed by the oral testimonoy of one witness.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 17:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:7)


יְדָא דְסַהֲדַיָּא תְּהֵי בֵיהּ בְּקַדְמֵיתָא לְמִקְטְלֵיהּ וִידָא דְכָל עַמָּא בְּבַתְרֵיתָא וּתְפַלֵּי עָבֵד **דְּבִישׁ** מִבֵּינָךְ:


Let the hand of the witnesses be against him first to execute him, and the hand of the entire people afterward; and you will eliminate the evil [**doer**] from within you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 17:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:8)


אֲרֵי יִתִּכְסֵי מִנָּךְ פִּתְגָּמָא לְדִינָא בֵּין דְּמָא לִדְמָא בֵּין דִּינָא לְדִינָא וּבֵין **מַכְתַּשׁ סְגִירוּ לְמַכְתַּשׁ סְגִירוּ** פִּתְגָּמֵי פְלֻגְתָּא **דִינָא** בְּקִרְוָיךְ וּתְקוּם וְתִסַּק לְאַתְרָא דִּי יִתִּרְעֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בֵּיהּ:


If a matter of law is too abstruse for you—between blood and blood, between decision and decision, or between leprosy [**a plague of confinement**] and leprosy [**a plague of confinement**], matters under [**legal**] dispute in your city; you shall rise and ascend to the place that Adonoy, your God, will have chosen.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 17:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:9)


וְתֵיתֵי לְוָת כָּהֲנַיָא לֵוָאֵי וּלְוָת דַּיָּנָא דִּי יְהֵי בְּיוֹמַיָּא הָאִנּוּן וְתִתְבַּע וִיחַוּוּן לָךְ יָת פִּתְגָּמָא דְדִינָא:


You are to come before the *kohanim*-the Levites and the judge officiating during those days; you will inquire and they will tell you the legal decision.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 17:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:10)


וְתַעְבֵּד עַל מֵימַר פִּתְגָּמָא דִּי יְחַוּוּן לָךְ מִן אַתְרָא הַהוּא דִּי יִתִּרְעֵי יְיָ וְתִטַּר לְמֶעְבַּד כְּכֹל דִּי **יַלְּפֻנָּךְ**:


You are to act according to the word that they tell you from that place that Adonoy will have chosen; and you are to be careful to fulfill exactly as they instruct you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 17:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:11)


עַל מֵימַר אוֹרַיְתָא דִּי **יַלְּפֻנָּךְ** וְעַל דִּינָא דִּי יֵימְרוּן לָךְ תַּעְבֵּד לָא תִסְטֵי מִן פִּתְגָּמָא דִּי יְחַוּוּן לָךְ יַמִּינָא וּשְׂמָאלָא:


In accord with the Torah that they instruct you and upon the law that they state to you, are you to act; do not deviate from the word they tell you, neither right or left.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 17:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:12)


וּגְבַר דִּי יַעְבֵּד בִּרְשַׁע בְּדִיל דְּלָא לְקַבָּלָא מִן כַּהֲנָא דְּקָאֵם לְשַׁמָּשָׁא תַמָּן **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ אוֹ מִן דַּיָּנָא וְיִתְקְטֵל גַּבְרָא הַהוּא וּתְפַלֵּי עָבֵד **דְּבִישׁ** מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל:


But the man who acts deliberately to not heed the *kohein* who stands to serve there [**before**] Adonoy, your God, or the judge; that man is to be executed and you will eliminate the evil [**doer**] from Yisroel.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 17:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:13)


וְכָל עַמָּא יִשְׁמְעוּן וְיִדְחֲלוּן וְלָא יַרְשִׁיעוּן עוֹד:


Let all the people hear and fear, and not sin deliberately again.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 17:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:14)


אֲרֵי תֵעוֹל לְאַרְעָא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ וְתֵירְתַהּ וְתֵיתֵב בַּהּ וְתֵימַר אֱמַנִּי עֲלַי מַלְכָּא כְּכָל עַמְמַיָּא דִי בְסַחֲרָנָי:


When you arrive in the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you and inherit it and live in it, and you say, ‘‘Let me appoint over me a king like all the nations around me;’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 17:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:15)


מַנָּאָה תְמַנֵּי עֲלָךְ מַלְכָּא דִּי יִתִּרְעֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בֵּיהּ מִגּוֹ אַחָיךְ תְּמַנֵּי עֲלָךְ מַלְכָּא **לֵית לָךְ רְשׁוּ** לְמַנָּאָה עֲלָךְ גְּבַר **נוּכְרַי** דִּי לָא אֲחוּךְ הוּא:


Appoint are you to appoint over yourself a king whom Adonoy, your God, will choose. From among your brothers are you to appoint over yourself a king; you may not [**you have no permission to**] place over yourself a foreigner who is not your brother.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 17:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:16)


לְחוֹד לָא יַסְגֵּי לֵיהּ סוּסָוָן וְלָא יָתֵב יָת עַמָּא לְמִצְרַיִם בְּדִיל לְאַסְגָּאָה **לֵיהּ** סוּסָוָן וַיְיָ אֲמַר לְכוֹן לָא תוֹסְפוּן לְמִתּוּב בְּאֹרַח הָדָא עוֹד:


However, he must not acquire an abundance of horses for himself so that he will not return the people to Egypt in order to acquire an abundance of horses, because Adonoy told you, ‘‘You are not to proceed to return along this route again.’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 17:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:17)


וְלָא יַסְגֵּי לֵיהּ נְשִׁין וְלָא יִטְעֵי לִבֵּיהּ וְכַסְפָּא וְדַהֲבָא לָא יַסְגֵּי לֵיהּ לַחֲדָא:


And he is not to acquire an abundance of wives for himself so that his heart will not veer; and silver and gold he may not accumulate for himself in great abundance.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 17:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:18)


וִיהֵי כְמִתְּבֵיהּ עַל כָּרְסָא מַלְכוּתֵיהּ וְיִכְתּוֹב לֵיהּ יָת **פַּתְשֶׁגֶן** אוֹרַיְתָא הָדָא עַל סִפְרָא מִן קֳדָם כָּהֲנַיָּא לֵוָאֵי:


It shall be, that when he occupies the throne of his kingdom, he must write for himself a duplicate of this Torah in a scroll form [the scroll] before the *kohanim*—the Levites.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 17:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:19)


וּתְהֵי עִמֵּיהּ וִיהֵי קָרֵי בֵיהּ כָּל יוֹמֵי חַיּוֹהִי בְּדִיל דְּיֵלַף לְמִדְחַל **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֶלָהֵיהּ לְמִטַּר יָת כָּל פִּתְגָּמֵי אוֹרַיְתָא הָדָא וְיָת קְיָמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין לְמֶעְבָּדְהוֹן:


It is to accompany him and he is to read in it all the days of his life, in order that he learn to fear [**before**] Adonoy, his God, to guard every word of this Torah and these statutes to fulfill them;


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 17:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/17:20)


בְּדִיל דְּלָא יְרִים לִבֵּיהּ מֵאֲחוֹהִי וּבְדִיל דְּלָא יִסְטֵי מִן תַּפְקֶדְתָּא יַמִּינָא וּשְׂמָאלָא בְּדִיל דְּיוֹרֵךְ יוֹמִין עַל מַלְכוּתֵיהּ הוּא וּבְנוֹהִי בְּגוֹ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


That his pride not increase over his brothers and he does not stray from the commandment right or left; so that the days of his reign are lengthy over his kingdom, he and his sons within Yisroel.



## Chapter 18

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:1)


לָא יְהֵי לְכָהֲנַיָּא לֵוָאֵי כָּל שִׁבְטָא דְלֵוִי חֳלָק וְאַחֲסָנָא עִם יִשְׂרָאֵל **קֻרְבָּנַיָּא** דַיְיָ וְאַחֲסַנְתֵּיהּ יֵיכְלוּן:


They will not have—the *kohanim,* the Levites, the entire tribe of Levi—a portion or an inheritance with Yisroel; Adonoy’s fire-offerings [**sacrifices**] and His inheritance will they eat.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:2)


וְאַחֲסָנָא לָא יְהֵי לֵיהּ בְּגוֹ אֲחוֹהִי **מַתְּנָן דִּיהַב לֵיהּ** יְיָ אִנּוּן אַחֲסַנְתֵּיהּ כְּמָא דִי מַלִּיל לֵיהּ:


But he will have no territory among his brothers; [**the gifts that**] Adonoy [**has given him**] is his territory, as He said to him.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:3)


וְדֵין יְהֵי דְחָזֵי לְכָהֲנַיָּא מִן עַמָּא מִן נָכְסֵי נִכְסָתָא אִם תּוֹר אִם אִמָּר וְיִתֵּן לְכַהֲנָא דְּרוֹעָא וְלוֹעָא וְקֻבְתָא:


And this will be the stipend of the *kohanim* from the people from the slaughterers of [permitted] meat, whether an ox or a sheep; he will give the *kohein* the foreleg and the jaw and the [fourth] stomach.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:4)


רֵישׁ עִבוּרָךְ חַמְרָךְ וּמִשְׁחָךְ וְרֵישׁ גִּזָּא דְעָנָךְ תִּתֶּן לֵיהּ:


The first portion of your grain, your wine, and your olive oil, and the first of the shearing of your sheep are you to give him.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 18:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:5)


אֲרֵי בֵיהּ יִתִּרְעֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ מִכָּל שִׁבְטָיךְ לְמֵקַם לְשַׁמָּשָׁא בִשְׁמָא דַיְיָ הוּא וּבְנוֹהִי כָּל יוֹמַיָּא:


For him did Adonoy, your God, choose from all your tribes to stand and perform the service in the name of Adonoy, he and his sons for all time.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 18:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:6)


וַאֲרֵי יֵיתֵי לֵוָאָה מֵחֲדָא מִקִּרְוָיךְ מִכָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל דִּי הוּא דָּר תַּמָּן וְיֵיתֵי בְּכָל רְעוּת נַפְשֵׁיהּ לְאַתְרָא דִּי יִתִּרְעֵי יְיָ:


If a Levite should come from one of your cities throughout Yisroel where he sojourns, coming will all his soul’s desire to the place that Adonoy chooses.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 18:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:7)


וִישַׁמַּשׁ בִּשְׁמָא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהֵיהּ כְּכָל אֲחוֹהִי לֵוָאֵי **דִּמְשַׁמְּשִׁין** תַּמָּן **(בִּצְלוֹ)** קֳדָם יְיָ:


He shall perform the service in the name of Adonoy, his God, like all his brother Levites who stand [**serve**] there [**(in prayer)**] in Adonoy’s Presence.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 18:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:8)


חֳלָק כָּחֳלָק יֵיכְלוּן בַּר **מִמַּטַּרְתָּא דְּיֵיתֵי בְשַׁבַּתָּא דְּכֵן אַתְקִינוּ אֲבָהָתָא**:


They shall eat equal portions [**portion for portions**], except for what the families sold [to one another] [**exept for the allotment which come on Shabbos, as our (fore)fathers have regulated**].


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 18:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:9)


אֲרֵי אַתְּ עָלֵל לְאַרְעָא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ לָא תֵילַף לְמֶעְבַּד כְּטַעֲוַת עַמְמַיָּא הָאִנּוּן:


Because you are entering the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you; do not learn to perpetrate the abominations of those nations.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 18:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:10)


לָא יִשְׁתְּכַח בָּךְ מַעֲבַר בְּרֵיהּ וּבְרַתֵּיהּ בְּנוּרָא קָסֵם קֻסְמִין מְעָנֵן וּמְנַחֵשׁ וְחָרָשׁ:


Let there not exist among you anyone who passes his son or daughter through fire, who practices the kosem-occult; who practices time-frame-occult or who divines portentuous events or a sorcerer.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 18:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:11)


וְרָטִין רְטָן וְשָׁאֵל בִּדִּין וּזְכוּרוּ וְתָבַע מִן מֵתַיָּא:


Or a snake charmer, or one who invokes the spirit of Ov or *yidoni,* or communicates with the dead.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 18:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:12)


אֲרֵי **מְרָחָק** יְיָ כָּל דְּיַעְבֵּד אִלֵּין וּבְדִיל תּוֹעֲבָתָא הָאִלֵּין יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ מְתָרַךְ יָתְהוֹן מִקֳּדָמָךְ:


For Adonoy’s abomination is [**distances**] anyone perpetrating this and because of these abominations, Adonoy, your God, is expelling them from before you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 18:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:13)


שְׁלִים תְּהֵי **בְּדַחַלְתָּא** דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ:


Walk in perfect trust with [**in the fear of**] Adonoy, your God.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 18:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:14)


אֲרֵי עַמְמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין דִּי אַתְּ יָרֵת יָתְהוֹן מִן מְעָנְנַיָּא וּמִן קַסָּמַיָּא שָׁמְעִין וְאַתְּ לָא כֵן יְהַב לָךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ:


Although these nations whom you are inheriting heed augurers and sorcerers, but as to you—Adonoy, your God, has not given you their status.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 18:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:15)


נְבִיָּא מִבֵּינָךְ מֵאַחָיךְ כְּוָתִי יְקִים לָךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ מִנֵּיהּ תְּקַבְּלוּן:


A prophet from your midst, of your brethren, like me, will Adonoy, your God, establish for you; heed him.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 18:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:16)


כְּכֹל דִּי שְׁאֶלְתָּא **מִקֳּדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בְּחֹרֵב בְּיוֹמָא דִקְהָלָא לְמֵימָר לָא אוֹסֵף לְמִשְׁמַע יָת קָל **מֵימְרָא** דַיְיָ אֱלָהַי וְיָת אֶשָּׁתָא רַבְּתָא הָדָא לָא אֶחֱזֵי עוֹד וְלָא אֵמוּת:


Exactly as you requested from [**before**] Adonoy, your God, at Choreiv, on the day of assembly, saying, ‘‘Let me not continue to hear the voice [**the word**] of Adonoy, my God, and this great fire let me not see any more as that I will not die.’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 18:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:17)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לִי אוֹטִיבוּ דִּי מַלִּילוּ:


Adonoy said to me, ‘‘What they said is excellent.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 18:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:18)


נְבִיָּא אֲקִים לְהוֹן מִגּוֹ אֲחֵיהוֹן כְּוָתָךְ וְאֶתֵּן פִּתְגָּמֵי **נְבוּאָתִי** בְּפוּמֵיהּ וִימַלֵּל עִמְּהוֹן יָת כָּל דִּי אֲפַקְּדִנֵּיהּ:


A prophet will I establish for them from among their brethren like you, and I will place My words [**of prophecy**] in his mouth and he will tell them everything that I command him.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 18:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:19)


וִיהֵי גַּבְרָא דִּי לָא יְקַבֵּל לְפִתְגָּמַי דִּי יְמַלֵּל בִּשְׁמִי **מֵימְרִי** יִתְבַּע מִנֵּיהּ:


Now, the man who does not heed My words that he speaks in My Name; I [**My word**] will demand from him.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 18:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:20)


בְּרַם נְבִיָּא דִּי יַרְשַׁע לְמַלָּלָא פִתְגָּמָא בִּשְׁמִי יָת דִּי לָא פַקֵּדִתֵּיהּ לְמַלָּלָא וְדִי יְמַלֵּל בְּשׁוּם **טַעֲוַת עַמְמַיָּא** וְיִתְקְטֵל נְבִיָּא הַהוּא:


But the prophet who will malevolently make a statement in My Name, something that I did not instruct him to say, or which he says in the name of foreign gods [**idols of the nations**], that prophet must die.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 18:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:21)


וַאֲרֵי תֵימַר בְּלִבָּךְ אֶכְדֵּין נִדַּע יָת פִּתְגָּמָא דִּי לָא מַלְּלֵיהּ יְיָ:


If you should say to yourself, ‘‘How can we know [which is] the statement that Adonoy did not speak?’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 18:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/18:22)


דִּי יְמַלֵּל נְבִיָּא בִּשְׁמָא דַיְיָ וְלָא יְהֵי פִתְגָּמָא וְלָא יִתְקַיַּם הוּא פִתְגָּמָא דִּי לָא מַלְּלֵיהּ יְיָ בִּרְשַׁע מַלְּלֵיהּ נְבִיָּא לָא תִדְחַל מִנֵּיהּ:


Should the prophet speak in Adonoy’s Name, and the matter does not happen and is not fulfilled, that is the statement that Adonoy did not speak. The prophet spoke it malevolently; do not fear him.



## Chapter 19

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:1)


אֲרֵי יְשֵׁצֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָת עַמְמַיָּא דִּי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ יָת אַרְעֲהוֹן וְתֵירְתִנּוּן וְתֵיתֵב בְּקִרְוֵיהוֹן וּבְבָתֵּיהוֹן:


When Adonoy, your God, annihilates the nations whose land Adonoy, your God is giving you; and you inherit them and live in their cities and in their houses;


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:2)


תְּלַת קִרְוִין תַּפְרַשׁ לָךְ בְּגוֹ אַרְעָךְ דִּי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ לְמֵירְתַהּ:


Separate three cities for yourself, within your land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you to inherit.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:3)


תְּתַקֵּן לָךְ אָרְחָא וּתְתַלַּת יָת תְּחוּם אַרְעָךְ דִּי יַחְסְנִנָּךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ וִיהֵי לְמֵעִירוֹק תַּמָּן כָּל קָטוֹלָא:


Ready the route for yourself and divide the borders of your land into three sections that Adonoy, your God, will allot you; this will serve for any murderer to flee there.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:4)


וְדֵין פִּתְגַּם קָטוֹלָא דְּיֵעִרוֹק תַּמָּן וְיִתְקַיַּם דִּי יִקְטוֹל יָת חַבְרֵיהּ בְּלָא מַנְדְּעֵיהּ וְהוּא לָא סָנֵי לֵיהּ מֵאִתְמַלֵּי וּמִדְּקָמוֹהִי:


And this is the matter of the murderer who may flee there to survive: whoever smites his peer without intent, and he had not been his enemy yesterday [or] the day before;


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 19:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:5)


וְדִי יֵעוּל עִם חַבְרֵיהּ בְּחֻרְשָׁא לְמִקַּץ אָעִין וְתִתְמְרֵג יְדֵיהּ בְּפַרְזְלָא לְמִקַּץ אָעִין וְיִשְׁתְּלֵף פַּרְזְלָא מִן אָעָא וְיַשְׁכַּח יָת חַבְרֵיהּ וִימוּת הוּא יֵעִרוֹק לַחֲדָא **מִן** קִרְוַיָּא הָאִלֵּין וְיִתְקַיַּם:


And whoever comes with his peer into the woods to chop trees, and as his hand swung the axe downward to cut the wood the iron flew off the wooden handle and encounters his peer and he dies; he is to flee to one of these cities to survive.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 19:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:6)


דִּילְמָא יִרְדּוֹף גָּאֵל דְּמָא בָּתַר קָטוֹלָא אֲרֵי יֵחַם לִבֵּיהּ וְיַדְבְּקִנֵּיהּ אֲרֵי תִסְגֵּי אָרְחָא וְיִקְטְלִנֵּיהּ נְפַשׁ וְלֵיהּ לֵית חוֹבַת **דִּין דִּקְטוֹל** אֲרֵי לָא סָנֵי הוּא לֵיהּ מִתְּמַלִּי וּמִדְּקָמוֹהִי:


Lest the blood-redeemer pursue the murderer when his heart grows heated, and he catches up with him over the length of the road and he smite him dead when he has no [**judgment of a**] death sentence because he had not been his enemy yesterday [or] the day before.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 19:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:7)


עַל כֵּן אֲנָא מְפַקְּדָךְ לְמֵימָר תְּלַת קִרְוִין תַּפְרֵשׁ לָךְ:


Therefore am I commanding you the following: three cities shall you separate for yourself.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 19:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:8)


וְאִם יַפְתֵּי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָת תְּחוּמָךְ כְּמָא דִי קַיִּים לַאֲבָהָתָךְ וְיִתֶּן לָךְ יָת כָּל אַרְעָא דִּי מַלִּיל לְמִתַּן לַאֲבָהָתָךְ:


And when Adonoy, your God, expands your boundary, in accordance with His oath to your forefathers, and He give you the entire land that He promised to give to your forefathers,


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 19:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:9)


אֲרֵי תִטַּר יָת כָּל תַּפְקֶדְתָּא הָדָא לְמֶעְבְּדַהּ דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקְּדָךְ יוֹמָא דֵין לְמִרְחַם יָת יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ וְלִמְהַךְ בְּאָרְחָן **דְּתַקְּנָן קֳדָמוֹהִי** כָּל יוֹמַיָּא וְתוֹסֵף לָךְ עוֹד תְּלַת קִרְוִין עַל תְּלַת אִלֵּין:


When you will be guarding this entire *mitzvah* to fulfill it, that I am commanding you today, to love Adonoy, your God, and to go in His ways [**that are proper before Him**] for all time; then you shall add three more cities to these three.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 19:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:10)


וְלָא יִשְׁתְּפֵךְ דָּם זַכַּי בְּגוֹ אַרְעָךְ דִּי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ אַחֲסָנָא וִיהֵי עֲלָךְ **חוֹבַת דִּין דִּקְטוֹל**:


And let innocent blood not be shed within your land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you as territory, [otherwise] you will bear liability for the blood [**the judgment of a death sentence**].


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 19:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:11)


וַאֲרֵי יְהֵי גְבַר סָנֵי לְחַבְרֵיהּ וְיִכְמַן לֵיהּ וִיקוּם עֲלוֹהִי וְיִמְחִנֵּיהּ נְפַשׁ וִימוּת וְיֵעִרוֹק לַחֲדָא מִן קִרְוַיָּא הָאִלֵּין:


If there is a man who hates his neighbor, and will ambush him, arising against him and smiting him dead, and he will flee to one of these cities;


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 19:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:12)


וִישַׁלְּחוּן סָבֵי קַרְתֵּיהּ וְיִדְבְּרוּן יָתֵיהּ מִתַּמָּן וְיִתְּנוּן יָתֵיהּ בְּיַד גָּאֵל דְּמָא וִימוּת:


The elders of his city will send for and take him from there, and will hand him over to the blood-redeemer, and he will be executed.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 19:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:13)


לָא תְחוּס עֵינָךְ עֲלוֹהִי וּתְפַלֵּי אַשְׁדִּי דָם זַכַּי מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל וְיֵיטַב לָךְ:


Do not view him with compassion. You are to eliminate the [shedding of] innocent blood from Yisroel, and you will have it good.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 19:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:14)


לָא תַשְׁנֵי תְּחוּמָא דְחַבְרָךְ דִּי **תְחִימוּ** קַדְמָאֵי בְּאַחֲסַנְתָּךְ דִּי תַחְסֵן בְּאַרְעָא דִּי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ לְמֵירְתַהּ:


Do not move back the boundary of your neighbor that the first [settlers] determine [**bounded**] in your territory that you will inherit in the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you to inherit.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 19:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:15)


לָא יְקוּם סָהִיד חַד בִּגְבַר לְכָל עַוָּיָן וּלְכָל חוֹבִין בְּכָל חֵט דִּי יֶחְטָא עַל מֵימַר תְּרֵין סָהֲדִין אוֹ עַל מֵימַר תְּלָתָא סָהֲדִין יִתְקַיַּם פִּתְגָּמָא:


One witness may not arise against a man about any sin or for any transgression or transgression that he transgresses; by the word of two witnesses or by the word of three witnesses let a matter be established.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 19:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:16)


אֲרֵי יְקוּם סָהִיד שְׁקַר בִּגְבָר **לְאַסְהָדָא** בֵיהּ סַטְיָא:


If false witnesses arise against a man and bear fallacious testimony [**with perversity**] against him;


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 19:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:17)


וִיקוּמוּן תְּרֵין גֻּבְרִין דִּי לְהוֹן דִּינָא קֳדָם יְיָ קֳדָם כָּהֲנַיָּא וְדַיָּנַיָּא דִּי יְהוֹן בְּיוֹמַיָּא הָאִנּוּן:


The two men shall stand, who are involved in the dispute, before Adonoy, before the *kohanim* and the judges who are in those days.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 19:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:18)


וְיִתְבְּעוּן דַּיָּנַיָּא יָאוּת וְהָא סָהִיד שְׁקַר סָהֲדָא שִׁקְרָא אַסְהַד בַּאֲחוֹהִי:


When the judges shall investigate thoroughly and behold, the witness testified falsely, they testified falsely against their brother;


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 19:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:19)


וְתַעְבְּדוּן לֵיהּ כְּמָא דִי חַשִּׁיב לְמֶעְבַּד לַאֲחוֹהִי וּתְפַלֵּי **עָבֵד** דְּבִישׁ מִבֵּינָךְ:


You are to do to [each of] them as he conspired to do to his brother, and eliminate the evil [**doers**] from among you;


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 19:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:20)


וּדְיִשְׁתָּאֲרוּן יִשְׁמְעוּן וְיִדְחֲלוּן וְלָא יוֹסְפוּן לְמֶעְבַּד עוֹד כְּפִתְגָּמָא בִישָׁא הָדֵין בֵּינָךְ:


And the remainder will hear and be fearful, and they will not repeat this evil thing among you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 19:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/19:21)


וְלָא תְחוּס עֵינָךְ נַפְשָׁא חֳלַף נַפְשָׁא עֵינָא חֳלַף עֵינָא שִׁנָּא חֳלַף שִׁנָּא יְדָא חֳלַף יְדָא רַגְלָא חֳלַף רַגְלָא:


You are not to have compassion: life for life, eye for eye, tooth for tooth, hand for hand, foot for foot.



## Chapter 20

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/20:1)


אֲרֵי תִפּוֹק **לַאֲגָחָא** **קְרָבָא** עַל בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבָךְ וְתֶחֱזֵי סוּסָוָן וּרְתִכִּין עַם סַגִּי מִנָּךְ לָא תִדְחַל מִנְּהוֹן אֲרֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ **מֵימְרֵיהּ בְּסַעְדָּךְ** דְּאַסְּקָךְ מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם:


When you go to [**wage**] war against your enemy, and you see horse and chariot, people who outnumber you; do not be afraid of them, for [**the word of**] Adonoy, your God, is with you [**will aid you**], He Who brought you up from the land of Egypt.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 20:2

Now, as you [**come**] near the battle [**to wage war**], the *kohein* shall approach and speak to the people.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/20:3)


וְיֵימַר לְהוֹן שְׁמַע יִשְׂרָאֵל אַתּוּן קְרִיבִין יוֹמָא דֵין **לַאֲגָחָא קְרָבָא** עַל בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבֵיכוֹן לָא יְזוּעַ לִבְּכוֹן לָא תִדְחֲלוּן וְלָא תְתְבַּהֲתוּן וְלָא **תִתָּבְרוּן** מִקֳּדָמֵיהוֹן:


He will say to them, ‘‘Hear, Yisroel! You are setting out today to battle [**to wage war**] against your enemies. Do not be faint hearted; or intimidated and do not panic, and do not be crushed [**broken**] before them;

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/20:4)

Because Adonoy, your God, marches with you [**before you**] to do battle [**wage war**] for you with your enemies to save you.’’

The officers will address the people as follows, ‘‘Whichever man has built a new house and did not inaugurate it as a dwelling, let him go and return home lest he die in battle [**while waging war**] and another man will inaugurate it.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/20:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/20:6)

And whichever man has planted a vineyard and did not redeem it[s fruit,] let him go and return home, lest he die in battle [**while waging war**] and another man redeem it.

When you near a city to do battle against it, you are to offer it [**words of**] peace.

In order that they do not teach you to do any of their abominations that they did for their gods [**idols**], and you will sin to [**before**] Adonoy, your God.

אֲרֵי תְצוּר לְקַרְתָּא יוֹמִין סַגִּיאִין לַאֲגָחָא עֲלַהּ לְמִכְבְּשַׁהּ לָא תְחַבַּל יָת אִילָנַהּ לַאֲרָמָא עֲלוֹהִי גַּרְזְנָא אֲרֵי מִנֵּיהּ תֵּיכוּל וְיָתֵיהּ לָא תְקוּץ אֲרֵי **לָא** כַאֲנָשָׁא אִילַן חַקְלָא לְמֵיעַל מִקֳּדָמָךְ בִּצְיָרָא:


If you besiege a city many days to wage war against it, to capture it, do not harm [any of] its trees by chopping it with an ax, because you eat from it you are not to cut it down; For, is [**not**] the tree in the field a man to join the besieged to escape you?
