[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/20:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/20:20)


לְחוֹד אִילָן דְּתִדַּע אֲרֵי לָא אִילַן דְּמַאֲכָל הוּא יָתֵיהּ תְּחַבַּל וּתְקוּץ וְתִבְנֵי **כַרְקוֹמִין** עַל קַרְתָּא דִּי הִיא עָבְדָא עִמָּךְ קְרָבָא עַד דְּתִכְבְּשַׁהּ:


Only a tree that you know that it is not a fruit tree may you harm or cut down; and you will build battlements [**fortifications**] against the city that is waging war against you until it is conquered.



## Chapter 21

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/21:1)


אֲרֵי יִשְׁתְּכַח קְטִילָא בְּאַרְעָא דִּי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ לְמֵירְתַהּ רָמֵי בְּחַקְלָא לָא אִתְיְדַע מַן קָטְלֵיהּ:


If a corpse is found in the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you to inherit, fallen in the field, it is not known who smote him.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/21:2)


וְיִפְּקוּן סָבָיךְ וְדַיָּנָיךְ וְיִמְשְׁחוּן לְקִרְוַיָּא דִּי סַחֲרָנוּת קְטִילָא:


Your elders shall go out—and your judges—and measure in the direction of the cities around the corpse.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/21:3)


וּתְהֵי קַרְתָּא דִּקָרִיבָא לִקְטִילָא וְיִדְבְּרוּן סָבֵי קַרְתָּא הַהִיא עֶגְלַת תּוֹרֵי דִּי לָא אִתְפְּלַח בַּהּ דִּי לָא נְגִידַת בְּנִיר:


Now, the city nearest the corpse—the elders of that city are to take a calf-heifer that has not been worked, that has not drawn [with] a yoke.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/21:4)


וְיֶחֱתוּן סָבֵי קַרְתָּא הַהִיא יָת עֲגַלְתָּא לְנַחֲלָא בַיָּר דִּי לָא אִתְפְּלַח בֵּיהּ וְלָא יִזְדְּרַע וְיִנְקְפוּן תַּמָּן יָת עֲגַלְתָּא בְּנַחֲלָא:


The elders of that city will take down the calf to a stony valley that is not to be tilled and not to be seeded, and they shall decapitate the calf in that valley.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 21:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/21:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/21:5)


וְיִתְקָרְבוּן כָּהֲנַיָּא בְּנֵי לֵוִי אֲרֵי בְהוֹן יִתִּרְעֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְשַׁמָּשׁוּתֵיהּ וּלְבָרָכָא בִּשְׁמָא דַיְיָ וְעַל מֵימְרֵהוֹן יְהֵי כָּל **דִּין** וְכָל מַכְתַּשׁ סְגִירוּ:


The *kohanim,* descendants of Levi, will approach; because them did Adonoy, your God, choose to serve Him and to bless in Adonoy’s Name; and they will decide every dispute [**law**] and every *nega.*


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 21:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/21:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/21:6)


וְכֹל סָבֵי קַרְתָּא הַהִיא דִּקְרִיבִין **לִקְטִילָא** יַסְחוּן יָת יְדֵיהוֹן עַל עֲגַלְתָּא דִּנְקֶפְתָּא בְנַחֲלָא:


And all the elders of that city, those near the corpse, will wash their hands over the calf beheaded in that valley.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 21:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/21:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/21:7)


**וִיתִיבוּן** וְיֵימְרוּן יְדָנָא לָא אַשְׁדּוֹ יָת דְּמָא הָדֵין וְעֵינָנָא לָא חֲזָאָה:


They will [**answer**] loudly declare, ‘‘Our hands have not spilled this blood and our eyes did not see.’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 21:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/21:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/21:8)


כָּהֲנַיָּא יֵימְרוּן כַּפַּר לְעַמָּךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל דִּי פְרַקְתָּא יְיָ וְלָא תִתֵּן **חוֹבַת** דַּם זַכַּי בְּגוֹ עַמָּךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל וְיִתְכַּפַּר לְהוֹן עַל דְּמָא:


[The *kohanim* will say,] ‘‘Forgive Your people Yisroel, whom You, Adonoy, have redeemed; and do not allow [**the responsibility of**] innocent blood [liability] within Your people Yisroel.’’ The blood shall thus be atoned for in their behalf.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 21:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:12)


וִיהֵי לְמִפְנֵי רַמְשָׁא יַסְחֵי בְמַיָּא וּכְמֵעַל שִׁמְשָׁא יֵעוּל לְגוֹ מַשְׁרִיתָא:


As sunset nears he is to immerse in water; and after the sun has set he may enter the the midst of the camp.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 23:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:13)


וַאֲתַר **מְתַקָּן** יְהֵי לָךְ מִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא וְתִפּוֹק תַּמָּן לְבָרָא:


Have a designated [**prepared**] place outside the camp, and you will go out there to excrete.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 23:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:14)


וְסִכְּתָא תְּהֵי לָךְ עַל זֵינָךְ וִיהֵי בְּמִתְבָךְ לְבָרָא וְתַחְפַּר בַּהּ וּתְתוּב וּתְכַסֵּי יָת מִפְּקָתָךְ:


And a peg are you to have in addition to your weapons; when you need to sit to excrete dig with it, then use it again to cover your excrement.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 23:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:15)


אֲרֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ **שְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ** מְהַלְּכָא בְּגוֹ מַשְׁרִיתָךְ לְשֵׁזָבוּתָךְ וּלְמִמְסַר בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבָךְ קֳדָמָךְ וּתְהֵי מַשְׁרִיתָךְ קַדִּישָׁא וְלָא יִתְחֲזֵי בָךְ **עֲבֵרַת פִּתְגָּם** וִיתוּב **מֵימְרֵיהּ מִלְּאוֹטָבָא לָךְ**:


For Adonoy, your God, [**His Shechinah**] is moving within your camp to save you and to defeat your enemies before you, so your camp must be holy; let Him not see within you disgraceful [**offensive**] objects lest He [**His word**] turn away from you [**from benefiting you**].


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 23:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:16)


לָא **תִמְסַר** עֶבֶד **עַמְמִין לְיַד** רִבּוֹנֵיהּ דְּיִשְׁתֵּזֵב לְוָתָךְ מִלְּוָת רִבּוֹנֵיהּ:


Do not deliver a [**foreign**] slave to [**the hand of**] his master, who escaped to you from his master.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 23:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:17)


עִמָּךְ יִתֵּב בֵּינָךְ בְּאַתְרָא דִּי יִתִּרְעֵי בַּחֲדָא מִן **קִרְוָיךְ** בִּדְיֵיטַב לֵיהּ לָא תוֹנִנֵּיהּ:


With you will he live among you, in the place that he chooses in any of your cities, wherever it is good for him; do not distress him.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 23:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:18)


לָא תְהֵי **אִתְּתָא** מִבְּנַת יִשְׂרָאֵל **לִגְבַר עָבֵד** וְלָא יִסַּב **גַּבְרָא** מִבְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל **אִתְּתָא אָמָא**:


There is not to be a [**woman**] harlot from the daughters of Yisroel [**to a man slave**]; and there shall not be a [**man**] male prostitute from the sons of Yisroel [**to a woman servant**].


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 23:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:19)


לָא תָעֵל אֲגַר זַנִּיתָא וְחוּלְפַן כַּלְבָּא לְבֵית מַקְדְּשָׁא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְכָל נִדְרָא אֲרֵי **מְרָחָק קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ אַף תַּרְוֵיהוֹן:


Do not bring a harlot’s fee or payment for a dog to the house of Adonoy, your God, in fulfillment of any vow; because [**distanced from**] Adonoy, your God’s abominations are even both of them.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 23:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:20)


לָא תְרַבֵּי לַאֲחוּךְ רִבִּית כְּסַף רִבִּית עִבוּר רִבִּית כָּל מִדַּעַם דְּמִתְרַבֵּי:


You may not pay usury to your brother, usury on money, usury on food; usury on any matter where it is paid.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 23:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:21)


**לְבַר עַמְמִין** תְּרַבֵּי וְלַאֲחוּךְ לָא תְרַבֵּי בְּדִיל דִּיבָרֵכִנָּךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בְּכֹל אוֹשָׁטוּת יְדָךְ עַל אַרְעָא דִּי אַתְּ עָלֵל לְתַמָּן לְמֵירְתַהּ:


You may pay usury to the stranger [**foreigner**], but to your brother do not pay usury; in order that Adonoy, your God, will bless you in all your commerce on the land that you are coming there to inherit.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 23:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:22)


אֲרֵי תִדַּר נְדַר **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לָא תְאַחַר לְשַׁלָּמוּתֵיהּ אֲרֵי מִתְבַּע יִתְבְּעִנֵּיהּ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ מִנָּךְ וִיהֵי בָךְ חוֹבָא:


If you make a vow to [**before**] Adonoy, your God, do not delay in discharging it; for Adonoy, your God, will surely demand it from you, and you will be at fault.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 23:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:23)


וַאֲרֵי תִתִּמְנַע לְמִנְדַּר לָא יְהֵי בָךְ חוֹבָא:


But if you desist from vowing, no fault will be found with you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 23:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:24)


אַפָּקוּת סִפְוָתָךְ תִּטַּר וְתַעְבֵּד כְּמָא דִי נְדַרְתָּא **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ נְדַבְתָּא דִּי מַלֶּלְתָּא בְּפוּמָךְ:


Keep what you have articulated; and perform whatever you vow; to [**before**] Adonoy, your God, voluntarily—whatever you say with your mouth.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 23:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:25)


אֲרֵי **תִתָּגַר** בְּכַרְמָא דְחַבְרָךְ וְתֵיכוּל עִנְּבִין כְּנַפְשָׁךְ שִׂבְעָךְ וּלְמָאנָךְ לָא תִתֵּן:


When you [**are hired to**] enter your fellow’s vineyard, you may eat grapes as you desire, to your satisfaction; but you may not place it in your container.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 23:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/23:26)


אֲרֵי **תִתָּגַר** בְּקַמְתָא דְחַבְרָךְ וְתִקְטוֹף מְלִילָן בִּידָךְ וּמַגְלָא לָא תְרִים עַל קַמְתָא דְחַבְרָךְ:


When you [**are hired to**] enter your fellow’s standing grainstalks, you may cut off stalks by hand, but do not lift a sickle over your fellow’s standing grainstalks.



## Chapter 24

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:1)


אֲרֵי יִסַּב גְּבַר אִתְּתָא וְיִבְעֲלִנַּהּ וִיהֵי אִם לָא תַשְׁכַּח רַחֲמִין בְּעֵינוֹהִי אֲרֵי אַשְׁכַּח בַּהּ **עֲבֵרַת** **פִּתְגָּם** וְיִכְתּוֹב לַהּ גֵּט פִּטּוּרִין וִיהַב בִּידַהּ וְיִפְטְרִנַּהּ מִבֵּיתֵיהּ:


If a man marries a woman and consummates with her; should she not find favor in his eyes because he found in her something lecherous [**sinful**], he is to write for her a document of severance and place [it] in her hand and send her from his home.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:2)


וְתִפּוֹק מִבֵּיתֵיהּ וּתְהַךְ וּתְהֵי לִגְבַר אָחֳרָן:


If she leaves his home and goes and becomes [wife] to another man.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:3)


וְיִסְנִנַּהּ גַּבְרָא בַתְרָאָה וְיִכְתּוֹב לַהּ גֵּט פִּטּוּרִין וְיִתֵּן בִּידַהּ וְיִפְטְרִנַּהּ מִבֵּיתֵיהּ אוֹ אֲרֵי יְמוּת גַּבְרָא בַתְרָאָה דְּנָסְבַהּ לֵיהּ לְאִנְתּוּ:


If her last husband hates her and writes for her a document of severance and places [it] in her hand and sends her from his house, or if the latter man dies, who had taken her as his wife;


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 24:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:4)


לֵית **לֵיהּ רְשׁוּ** לְבַעֲלַהּ קַדְמָאָה דִי פָטְרַהּ לְמִתֻּב לְמִסְּבַהּ לְמֶהֱוֵי לֵיהּ לְאִנְתּוּ בָּתַר דִּי אִסְתָּאָבַת אֲרֵי מְרָחָקָא הִיא קֳדָם יְיָ וְלָא תְחַיַּב יָת אַרְעָא דִּי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ אַחֲסָנָא:


Her first husband who divorced her may not [**has no permission to**] remarry her to be his wife after her having been defiled, for it is an abomination before Adonoy; and you are not to make liable the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you as territory.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 24:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:5)


אֲרֵי יִסַּב גְּבַר אִתְּתָא חַדְתָּא לָא יִפּוֹק בְּחֵילָא וְלָא יֵעִבַר עֲלוֹהִי לְכָל מִדָּעַם **פָּנֵי** יְהֵי לְבֵיתֵיהּ שַׁתָּא חֲדָא וְיַחְדֵּי יָת אִתְּתֵיהּ דִּי נְסַב:


When a man marries a new wife, he is not to be mobilized into the army, it shall not impose itself over him in any matter; he shall be free for his home one year and he shall bring joy to the wife he married.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 24:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:6)


לָא יִסַּב מַשְׁכּוֹנָא רִחְיָא וְרִכְבָּא **אֲרֵי בְהוֹן מִתְעֲבֵד מָזוֹן לְכָל נְפַשׁ**:


He may not secure as collateral a mortar or pestle, for his is taking life as security [**for sustinance is made with these for all souls**].


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 24:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:7)


אֲרֵי יִשְׁתְּכַח גְּבַר גָּנֵב נַפְשָׁא מֵאֲחוֹהִי מִבְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְיִתָּגַר בֵּיהּ וִיזַבְּנִנֵּיהּ וְיִתְקְטֵל גַּנָּבָא הַהוּא וּתְפַלֵּי **עָבֵד** **דְּבִישׁ** מִבֵּינָךְ:


If a man is apprehended having kidnaped a person from his brothers, from the descendants of Yisroel, and he exploited him and sold him; that kidnaper is to be put to death and you will eliminate the evil [**doer**] within you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 24:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:8)


אִסְתַּמַּר בְּמַכְתַּשׁ סְגִירוּ לְמִטַּר לַחֲדָא וּלְמֶעְבָּד כְּכֹל דִּי יַלְּפוּן יָתְכוֹן כָּהֲנַיָּא לֵוָאֵי כְּמָא דִי פַקֶּדְתִּנּוּן תִּטְּרוּן לְמֶעְבָּד:


Take precautions with the leprosy affliction to be extremely careful and to do—in accordance with everything that the *kohanim* the Levites shall instruct you; as I commanded them are you to make sure to do.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 24:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:9)


הֲוֵי דְכִיר יָת דִּי עֲבַד יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְמִרְיָם בְּאָרְחָא בְּמִפָּקְכוֹן מִמִּצְרָיִם:


Remember what Adonoy, your God, did to Miriam on the way when you were going out of Egypt.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 24:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:10)


אֲרֵי תַרְשֵׁי בְחַבְרָךְ רְשׁוּ מִדָּעַם לָא תֵעוּל לְבֵיתֵיהּ לְמִסַּב **מַשְׁכּוֹנֵיהּ**:


If you become your fellow’s creditor for any amount of debt, do not enter his house to take his pledge [**collateral**].


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 24:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:11)


בְּבָרָא תְּקוּם וְגַבְרָא דִּי אַתְּ רָשֵׁי בֵיהּ יַפֵּק לְוָתָךְ יָת **מַשְׁכּוֹנָא** לְבָרָא:


Stand outside, and the man from whom you are claiming, will bring out the pledge [**collateral**] to you outside.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 24:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:12)


וְאִם גְּבַר מִסְכֵּן הוּא לָא תִשְׁכּוּב **בְּמַשְׁכּוֹנֵיהּ**:


And if he is a poor man, do not repose with his collateral [in your possession].


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 24:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:13)


אָתָבָא תָתֵב לֵיהּ יָת **מַשְׁכּוֹנָא** כְּמֵעַל שִׁמְשָׁא וְיִשְׁכּוּב בִּכְסוּתֵיהּ וִיבָרֵכִנָּךְ וְלָךְ תְּהֵי **זְכוּתָא** קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ:


Return are you to return the pledge [**collateral**] to him at sunset, and he will sleep with his garment and bless you; and it will be to your credit [**merit**] before Adonoy, your God.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 24:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:14)


לָא תְעִשּׁוּק אֲגִירָא עַנְיָא וּמִסְכֵּנָא מֵאַחָיךְ אוֹ מִגִּיּוֹרָךְ דִּי בְאַרְעָךְ בְּקִרְוָיךְ:


Do not steal from the wage earner who is poor or destitute; from your brothers or your converts who are in your land in your cities.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 24:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:15)


בְּיוֹמֵיהּ תִּתֵּן אַגְרֵיהּ וְלָא תֵעוּל עֲלוֹהִי שִׁמְשָׁא אֲרֵי עַנְיָא הוּא וְלֵיהּ הוּא **מְסַר** יָת נַפְשֵׁיהּ וְלָא יִקְרֵי עֲלָךְ **קֳדָם** יְיָ וִיהֵי בָךְ חוֹבָא:


On its day give his wage and let the sun not set upon it, for he is a poor man, and he places his life in jeopardy for it; [so that] he should not complain about you to [**before**] Adonoy, and you will have a sin.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 24:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:16)


לָא יְמוּתוּן אֲבָהָן עַל **פּוּם** בְּנִין וּבְנִין לָא יְמוּתוּן עַל **פּוּם** אֲבָהָן אֱנַשׁ בְּחוֹבֵיהּ יְמוּתוּן:


Fathers shall not be executed through [**by the mouths of**] sons, and sons shall not be executed through [**by the mouths of**] fathers; each to be executed for his sin.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 24:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:17)


לָא **תַצְלֵי** דִין גִּיּוֹרָא וְיִתַּמָּא וְלָא תִסַּב **מַשְׁכּוֹנָא** לְבוּשׁ אַרְמְלָא:


Do not be devious in [**forcibly wrench**] the judgment of the proselyte or orphan; and you may not secure as collateral, a widow’s garment.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 24:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:18)


וְתִדְכַּר אֲרֵי עַבְדָּא הֲוֵיתָא בְּמִצְרַיִם וּפָרְקָךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ מִתַּמָּן עַל כֵּן אֲנָא מְפַקְּדָךְ לְמֶעְבַּד יָת פִּתְגָּמָא הָדֵין:


Remember that you were a slave in Egypt, and Adonoy, your God, redeemed you from there; that is why I am commanding you to do this thing.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 24:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:19)


אֲרֵי תַחְצוֹד חֲצָדָךְ בְּחַקְלָךְ וְתִנְשֵׁי עֻמְרָא בְחַקְלָא לָא תְתוּב לְמִסְּבֵיהּ לְגִיּוֹרָא לְיִתַּמָּא וּלְאַרְמְלָא יְהֵי בְּדִיל דִּיבָרֵכִנָּךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בְּכֹל עוֹבָדֵי יְדָךְ:


When you reap your reaping in your field, and you forget a sheaf in the field, you may not return to take it; for the proselyte, for the orphan, and for the widow let it be; in order that Adonoy, your God, will bless you in all your endeavors.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 24:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:20)


אֲרֵי תַחְבּוֹט זֵיתָךְ לָא תְפַלֵּי בַּתְרָךְ לְגִיּוֹרָא לְיִתַּמָּא וּלְאַרְמְלָא יְהֵי:


When you harvest your olive tree, you may not strip it of its glory behind you. for the proselyte, for the orphan, and for the widow let it be.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:21)


אֲרֵי תִקְטוֹף כַּרְמָךְ לָא תְעָלֵל בַּתְרָךְ לְגִיּוֹרָא לְיִתַּמָּא וּלְאַרְמְלָא יְהֵי:


When you harvest your vineyard you may not harvest pygmy vines behind you; for the proselyte, for the orphan, and for the widow let it be.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/24:22)

Remember that you were a slave in the land of Egypt; that is why I am commanding you to do this thing.

If a quarrel [**lawsuit**] should occur among men and they bring it to court and they judge them and they determine who is righteous and they convict the villain.

Should the wicked one deserve flogging, the judge shall incline him and have him flogged before him in the amount befitting his crime, with the number near.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:3)


אַרְבְּעִין יַלְקִנֵּיהּ לָא יוֹסֵף דִּילְמָא יוֹסֵף לְאַלְקָיוּתֵיהּ עַל אִלֵּין מָחָא רַבָּא וְיֵקַל אֲחוּךְ לְעֵינָיךְ:


Forty is he to have him flogged, he may not add; lest he additionally flog him over these, a great flogging, when your brother will be slighted before you.

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 25:5

אֲרֵי יֵתְבוּן אַחִין כַּחֲדָא וִימוּת חַד מִנְּהוֹן וּבַר לֵית לֵיהּ לָא תְהֵי אִתַּת מֵתָנָא לְבָרָא לִגְבַר אוֹחֲרָן (נ"א חִלּוֹנָי) יְבָמַהּ יֵעוּל עֲלַהּ וְיִסְּבַהּ לֵיהּ לְאִנְתּוּ וְיִבְּמַהּ:


If brothers reside together, and one of them dies having no son, let the wife of the dead man not marry outside [the family] to a strange man; her brother-in-law will consummate with her thus marrying her to be his wife, and perform levirate marriage with her.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 25:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:6)

It shall be that the firstborn, when she is capable of bearing children, shall be established in place of his deceased brother, so that his name may not be obliterated from Yisroel.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 25:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:7)

But if the man will not want to marry his sister-in-law; his sister-in-law must go up to the portal [**of the court**], to [**before**] the judges, and say, ‘‘My brother-in-law refuses [**does not agree**] to establish for his brother a name in Yisroel; he is unwilling to do perform levirate marriage with me.’’

וְיִקְרוּן לֵיהּ סָבֵי קַרְתֵּיהּ וִימַלְּלוּן עִמֵּיהּ וִיקוּם וְיֵימַר לָא רְעֵנָא לְמִסְבַּהּ:


The judges of his city will call him and converse with him. He shall stand and say, ‘‘I do not want to marry her.’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 25:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:9)


וְתִתְקְרַב יְבִמְתֵּיהּ לְוָתֵיהּ לָקֳדָם סָבַיָּא וְתַשְׁרֵי סֵינֵיהּ מֵעַל רַגְלֵיהּ וְתֵרוֹק בְּאַנְפּוֹהִי וְתָתֵב וְתֵימַר כְּדֵין יִתְעֲבֵד לִגְבַר דִּי לָא יִבְנֵי יָת בֵּיתָא דַאֲחוּהִי:


And his sister-in-law will approach him in the sight of the judges, and she will remove his shoe from upon his foot, and spit before him; and she will say aloud, ‘‘This is done to the man who will not build his brother’s family.’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 25:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:10)

And it will be entitled in Yisroel, the house of the divestiture of the shoe.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 25:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:11)


אֲרֵי יִנְצוּן גֻּבְרִין כַּחֲדָא גְּבַר וַאֲחוּהִי וְתִתְקְרֵב אִתַּת חַד לְשֵׁזָבָא יָת בַּעֲלַהּ מִיַּד מָחוּהִי וְתוֹשֵׁט יְדַהּ וְתִתְקֵף **בְּבֵית בַּהַתְּתֵיהּ**:


If men engage in an altercation—a man and his brother—and the wife of one approaches to save her husband from his assailant, and she puts out her hand and grasps his genitals [**the place of his shame**],

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 25:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:13)


לָא יְהֵי לָךְ בְּכִיסָךְ מַתְקַל וּמַתְקָל רַבְּתָא וּזְעֶרְתָּא:


You are not to have for yourself in your pouch varying weight-stones, large and small.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 25:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:14)

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 25:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:15)


**מַתְקְלִין** שָׁלְמִין דִּקְשׁוֹט יְהוֹן לָךְ **מְכִילָן** שָׁלְמִין דִּקְשׁוֹט יְהוֹן לָךְ בְּדִיל דְּיוֹרְכוּן יוֹמָיךְ עַל אַרְעָא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ:


A fully accurate, just weight, you shall have, you are to have whole and honest measures; in order that you live long on the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 25:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:16)


אֲרֵי **מְרָחָק קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ כָּל עָבֵד אִלֵּין כֹּל עָבֵד **שְׁקַר**:


Because [**distanced from before**] Adonoy, your God’s abomination, are all who do these [things]; all who do falsehood.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 25:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:17)


הֲוֵי דְכִיר יָת דִּי עֲבַד לָךְ עֲמָלֵק בְּאָרְחָא בְּמִפָּקְכוֹן מִמִּצְרָיִם:


Remember what Amalek perpetrated against you on the way when you were going out of Egypt.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 25:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:18)


דִּי **עָרְעָךְ** בְּאָרְחָא **וְקָטֵל** בָּךְ כָּל דַּהֲווֹ **מִתְאַחֲרִין** בַּתְרָךְ וְאַתְּ מְשַׁלְהֵי וְלָאֵי וְלָא דָחֵיל **מִן קֳדָם** יְיָ:


When they chanced upon [**overtook**] you en route struck down [**killed**] your appendage—all the feeble ones behind you—and you were exhausted and wearied, and they had no fear of [**before**] God.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 25:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/25:19)


וִיהֵי כַּד יְנִיחַ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לָךְ מִכָּל בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבָךְ מִסְּחוֹר סְחוֹר בְּאַרְעָא דִּי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ אַחֲסָנָא לְמֵירְתַהּ תִּמְחֵי יָת **דּוּכְרָנָא** דַעֲמָלֵק מִתְּחוֹת שְׁמַיָּא לָא תִּתִּנְשֵׁי:


When Adonoy, your God, has given you repose from all your enemies around, in the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you as territory to inherit, you shall obliterate the memory [**males**] of Amalek from beneath the sky; do not forget.



## Chapter 26

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:1)

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:2)


וְתִסַּב מֵרֵישׁ כָּל אִבָּא דְאַרְעָא דִּי תָעֵל מֵאַרְעָךְ דִּי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ וּתְשַׁוֵּי בְסַלָּא וּתְהַךְ לְאַתְרָא דִּי יִתִּרְעֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְאַשְׁרָאָה שְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ תַּמָּן:


You are to take of the first of all the fruits of the soil that you bring from your land, that Adonoy, your God, is giving you, and place [it] in a basket; and go to the place that Adonoy, your God, chooses to house His Presence there.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:3)


וְתֵיתֵי לְוָת כַּהֲנָא דִּי יְהֵי בְּיוֹמַיָּא הָאִנּוּן וְתֵימַר לֵיהּ חַוֵּיתִי יוֹמָא דֵין **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ אֲרֵי עַלִּית לְאַרְעָא דִּי קַיִּים יְיָ לַאֲבָהָתָנָא לְמִתַּן לָנָא:


You are to come to the *kohein* who will be [of service] during those days and you will say to him, ‘‘I ascertain today to [**before**] Adonoy, your God, that I have arrived in the land that Adonoy swore to our forefathers to give us.’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 26:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:4)


וְיִסַּב כַּהֲנָא סַלָּא מִידָךְ וְיַחְתִנֵּיהּ קֳדָם מַדְבְּחָא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ:


The *kohein* shall then take the basket from your hand, and he will place it before the altar of Adonoy, your God.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 26:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:5)


וְתָתֵיב וְתֵימַר קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ **לָבָן** אֲרַמָּאָה **בָּעָא** לְאוֹבָדָא יָת אַבָּא וּנְחַת לְמִצְרַיִם וְדָר תַּמָּן **בְּעַם זְעֵר** וַהֲוָה תַמָּן לְעַם רַב תַּקִּיף וְסַגִּי:


And you shall then proclaim and say before Adonoy, your God, ‘‘[**Lovon**] the Aramite [**wanted to**] destroy[ed] my forefather, then he descended to Egypt and sojourned there with a tiny community [**small nation**]; and there he became a great people, powerful and numerous.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 26:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:6)


וְאַבְאִישׁוּ לָנָא מִצְרַיִם וְעַנְּיָנָא וִיהָבוּ עֲלָנָא פָּלְחָנָא קַשְׁיָא:


‘‘The Egyptians treated us badly and oppressed us, and they imposed hard labor upon us.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 26:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:7)


וְצַלֵּינָא **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָא דַאֲבָהָתָנָא וְקַבִּיל יְיָ צְלוֹתָנָא וּגְלֵי **קֳדָמוֹהִי** עַמְלָנָא וְלֵאוּתָנָא וְדוּחֲקָנָא:


‘‘We prayed to [**before**] Adonoy, God of our forefathers, and Adonoy accepted our prayer, and perceived [**before Him**] our oppression, and our labor, and the pressure upon us.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 26:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:8)


וְאַפְּקָנָא יְיָ מִמִּצְרַיִם בִּידָא תַקִּיפָא וּבִדְרָעָא מְרָמְמָא וּבְחֶזְוָנָא רַבָּא וּבְאָתִין וּבְמוֹפְתִין:


‘‘And Adonoy took us out of Egypt with a powerful hand and with an extended arm, and with great display, and with signs, and with wonders.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 26:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:9)


וְאַיְתָנָא לְאַתְרָא הָדֵין וִיהַב לָנָא יָת אַרְעָא הָדָא אַרְעָא עָבְדָא חֲלַב וּדְבָשׁ:


‘‘And He brought us to this place, and He gave us this land, a land flowing milk and honey.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 26:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:10)


וּכְעַן הָא אַיְתֵתִי יָת רֵישׁ אִבָּא דְאַרְעָא דִּיהַבְתְּ לִי יְיָ וְתַחְתִנֵיהּ קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ וְתִסְגּוּד קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ:


And now, see! I have brought the first of the fruits of the soil that You have given me Adonoy.’’ And you shall then place it before Adonoy, your God, and you will prostrate yourself in the presence of Adonoy, your God.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 26:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:11)


וְתֶחֱדֵי בְכָל טַבְתָא דִּי יְהַב לָךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ **וְלֶאֱנַשׁ** בֵּיתָךְ אַתְּ וְלֵוָאֵי וְגִיּוֹרָא דִּי בֵינָךְ:


You shall rejoice with all the good that Adonoy, your God, gave you and [**the members of**] your household; you, the levi, and the proselyte in your midst.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 26:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:12)


אֲרֵי תְשֵׁצֵי לְעַשָּׂרָא יָת כָּל מַעֲשַׂר עֲלַלְתָּךְ בְּשַׁתָּא תְלִיתָאָה שְׁנַת מַעְשְׂרָא וְתִתֵּן לְלֵוָאֵי לְגִיּוֹרָא לְיִתַּמָּא וּלְאַרְמְלָא וְיֵכְלוּן בְּקִרְוָיךְ וְיִשְׂבְּעוּן:


When you finish tithing all the tithes of your produce in the third year, the year of the tithe; and you have given to the Levi, to the proselyte, to the orphan, and to the widow, so that they may eat within your portals to their satisfaction;


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 26:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:13)


וְתֵימַר קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ פַּלֵּתִי קֹדֶשׁ **מַעְשְׂרָא** מִן בֵּיתָא וְאַף יְהָבִתֵּיהּ לְלֵוָאֵי וּלְגִיּוֹרָא לְיִתַּמָּא וּלְאַרְמְלָא כְּכָל פִּקּוּדָךְ דִּי פַקִּדְתַּנִי לָא עֲבָרִית מִפִּקּוּדָיךְ וְלָא אִתִּנְשֵׁיתִי:


You shall declare before Adonoy, your God, ‘‘I have divested my estate of sacred [**tithe**] material, and I have also presented it to the Levi and also to the proselyte, the orphan, and the widow, totally according to Your command that You commanded me; I did not transgress any of Your commandments nor did I forget.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 26:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:14)


לָא אֲכָלִית בְּאֶבְלִי מִנֵּיהּ וְלָא **פַלֵּתִי** מִנֵּיהּ בְּדִמְסָאָב וְלָא יְהָבִית מִנֵּיהּ לְמִית קַבֵּלִית **לְמֵימְרָא** דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָי עֲבָדִית כְּכֹל דִּי פַקִּדְתַּנִי:


I did not eat of it when grieving, nor did I devour it when ritually defiled, nor did I make use of it for the dead; I have heeded the voice [**word**] of Adonoy, my God; I have fulfilled everything that You commanded me.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 26:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:15)


אִסְתְּכִי מִמְּדוֹר קָדְשָׁךְ מִן שְׁמַיָּא וּבָרֵךְ יָת עַמָּךְ יָת יִשְׂרָאֵל וְיָת אַרְעָא דִּי יְהַבְתְּ לָנָא כְּמָא דִי קַיֶּמְתָּא לַאֲבָהָתָנָא אֲרַע עָבְדָא חֲלַב וּדְבָשׁ:


View, from Your sacred residence, from the heavens, and bless Your people, Yisroel and the soil which You have given us, as You swore to our forefathers, a land flowing with milk and honey.’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 26:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:16)


יוֹמָא הָדֵין יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ מְפַקְּדָךְ לְמֶעְבַּד יָת קְיָמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין וְיָת דִּינַיָּא וְתִטַּר וְתַעְבֵּד יָתְהוֹן בְּכָל לִבָּךְ וּבְכָל נַפְשָׁךְ:


This very day, Adonoy, your God, commands you to perform these statutes and the laws; you shall observe and perform them wholeheartedly and with your whole being.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 26:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:17)


יָת יְיָ חֲטַבְתָּ יוֹמָא דֵין לְמֶהֱוֵי לָךְ לֶאֱלָהּ וְלִמְהַךְ בְּאָרְחָן **דְּתָקְנָן קֳדָמוֹהִי** וּלְמִטַּר קְיָמוֹהִי וּפִקּוּדוֹהִי וְדִינוֹהִי **וּלְקַבָּלָא בְמֵימְרֵיהּ**:


Adonoy, you have distinguised today to be a God for you and to go in His ways [**which are proper before Him**] and to guard His statutes and His commandments and His laws, and to obey Him [**accept His word**].


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 26:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:18)


וַייָ חָטְבָךְ יוֹמָא דֵין לְמֶהֱוֵי לֵיהּ לְעַם חַבִּיב כְּמָא דִי מַלִּיל לָךְ וּלְמִטַּר כָּל פִּקּוּדוֹהִי:


And Adonoy has distinguished you today to be for Him a treasured people as He said to you, and to guard all His commandments.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 26:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/26:19)


וּלְמִתְּנָךְ עִלָּאָה עַל כָּל עַמְמַיָּא דִּי עֲבַד לְתֻשְׁבָּחָא וּלְשׁוּם וְלִרְבוּ וּלְמֶהֱוָךְ עַם קַדִּישׁ **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ כְּמָא דִי מַלִּיל:


And to place you superior to all the nations whom He made, for praise and for renown and for glory, and so that you may be a people sanctified to [**before**] Adonoy, your God, as He said.



## Chapter 27

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/27:1)


וּפַקִּיד משֶׁה וְסָבֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל יָת עַמָּא לְמֵימָר טַר יָת כָּל תַּפְקֶדְתָּא דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקֵּד יָתְכוֹן יוֹמָא דֵין:


Moshe and the elders of Yisroel commanded the people the following, ‘‘Keep all of the commandment that I am commanding you today.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/27:2)


וִיהֵי בְּיוֹמָא דִּי תַעְבְּרוּן יָת יַרְדְּנָא לְאַרְעָא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ וּתְקִים לָךְ אַבְנִין רַבְרְבִין וּתְסוּד יָתְהוֹן בְּסִידָא:


When the day comes that you cross the Yardein to the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you, erect large stones and coat them with whitewash.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/27:3)


וְתִכְתּוֹב עֲלֵיהוֹן יָת כָּל פִּתְגָּמֵי אוֹרַיְתָא הָדָא בְּמֶעְבְּרָךְ בְּדִיל דִּי תֵעוּל לְאַרְעָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ אַרְעָא עָבְדָא חֲלַב וּדְבַשׁ כְּמָא דִי מַלִּיל יְיָ אֱלָהָא דַאֲבָהָתָךְ לָךְ:


You are to write on them all statements of this Torah upon your crossing; in order that you come to the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you, a land flowing milk and honey, as promised by Adonoy, God of your forefathers, to you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 27:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/27:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/27:4)


וִיהֵי בְּמֵעֲבָרְכוֹן יָת יַרְדְּנָא תְּקִימוּן יָת אַבְנַיָּא הָאִלֵּין דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקֵּד יָתְכוֹן יוֹמָא דֵין בְּטוּרָא דְעֵיבָל וּתְסוּד יָתְהוֹן בְּסִידָא:


As soon as you have crossed the Yardein, set up these stones that I am commanding you today, on Mount Eival, and coat them with whitewash.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 27:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/27:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/27:5)


וְתִבְנֵי תַמָּן מַדְבְּחָא **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ מַדְבַּח אַבְנִין לָא תְרִים עֲלֵיהוֹן פַּרְזְלָא:


Build there an altar to [**before**] Adonoy, your God; an altar of stones; do not lift iron upon them.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 27:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/27:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/27:6)


אַבְנִין שָׁלְמִין תִּבְנֵי יָת מַדְבְּחָא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ וְתַסֵּק עֲלוֹהִי עֲלָוָן **קֳדָם** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ:


[Of] whole stones are you to build the altar of Adonoy, your God; and you are to bring upon it burnt-offerings to [**before**] Adonoy, your God.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 27:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/27:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/27:7)


וְתִכּוֹס **נִכְסַת קוּדְשִׁין** וְתֵיכוּל תַּמָּן וְתֶחֱדֵי קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ:


You are to slaughter peace [**sacred**]-offerings and eat [them] there; and you are to rejoice in the presence of Adonoy, your God.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 27:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/27:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/27:8)


וְתִכְתּוֹב עַל אַבְנַיָּא יָת כָּל פִּתְגָּמֵי אוֹרַיְתָא הָדָא פָּרַשׁ יָאוּת:


You are to write on the stones all the statements of this Torah, explained properly.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 27:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/27:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/27:9)

Moshe and the *kohanim*-Levi’im addressed all of Yisroel, to say, ‘‘Take heed and accept, Yisroel! [On] this day you have become a people to [**before**] Adonoy, your God.

The Levi’im are to respond and say to every man of Yisroel aloud:

## Chapter 28

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:1)


וִיהֵי אִם **קַבָּלָא תְקַבֵּל לְמֵימְרָא** דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְמִטַּר לְמֶעְבַּד יָת כָּל פִּקּוּדוֹהִי דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקְּדָךְ יוֹמָא דֵין וְיִתְּנִנָּךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ עִלַּי עַל כָּל עַמְמֵי אַרְעָא:


When you hearken and obey [**accept the word of**] Adonoy, your God, to be sure to perform all of His commandments that I am commanding you today; Adonoy, your God, will place you superior to all the nations of the earth.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:2)


וְיֵיתוּן עֲלָךְ כָּל בִּרְכְּתָא הָאִלֵּין **וְיִדְבְּקֻנָּךְ** אֲרֵי **תְקַבַּל לְמֵימְרָא** דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ:


All these blessings will come upon you and will reach [**adhere to**] you, when you obey [**accept the word of**] Adonoy, your God.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:3)


בְּרִיךְ אַתְּ בְּקַרְתָּא וּבְרִיךְ אַתְּ בְּחַקְלָא:


Blessed are you in the city, and blessed are you in the field.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 28:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:4)


בְּרִיךְ **וַלְדָּא דִמְעָךְ וְאִבָּא דְאַרְעָךְ וּוַלְדָּא** דִבְעִירָךְ בַּקְרֵי תוֹרָיךְ וְעֶדְרֵי עָנָךְ:


Blessed is the fruit [**offspring**] of your belly, and the produce [**fruit**] of your soil, and the offspring of your animals, the issue of your cattle and the flocks of your sheep and goats.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 28:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:5)


בְּרִיךְ סַלָּךְ וְאַצְוָתָךְ:


Blessed will be your [fruit] basket and your solid foods.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 28:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:6)


בְּרִיךְ אַתְּ בְּמֵעֲלָךְ וּבְרִיךְ אַתְּ בְּמִפְּקָךְ:


You will be blessed at your arrival, and blessed at your departure.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 28:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:7)


יִתֵּן יְיָ יָת בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבָךְ דְּקָיְמִין עֲלָךְ **תְּבִירִין** קֳדָמָךְ בְּאָרְחָא חַד יִפְּקוּן לְוָתָךְ וּבְשִׁבְעָא אָרְחָן יֵעִרְקוּן מִקֳּדָמָךְ:


Adonoy will set your enemies who rise against you smitten [**broken**] before you; by one road will they approach you, but by seven roads they will flee from you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 28:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:8)


יְפַקֵּד יְיָ עִמָּךְ יָת בִּרְכְּתָא בְּאוֹצָרָךְ וּבְכֹל אוֹשָׁטוּת יְדָךְ וִיבָרֵכִנָּךְ בְּאַרְעָא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵב לָךְ:


Adonoy will order upon you the blessing in your storehouses and in all your commerce; and He will bless you in the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 28:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:9)


יְקִימִנָּךְ יְיָ לֵיהּ לְעַם קַדִּישׁ כְּמָא דִי קַיִּים לָךְ אֲרֵי תִטַּר יָת פִּקּוּדַיָּא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ וּתְהַךְ **בְּאָרְחָן דְּתָקְנָן קֳדָמוֹהִי**:


Adonoy will establish you for Himself as a holy people as He swore to you; when you will guard the commandments of Adonoy, your God, and will go in His ways [**that are correct before Him**].


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 28:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:10)


וְיֶחֱזוּן כָּל עַמְמֵי אַרְעָא אֲרֵי שְׁמָא דַיְיָ אִתְקְרֵי עֲלָךְ וְיִדְחֲלוּן מִנָּךְ:


And all the peoples of the earth will see that you are called by Adonoy’s Name and they will fear you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 28:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:11)


וְיוֹתְרִנָּךְ יְיָ לְטָבָא **בְּוַלְדָּא** דִמְעָךְ **וּבְוַלְדָּא** דִבְעִירָךְ **וּבְאִבָּא** דְאַרְעָךְ עַל אַרְעָא דִּי קַיִּים יְיָ לַאֲבָהָתָךְ לְמִתַּן לָךְ:


Adonoy will give you surplus as benefit in the fruit [**offspring**] of your belly, and the offspring of your animals, and the produce [**fruit**] of your soil; upon the land that Adonoy swore to your forefathers to give you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 28:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:12)


יִפְתַּח יְיָ לָךְ יָת אוֹצָרֵיהּ טָבָא יָת שְׁמַיָּא לְמִתַּן מְטַר אַרְעָךְ בְּעִדָּנֵיהּ וּלְבָרָכָא יָת כָּל עוֹבָדֵי יְדָךְ **וְתוֹזֵף** לְעַמְמִין סַגִּיאִין וְאַתְּ לָא **תְזוּף**:


Adonoy will open for you His good storehouse, the sky, to give you your land’s rain on time and to bless all your endeavors; and you will lend to many nations, but you will not borrow.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 28:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:13)


וְיִתְּנִנָּךְ יְיָ **לְתַקִּיף** וְלָא **לְחַלָּשׁ** וּתְהֵי בְּרַם לְעֵלָּא וְלָא תְהֵי לְתַחְתָּא אֲרֵי תְקַבַּל לְפִקּוּדַיָּא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקְּדָךְ יוֹמָא דֵין לְמִטַּר וּלְמֶעְבָּד:


Adonoy will place you at the head [**to be powerful**], not at the tail [**to be weak**]; and you will be solely on top, you will not be on the bottom; when you heed the commandments of Adonoy, your God, that I am commanding you today to guard and to perform.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 28:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/28:14)


וְלָא תִסְטוּן מִכָּל פִּתְגָּמַיָּא דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקֵּד יָתְכוֹן יוֹמָא דֵין יַמִּינָא וּשְׂמָאלָא לִמְהַךְ בָּתַר **טַעֲוַת עַמְמַיָּא** לְמִפְלְחִנּוּן:


And you do not deviate from all the statements that I am commanding you today to the right or to the left, to follow other gods [**idols of the nations**] to serve them.

## Chapter 29

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:1)


וּקְרָא משֶׁה לְכָל יִשְׂרָאֵל וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן אַתּוּן חֲזֵתוּן יָת כָּל דִּי עֲבַד יְיָ לְעֵינֵיכוֹן בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם לְפַרְעֹה וּלְכָל עַבְדוֹהִי וּלְכָל אַרְעֵיהּ:


Moshe called to all of Yisroel and said to them, ‘‘You have seen everything that Adonoy did as you watched, in the land of Egypt, to Pharoah and to all his attendants and to his entire land.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:2)


נִסֵּי רַבְרְבַיָּא דִּי חֲזָאָה עֵינָיךְ אָתַיָּא וּמוֹפְתַיָּא רַבְרְבַיָּא הָאִנּוּן:


The great miracles that you witnessed, those great signs and wonders.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:3)


וְלָא יְהַב יְיָ לְכוֹן לִבָּא לְמִדַּע וְעַיְנִין לְמֶחֱזֵי וְאֻדְנִין לְמִשְׁמַע עַד יוֹמָא הָדֵין:


Yet Adonoy did not grant you a knowledgeable mind and eyes to see, and ears to hear until this day.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:4)


וְדַבָּרִית יָתְכוֹן אַרְבְּעִין שְׁנִין בְּמַדְבְּרָא לָא בְלִיאַת כְּסוּתְכוֹן מִנְּכוֹן וּמְסָנָךְ לָא עֲדוֹ מֵעַל רַגְלָךְ:


I have led you forty years in the wilderness, your garments have not worn out on you and your shoes have not worn out on your feet.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:5)


לַחְמָא לָא אֲכַלְתּוּן וַחֲמַר חֲדַת וְעַתִּיק לָא שְׁתֵיתוּן בְּדִיל דְּתִדְּעוּן אֲרֵי אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן:


Bread have you not eaten, neither fresh nor aged wine have you drunk, in order that you know that I, Adonoy, am your God.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:6)


וַאֲתֵתוּן לְאַתְרָא הָדֵין וּנְפַק סִיחוֹן מַלְכָּא דְחֶשְׁבּוֹן וְעוֹג מַלְכָּא **דְמַתְנָן** לָקֳדָמוּתָנָא לַאֲגָחָא **קְרָבָא** וּמְחוֹנוּן:


Then when you came to this place, Sichon, king of Cheshbon, came out—and Og, king of Boshon [**Masnon**]—toward us to wage war and we smote them.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:7)


וּנְסִבְנָא יָת אַרְעֲהוֹן וִיהָבִנַּהּ לְאַחֲסָנָא **לְשִׁבְטָא** דִרְאוּבֵן **וּלְשִׁבְטָא** דְגָד וּלְפַלְגּוּת שִׁבְטָא דִמְנַשֶּׁה:


And we took the their land and gave it as territory to the [**tribe of the**] Reuvenites and to the [**tribe of the**] Gaddites, and to half the tribe of the Menashians.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:8)


וְתִטְּרוּן יָת פִּתְגָּמֵי קְיָמָא הָדָא וְתַעְבְּדוּן יָתְהוֹן בְּדִיל דְּתִצְלְחוּן יָת כָּל דִּי תַעְבְּדוּן:


You are to guard the tenets of this covenant and fulfill them, in order that you succeed in everything that you do.’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:9)


אַתּוּן קָיְמִין יוֹמָא דֵין כָּלְּכוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן רֵישֵׁיכוֹן שִׁבְטֵיכוֹן סָבֵיכוֹן וְסָרְכֵיכוֹן כֹּל אֱנַשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


‘‘You stand, this day all of you before Adonoy, your God; your tribal chieftains, your elders and your law officers, every man of Yisroel.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:10)


טַפְלְכוֹן נְשֵׁיכוֹן וְגִיּוֹרָךְ דִּי בְּגוֹ מַשְׁרִיתָיךְ מִלָּקֵט אָעָיךְ עַד מְלֵי מַיָּיךְ:


Your young, your wives, and your convert who is within your camps; from your wood cutters to the water drawers.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:11)


לְאָעָלוּתָךְ בִּקְיָמָא דַייָ אֱלָהָךְ וּבְמוֹמָתֵיהּ דִּי יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ גְּזַר עִמָּךְ יוֹמָא דֵין:


For your passage into the covenant of Adonoy, your God, and His oath-curse, that Adonoy, your God, is making with you today.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:12)


בְּדִיל לַאֲקָמָא יָתָךְ יוֹמָא דֵין **קֳדָמוֹהִי** לְעַמָּא וְהוּא יְהֵי לָךְ לֶאֱלָהּ כְּמָא דִי מַלִּיל לָךְ וּכְמָא דִי קַיִּים לַאֲבָהָתָךְ לְאַבְרָהָם לְיִצְחָק וּלְיַעֲקֹב:


In order to sustain you today [**before Him**] as His people, and He will be for you a God as He promished you and as He promised your forefathers Avrohom, Yitzchok, and Yaakov.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:13)


וְלָא עִמְּכוֹן בִּלְחוֹדֵיכוֹן אֲנָא גְּזַר יָת קְיָמָא הָדָא וְיָת מוֹמָתָא הָדָא:


And not with you alone am I making this covenant and this oath-curse.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:14)


אֲרֵי יָת **מָן** דְּאִיתוֹהִי הָכָא עִמָּנָא קָאֵם יוֹמָא דֵין קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָנָא וְיָת דִּי לֵיתוֹהִי הָכָא עִמָּנָא יוֹמָא דֵין:


But with whoever is here with us standing today in the presence of Adonoy, our God, and with those who are not here with us today.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:15)


אֲרֵי אַתּוּן יְדַעְתּוּן יָת דִּיתֵבְנָא בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם וְיָת דִּי עֲבַרְנָא בְּגוֹ עַמְמַיָּא דִּי עֲבַרְתּוּן:


For you are aware how we lived in the land of Egypt, and how we passed through the nations whom you passed through.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:16)


וַחֲזֵתוּן יָת שִׁקּוּצֵיהוֹן וְיָת טַעֲוָתְהוֹן אָעָא וְאַבְנָא כַּסְפָּא וְדַהֲבָא דִּי עִמְּהוֹן:


Where you observed their abominations and their loathsomeness [idols], wood and stone, silver and gold, that they owned.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:17)


דִּילְמָא אִית בְּכוֹן גְּבַר אוֹ אִתְּתָא אוֹ **זַרְעִית** אוֹ שִׁבְטָא דִּי לִבֵּיהּ פָּנֵי יוֹמָא דֵין **מִדַּחַלְתָּא** דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָנָא לְמֵהַךְ לְמִפְלַח יָת **טַעֲוַת** **עַמְמַיָּא** הָאִנּוּן דִּילְמָא אִית בְּכוֹן **גְּבַר מְהַרְהַר חֶטְאִין אוֹ זָדוֹן**:


Perhaps there is among you a man or a woman or a family [**offspring**] or a tribe whose thoughts stray today from [being] with [**the fear of**] Adonoy, our God, to go serve the gods [**idols**] of those nations; lest there is within you a root producing gall and bitter fruit [**a man who imagines sin or evil**].


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:18)


וִיהֵי בְּמִשְׁמְעֵיהּ יָת פִּתְגָּמֵי מוֹמָתָא הָדָא **וִיחַשַּׁב בְּלִבֵּיהּ** לְמֵימַר שְׁלָמָא יְהֵי לִי אֲרֵי **בְּהַרְהוֹר** לִבִּי אֲנָא אָזֵל בְּדִיל **לְאוֹסָפָא לֵיהּ חֶטְאֵי שָׁלוּתָא עַל זְדָנוּתָא**:


When he hears the statements of this oath-curse, he will imagine self-blessings [**think to himself**], saying, ‘‘Peace will be my lot when I shall follow what my thoughts envision,’’ so that the unintentional may be added to the sinful [**I will add unintentional sins to the intentional**].


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:19)


לָא יֵיבֵי יְיָ לְמִשְׁבַּק לֵיהּ אֲרֵי בְּכֵן **יִתְקֵף רָגְזָא** דַיְיָ וְחֶמְתֵיהּ בְּגַבְרָא הַהוּא **וְיִדְבְּקוּן** בֵּיהּ כָּל לְוָטַיָּא דִּכְתִיבִין בְּסִפְרָא הָדֵין וְיִמְחֵי יְיָ יָת שְׁמֵיהּ מִתְּחוֹת שְׁמַיָּא:


Adonoy will be unwilling to forgive him, because then Adonoy’s nostrils will fume [**anger will be aroused**] and His vengeful fury will enflame against [**cleave to**] that man, and there will cling to him the entire oath-curse written in this book; and Adonoy will eliminate his name from beneath the sky.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:20)


וְיַפְרְשִׁנֵּיהּ יְיָ לְבִישָׁא מִכֹּל שִׁבְטַיָּא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל כְּכֹל לְוָטֵי קְיָמָא דִּכְתִיבִין בְּסִפְרָא דְאוֹרַיְתָא הָדֵין:


And Adonoy will seperate him for harm from all the tribes of Yisroel, in accord with all the oath-curses of the covenant inscribed in this Torah scroll.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:21)


וְיֵימַר דָּרָא בַתְרָאָה בְּנֵיכוֹן דִּי יְקוּמוּן מִבַּתְרֵיכוֹן **וּבַר עַמְמִין** דְּיֵיתֵי מֵאַרְעָא רְחִיקָא וְיֶחֱזוּן יָת מְחָתָא דְּאַרְעָא הַהִיא וְיָת מַרְעָהָא דִּי אַמְרַע יְיָ בַּהּ:


The latter generation will say—your children who will arise after you and the stranger [**foreign alien**] who will come from a distant land; and they will see the plagues of that land and its illnesses that Adonoy has harmed it with.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:22)


גָּפְרֵתָא וּמִלְחָא יְקִידַת כָּל אַרְעַהּ לָא תִזְרַע וְלָא תַצְמַח וְלָא יִסַּק בַּהּ כָּל עֶסְבָּא כְּמַהְפֶּכְתָּא דִּסְדֹם וַעֲמֹרָה אַדְמָה וּצְבוֹיִם דִּי הֲפַךְ יְיָ **בְּרָגְזֵיהּ** וּבְחֶמְתֵיהּ:


Sulfur and salt, all its land, will be burnt, it will not be seeded, and it will not sprout, and no grass will grow there; like the overturning of Sodom and Amoroh, Admoh and Tzevoyim, which Adonoy overturned in His anger and His wrath.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:23)


וְיֵימְרוּן כָּל עַמְמַיָּא עַל מֶה עֲבַד יְיָ כְּדֵין לְאַרְעָא הָדָא מָא **תְקוֹף רָגְזָא** רַבָּא הָדֵין:


And all the nations will say, ‘‘For what did Adonoy do so to this land? Why the vigor of this great [**aroused**] anger?’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:24)


וְיֵימְרוּן עַל דִּשְׁבָקוּ יָת קְיָמָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָא דַאֲבָהָתְהוֹן דִּי גְּזַר עִמְּהוֹן בְּאַפָּקוּתֵיהּ יָתְהוֹן מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם:


And they will reply, ‘‘Because they forsook the covenant of Adonoy, the God of their forefathers, which He made with them when He took them out of the land of Egypt.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:25)


וַאֲזָלוּ וּפְלָחוּ **לְטַעֲוַת עַמְמַיָּא** וּסְגִידוּ לְהוֹן **דַּחֲלָן** דִּי לָא יְדָעֻנּוּן וְלָא **אוֹטִיבָא** לְהוֹן:


And they went and served other gods and prostrated themselves to them; gods unknown to them and [which] He had not apportioned to them.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:26)


**וּתְקֵף** **רָגְזָא** דַיְיָ בְּאַרְעָא הַהִיא לְאַיְתָאָה עֲלַהּ יָת כָּל לְוָטַיָּא דִּכְתִיבִין בְסִפְרָא הָדֵין:


And Adonoy became furious with that land, [causing Him] to bring on it the entire curse written in this book.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:27)


וְטַלְטְלִנּוּן יְיָ מֵעַל אַרְעֲהוֹן בִּרְגַז וּבֶחֶמָה (וּבְחֵמְתָא) **וּבִתְקוֹף רְגַז וְאַגְלִנּוּן** לְאַרְעָא אָחֳרִי כְּיוֹמָא הָדֵין:


And Adonoy forced them away from their land with anger and wrath and great fury [**aroused anger**]; and He cast [**exile**] them into another land like this day.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 29:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/29:28)


דְּמִטַּמְּרָן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָנָא וּדְמִגָּלְיָן לָנָא וְלִבְנָנָא עַד עֳלָם לְמֶעְבַּד יָת כָּל פִּתְגָּמֵי אוֹרַיְתָא הָדָא:


The hidden [matters] are Adonoy’s, our God’s, and the revealed [matters] are ours and our children’s forever, to perform all the statements of this Torah.



## Chapter 30

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:1)


וִיהֵי אֲרֵי יֵיתוּן עֲלָךְ כָּל פִּתְגָּמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין בִּרְכַן וּלְוָטִין דִּי יְהָבִית קֳדָמָךְ וּתְתוּב לְלִבָּךְ בְּכָל עַמְמַיָּא דִּי אַגְלָךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְתַמָּן:


When it happens that there come upon you all these statements, the blessing and the curse that I have set before you; and you will restore to your perception amid all the nations where Adonoy, your God, has exiled you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:2)


וּתְתוּב **לְדַחַלְתָּא** דַיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ **וּתְקַבַּל לְמֵימְרֵיהּ** כְּכֹל דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקְּדָךְ יוֹמָא דֵין אַתְּ וּבְנָיךְ בְּכָל לִבָּךְ וּבְכָל נַפְשָׁךְ:


You will return to [**the fear of**] Adonoy, your God, and obey Him [**accept His word**] exactly as I am commanding you today, you and your sons, wholeheartedly and with your whole being.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:3)


וִיתוּב יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָת שָׁבֵי **גַלְוָתָךְ** וִירַחַם עֲלָךְ וִיתוּב וְיִכְנְשִׁנָּךְ מִכָּל עַמְמַיָּא דִּי יְבַדְּרִנָּךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְתַמָּן:


Adonoy, your God, will bring back your returnees [**of your exile**] and will be merciful toward you; and He will return and gather you from all the peoples that Adonoy, your God, has dispersed you there.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 30:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:4)


אִם יְהֵי גַלְוָתָךְ בִּסְיָפֵי שְׁמַיָּא מִתַּמָּן יִכְנְשִׁנָּךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ וּמִתַּמָּן **יְקָרְבִנָּךְ**:


If your exiled one will be at the edge of the heavens, from there will Adonoy, your God, gather you and from there will He take you [**bring you near**].


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 30:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:5)


וְיָעֵלִנָּךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לְאַרְעָא דִּירִיתוּ אֲבָהָתָךְ וְתֵרְתַהּ וְיוֹטֶב לָךְ וְיַסְגִּנָּךְ מֵאֲבָהָתָךְ:


And Adonoy, your God, will bring you to the land that your forefathers inherited and you will inherit it; and He will benefit you and multiply you more than your forefathers.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 30:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:6)


**וְיֶעְדֵּי** יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָת **טַפְשׁוּת** לִבָּךְ וְיָת טַפְשׁוּת לִבָּא דִבְנָיךְ לְמִרְחַם יָת יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בְּכָל לִבָּךְ וּבְכָל נַפְשָׁךְ בְּדִיל חַיָּיךְ:


And Adonoy, your God, will circumcise [**remove the foolishness from**] your heart and the heart of your descendants, to love Adonoy, your God, wholeheartedly and with all your being in order that you live.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 30:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:7)


וְיִתֵּן יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָת כָּל לְוָטַיָּא הָאִלֵּין עַל בַּעֲלֵי **דְבָבָךְ** וְעַל סָנְאָךְ דִּי רְדָפוּךְ:


Adonoy, your God, will place all these oath-curses upon your enemies and upon your foes who chased you.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 30:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:8)


וְאַתְּ תְּתוּב **וּתְקַבַּל בְּמֵימְרָא** דַיְיָ וְתַעְבֵּד יָת כָּל פִּקּוּדוֹהִי דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקְּדָךְ יוֹמָא דֵין:


And you will turn back and obey [**accept the word of**] Adonoy; and you will perform all His commandments that I am commanding you today.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 30:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:9)


וְיוֹתְרִנָּךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בְּכֹל עוֹבָדֵי יְדָךְ **בְּוַלְדָּא** דִמְעָךְ וּבְוַלְדָּא דִבְעִירָךְ **וּבְאִבָּא** דְאַרְעָךְ לְטָבָא אֲרֵי יְתוּב יְיָ לְמֶחֱדֵי עֲלָךְ לְטָב כְּמָא דִי חֲדִי עַל אֲבָהָתָךְ:


Adonoy, your God, will give you surplus in all your endeavors, in the fruit [**offspring**] of your belly, and in the offspring of your animals, and in the produce [**fruit**] of your soil—for benefit; for Adonoy will return to rejoice over you for benefit as He rejoiced over your forefathers.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 30:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:10)


אֲרֵי **תְקַבַּל בְּמֵימְרָא** דַּייָ אֱלָהָךְ לְמִטַּר פִּקּוּדוֹהִי וּקְיָמוֹהִי דִּכְתִיבִין בְּסִפְרָא דְאוֹרַיְתָא הָדֵין אֲרֵי תְתוּב קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בְּכָל לִבָּךְ וּבְכָל נַפְשָׁךְ:


When you obey [**accept the word of**] Adonoy, your God, to guard His commandments and His statutes, written in this Torah scroll; when you turn back to Adonoy, your God, wholeheartedly and with all your being.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 30:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:11)


אֲרֵי תַּפְקֶדְתָּא הָדָא דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקְּדָךְ יוֹמָא דֵין לָא מַפְרְשָׁא הִיא מִנָּךְ וְלָא רְחִיקָא הִיא:


For this *mitzvah* that I am commanding you today; it is not abstruse to you nor is it distant.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 30:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:12)


לָא בִשְׁמַיָּא הִיא לְמֵימָר מָן יִסַּק לָנָא לִשְׁמַיָּא וְיִסְּבַהּ לָנָא וְיַשְׁמְעִנָּנָא יָתַהּ וְנַעְבְּדִנַּהּ:


It is not in heaven, [for you] to say, ‘‘Who will go up to heaven for us, and acquire it for us, and inform us of it, and we will fulfill it?’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 30:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:13)


וְלָא מֵעִבְרָא לְיַמָּא הִיא לְמֵימַר מָן יֵעִבַר לָנָא לְעִבְרָא דְיַמָּא וְיִסְּבַהּ לָנָא וְיַשְׁמְעִנָּנָא יָתַהּ וְנַעְבְּדִנַּהּ:


Nor is it overseas, [for you] to say, ‘‘Who will travel overseas for us, and acquire it for us, and inform us of it, and we will fulfill it?’’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 30:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:14)


אֲרֵי קָרִיב לָךְ פִּתְגָּמָא לַחֲדָא בְּפוּמָךְ וּבְלִבָּךְ לְמֶעְבְּדֵיהּ:


For the matter is extremely close to you; in your mouth and in your mind to fulfill it.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 30:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:15)


חֲזֵי דִיהָבִית קֳדָמָךְ יוֹמָא דֵין יָת חַיֵּי וְיָת טַבְתָא וְיָת מוֹתָא וְיָת בִּישָׁא:


See, I have placed before you today life and good, and death and bad.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 30:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:16)


דִּי אֲנָא מְפַקְּדָךְ יוֹמָא דֵין לְמִרְחַם יָת יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לִמְהַךְ בְּאָרְחָן **דְּתָקְנָן קֳדָמוֹהִי** וּלְמִטַּר פִּקּוּדוֹהִי וּקְיָמוֹהִי וְדִינוֹהִי וְתֵיחֵי וְתִסְגֵּי וִיבָרֵכִנָּךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בְּאַרְעָא דִּי אַתְּ עָלֵל לְתַמָּן לְמֵירְתַהּ:


For which I command you this day, to love Adonoy, your God, to go in His ways [**proper before Him**] and to guard His commandments and His statutes and His laws; and you will live and you will flourish, and Adonoy, your God, will bless you in the land that you are coming there to inherit.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 30:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:17)


וְאִם יִפְנֵי לִבָּךְ וְלָא **תְקַבֵּל** וְתִטְעֵי וְתִסְגּוּד **לְטַעֲוַת עַמְמַיָּא** וְתִפְלְחִנּוּן:


But if your heart strays and you do not listen [**accept**], and you are misled, and you prostrate yourself to other gods [**idols of the nations**] and worship them.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 30:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:18)


**חַוֵּיתִי** לְכוֹן יוֹמָא דֵין אֲרֵי מֵיבַד תֵּיבְדוּן לָא תוֹרְכוֹן יוֹמִין עַל אַרְעָא דִּי אַתְּ עָבֵר יָת יַרְדְּנָא לְמֵעַל לְתַמָּן לְמֵירְתַהּ:


I tell [**have shown**] you today that you shall certainly perish; you will not remain long on the land that you are crossing the Yardein to come there to inherit.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 30:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:19)


אַסְהֵדִית בְּכוֹן יוֹמָא דֵין יָת שְׁמַיָּא וְיָת אַרְעָא חַיֵּי וּמוֹתָא יְהָבִית קֳדָמָךְ בִּרְכָן וּלְוָטִין וְתִתִּרְעֵי בְּחַיֵּי בְּדִיל דְּתֵיחֵי אַתְּ וּבְנָיךְ:


I invoke, as witnesses against you this day, heaven and earth: Life and death have I placed before you, blessing and curse; you choose life in order that you live, you and your descendants,


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 30:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/30:20)


לְמִרְחַם יָת יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ **לְקַבָּלָא לְמֵימְרֵיהּ** **וּלְאִתְקָרָבָא לְדַחַלְתֵּיהּ** אֲרֵי הוּא חַיָּיךְ וְאוֹרָכוּת יוֹמָיךְ לְמִתַּב עַל אַרְעָא דִּי קַיִּים יְיָ לַאֲבָהָתָךְ לְאַבְרָהָם לְיִצְחָק וּלְיַעֲקֹב לְמִתַּן לְהוֹן:


To love Adonoy, your God, to obey Him [**accept His word**] and to cling to [**the fear of**] Him; for He is your life and your longevity, to live on the soil that Adonoy swore to your forefathers—to Avrohom, to Yitzchok, and to Yaakov—to give them.



## Chapter 31

###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/31:1)


וַאֲזַל משֶׁה וּמַלִּיל יָת פִּתְגָּמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין עִם כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל:


Moshe went and he addressed these statements to all of Yisroel.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/31:2)


וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן בַּר מְאָה וְעֶשְׂרִין שְׁנִין אֲנָא יוֹמָא דֵין לֵית אֲנָא יָכִיל עוֹד לְמִפַּק וּלְמֵעָל וַיְיָ אֲמַר לִי לָא תְעִבַּר יָת יַרְדְּנָא הָדֵין:


He said to them, ‘‘I am 120 years old today, I cannot any longer go forth and return; for Adonoy has said to me, ‘You will not cross this Yardein.’


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/31:3)


יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ **מֵימְרֵיהּ** יֵעִבַר קֳדָמָךְ הוּא יְשֵׁצֵי יָת עַמְמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין מִקֳּדָמָךְ וְתֵירְתִנּוּן יְהוֹשֻׁעַ הוּא עָבֵר קֳדָמָךְ כְּמָא דִי מַלִּיל יְיָ:


Adonoy, your God, He [**His word**] is crossing before you, He will destroy these nations before you and you will inherit them. Yehoshua, he is crossing before you as Adonoy has stated.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 31:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/31:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/31:4)


וְיַעְבֵּד יְיָ לְהוֹן כְּמָא דִי עֲבַד לְסִיחוֹן וּלְעוֹג מַלְכֵי אֱמֹרָאָה וּלְאַרְעֲהוֹן דִּי שֵׁצִי יָתְהוֹן:


And Adonoy will do to them as He did to Sichon and to Og, kings of the Emorites, and to their land; whom He destroyed.


###### Onkelos Deuteronomy 31:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/31:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Deuteronomy/r/31:5)
